## Add-CloudProject


NAME
    Add-CloudProject
    
SYNOPSIS
    Create a new Bentley Infrastructure Cloud Project and optionally 
    associate with a ProjectWise work area.
    
    
SYNTAX
    Add-CloudProject [[-InputFolder] <ProjectWiseFolder[]>] 
    [[-CloudProjectName] <string>] [-OIDCToken] <OIDCAuthToken> 
    [[-SetAsTemplate]] [[-UseOrganizationTemplates]] [[-TemplateName] 
    <string>] [[-SetDriveSync]] [[-SetUserSync]] [[-SetAutoUserSync]] 
    [[-WSGURL] <string>] [[-CloudProjectNumber] <string>] [[-Location] 
    <string>] [[-Latitude] <double>] [[-Longitude] <double>] 
    [[-BillingCountry] <string>] [[-DataCenterLocation] <string>] 
    [[-TimeZone] <string>] [[-MakeUnique]] [<CommonParameters>]
    
    
DESCRIPTION
    Create a new Bentley Infrastructure Cloud Project and optionally 
    associate with a ProjectWise work area.
    

PARAMETERS
    -InputFolder <ProjectWiseFolder[]>
        Array of ProjectWise folders from pipeline. Bentley Infrastructure 
        Cloud projects will be added to these folders
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    0
        Default value                PWPS_DAB.CommonTypes+ProjectWiseFolder[]
        Accept pipeline input?       True (ByValue)
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -CloudProjectName <string>
        Project Name to create (must be unique)
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    1
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -CloudProjectNumber <string>
        Project Number to create (must be unique). Can be non-numeric.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    2
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -Location <string>
        
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    3
        Default value                GeoLocation
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -Latitude <double>
        May include a decimal value. Between -90.0 and 90.0
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    4
        Default value                0
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -Longitude <double>
        May include a decimal value. Between -180.0 and 180.0
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    5
        Default value                0
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -BillingCountry <string>
        Country Code for project. Defaults to United States of America
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    6
        Default value                United States of America
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -DataCenterLocation <string>
        Data Center Location for project. Defaults to East US. If provided, 
        will use the Organization template's data center location.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    7
        Default value                East US
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -TimeZone <string>
        Defaults to Eastern Standard Time.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    8
        Default value                Eastern Standard Time
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -MakeUnique [<SwitchParameter>]
        If set, will add a date/time suffix to the name and number to ensure 
        uniqueness. Project creation will fail if name and number are not 
        unique.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    9
        Default value                False
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -OIDCToken <OIDCAuthToken>
        Token from Get-OIDCToken -Authority https://ims.bentley.com
        
        Required?                    true
        Position?                    10
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -SetAsTemplate [<SwitchParameter>]
        Set new project as "Template".
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    11
        Default value                False
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -UseOrganizationTemplates [<SwitchParameter>]
        If specifified, uses organization templates. Otherwise uses standard 
        template.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    12
        Default value                False
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -TemplateName <string>
        Template name for project creation. Defaults to "Standard Project 
        Template" if not specified. Users can specify other template names 
        such as "Standard CDE Template" for different project types. 
        Required if UseOrganizationTemplates is specified. When creating 
        from a template, Default Documents area will not be created.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    13
        Default value                Standard Project Template
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -SetDriveSync [<SwitchParameter>]
        Set Drive-sync for new WAC.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    14
        Default value                False
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -SetUserSync [<SwitchParameter>]
        Set On Demand User Sync for new WAC.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    15
        Default value                False
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -SetAutoUserSync [<SwitchParameter>]
        Set Auto User Sync for new WAC.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    16
        Default value                False
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -WSGURL <string>
        Required to register new WAC.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    17
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    <CommonParameters>
        This cmdlet supports the common parameters: Verbose, Debug,
        ErrorAction, ErrorVariable, WarningAction, WarningVariable,
        OutBuffer, PipelineVariable, and OutVariable. For more information, 
    see 
        about_CommonParameters 
    (https:/go.microsoft.com/fwlink/?LinkID=113216). 
    
INPUTS
    PWPS_DAB.CommonTypes+ProjectWiseFolder[]
        Array of ProjectWise folders from pipeline. Bentley Infrastructure 
        Cloud projects will be added to these folders
    
    
OUTPUTS
    System.Guid
        Guid of new Bentley Infrastructure Cloud project.
    
    
NOTES
    
    
        
    
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 1 --------------------------
    
    #Bare bones example of creating a Bentley Infrastructure Cloud project.  
    Uses only required parameters.
    $oidcToken = Get-OIDCToken -Authority https://ims.bentley.com
    $CloudProjectId = Add-CloudProject -CloudProjectName MyCloudProjectName 
    -CloudProjectNumber MyCloudProjectNumber -OIDCToken $oidcToken
    
    
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 2 --------------------------
    
    #Create new work area and add a Bentley Infrastructure Cloud project to 
    it.
    New-PWLogin
    $oidcToken = Get-OIDCToken -Authority https://ims.bentley.com
    $today = Get-Date -Format "yyyy-MM-dd"
    $projectName = $today + "_MyTestProject"
    $topLevel = "Projects\$today" 
    $rp = New-PWWorkArea -NewFolderPath "$topLevel\$projectName" 
    -ProjectType Civil -StorageArea Storage
    $CloudProjectId = Add-CloudProject -InputFolder $rp -CloudProjectName 
    $projectName -CloudProjectNumber $projectName -OIDCToken $oidcToken 
    -SetDriveSync -SetUserSync -SetAutoUserSync -WSGURL 
    "https://mygateway.com/ws"
    
    
    
RELATED LINKS

## Add-PWDocumentsWithCode


NAME
    Add-PWDocumentsWithCode
    
SYNOPSIS
    Create new abstract documents with document codes.
    
    
SYNTAX
    Add-PWDocumentsWithCode [-InputFolder] <ProjectWiseFolder[]> 
    [-Attributes] <Hashtable> [[-NumberOfDocuments] <int>] [[-StartNumber] 
    <int>] [<CommonParameters>]
    
    
DESCRIPTION
    Will will create abstract documents using the document code.
    

PARAMETERS
    -InputFolder <ProjectWiseFolder[]>
        Array of folders to process. Should use Get-PWFolders or related 
        cmdlet to generate
        
        Required?                    true
        Position?                    0
        Default value                PWPS_DAB.CommonTypes+ProjectWiseFolder[]
        Accept pipeline input?       True (ByValue)
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -Attributes <Hashtable>
        Hashtable of attributes. Hashtable in PowerShell looks like this 
        @{Name = 'Value'; Name2 = 'Value2' }
        
        Required?                    true
        Position?                    1
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       True (ByValue)
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -NumberOfDocuments <int>
        Number of documents to create (Required).
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    2
        Default value                0
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -StartNumber <int>
        Start number for naming (Optional).
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    3
        Default value                -1
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    <CommonParameters>
        This cmdlet supports the common parameters: Verbose, Debug,
        ErrorAction, ErrorVariable, WarningAction, WarningVariable,
        OutBuffer, PipelineVariable, and OutVariable. For more information, 
    see 
        about_CommonParameters 
    (https:/go.microsoft.com/fwlink/?LinkID=113216). 
    
INPUTS
    PWPS_DAB.CommonTypes+ProjectWiseFolder[]
        Array of folders to process. Should use Get-PWFolders or related 
        cmdlet to generate
    
    System.Collections.Hashtable
        Hashtable of attributes. Hashtable in PowerShell looks like this 
        @{Name = 'Value'; Name2 = 'Value2' }
    
    
OUTPUTS
    PWPS_DAB.CommonTypes+ProjectWiseDocument[]
        
    
    
NOTES
    
    
        
    
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 1 --------------------------
    
    # Create 5 abstract documents using document code populated with the 
    passed default attribute values
    Get-PWFolders -FolderPath 'Replicated\Project 01' | 
    Add-PWDocumentsWithCode -Attributes @{ ProjectNumber = 'BSI400'; 
    AreaShort = 'RW'; DocumentTypeShort = '2DM'; LevelShort = 'NA' } 
    -NumberOfDocuments 5
    
    
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 2 --------------------------
    
    # Create an abstract document using document code populated with the 
    passed default attribute values and then attach a dummy file to each 
    with the appropriate name
    Get-PWFolders -FolderPath 'Replicated\Project 01' | 
    Add-PWDocumentsWithCode -Attributes @{ ProjectNumber = 'BSI400'; 
    AreaShort = 'RW'; DocumentTypeShort = '2DM'; LevelShort = 'NA' }  | 
    Update-PWDocumentFile -NewFilePathName 'C:\temp\dummy.txt'
    
    
    
RELATED LINKS





--

## Add-PWGroupMember


NAME
    Add-PWGroupMember
    
SYNOPSIS
    Adds a User to a Group in the current ProjectWise Datasource.
    
    
SYNTAX
    Add-PWGroupMember [-GroupID] <Int32> [-UserID] <Int32> 
    [<CommonParameters>]
    
    Add-PWGroupMember [-GroupID] <Int32> [-UserName] <String> 
    [<CommonParameters>]
    
    Add-PWGroupMember [-GroupName] <String> [-UserID] <Int32> 
    [<CommonParameters>]
    
    Add-PWGroupMember [-GroupName] <String> [-UserName] <String> 
    [<CommonParameters>]
    
    
DESCRIPTION
    The Add-PWGroupMember cmdlet adds a User to a User Group in the current 
    ProjectWise Datasource.
    
    Users and Groups can be selected either by ID or by name.
    

PARAMETERS
    -GroupID <Int32>
        The ID of the User Group to add to.
        
        Required?                    true
        Position?                    0
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       True (ByPropertyName)
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -GroupName <String>
        The name of the User Group to add to. Some wildcards (*, ?) are 
        technically allowed, but Group Members can only be added one at a 
        time, so this name must only match one Group.
        
        Required?                    true
        Position?                    0
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -UserID <Int32>
        The UserID of the User to add.
        
        Required?                    true
        Position?                    1
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       True (ByPropertyName)
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -UserName <String>
        The UserName of the User to add. Some wildcards (*, ?) are 
        technically allowed, but Group Members can only be added one at a 
        time, so this name must only match one User.
        
        The two wildcard characters supported are:  * (matches zero or more 
        characters, starting at the specified position)  ? (matches any 
        character at the specified position)
        
        Required?                    true
        Position?                    1
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    <CommonParameters>
        This cmdlet supports the common parameters: Verbose, Debug,
        ErrorAction, ErrorVariable, WarningAction, WarningVariable,
        OutBuffer, PipelineVariable, and OutVariable. For more information, 
    see 
        about_CommonParameters 
    (https:/go.microsoft.com/fwlink/?LinkID=113216). 
    
INPUTS
    None
        You can optionally pipe either User or Group objects to this cmdlet.
    
    
OUTPUTS
    Bentley.ProjectWise.PowerShell.Common.User
        Add-PWGroupMember returns an object that represents the User that 
        was added to the User Group.
    
    
NOTES
    
    
        Add-PWGroupMember requires an open ProjectWise Connection in the 
        current thread.  You can open a Connection with Open-PWConnection 
        before calling Add-PWGroupMember.
        
        Add-PWGroupMember requires an Admin session.  To open an admin 
        session, use the -Admin flag when opening the ProjectWise Connection 
        with Open-PWConnection.
    
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 1 --------------------------
    
    Add-PWGroupMember -GroupID 3 -UserName admin
    
    Add a User with UserName = admin to the Group with GroupID = 3
    
    UserID UserName      Description   Type    SecProvider  IsConnected 
    IsDisabled ------ --------      -----------   ----    -----------  
    ----------- ----------   1    admin                       Logical        
               Y           N
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 2 --------------------------
    
    Add-PWGroupMember -GroupID 3 -UserID 2
    
    Add a User with UserID = 2 to the Group with GroupID = 3
    
    UserID UserName      Description   Type    SecProvider  IsConnected 
    IsDisabled ------ --------      -----------   ----    -----------  
    ----------- ----------   2    user                        Logical        
               N           N
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 3 --------------------------
    
    Add-PWGroupMember -GroupName DomainGroup -UserName admin
    
    Add a User with UserName = admin to the Group with GroupName = 
    DomainGroup
    
    UserID UserName      Description   Type    SecProvider  IsConnected 
    IsDisabled ------ --------      -----------   ----    -----------  
    ----------- ----------   1    admin                       Logical        
               Y           N
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 4 --------------------------
    
    Add-PWGroupMember -GroupName DomainGroup -UserID 2
    
    Add a User with UserID = 2 to the Group with GroupName = DomainGroup
    
    UserID UserName      Description   Type    SecProvider  IsConnected 
    IsDisabled ------ --------      -----------   ----    -----------  
    ----------- ----------   2    user                        Logical        
               N           N
    
RELATED LINKS
    Get-PWGroupMember 
    Remove-PWGroupMember 





--

## Add-PWUserDynamicCreationFilter


NAME
    Add-PWUserDynamicCreationFilter
    
SYNOPSIS
    Appends to the User Dynamic Creation Filter of the current ProjectWise 
    Datasource.
    
    
SYNTAX
    Add-PWUserDynamicCreationFilter [-And] [-Email <String>] [-GivenName 
    <String>] [-Name <String>] [-NameId <String>] [-Or] [-Organization 
    <String>] [-OrganizationId <String>] [-RawCondition <String>] [-Surname 
    <String>] [<CommonParameters>]
    
    
DESCRIPTION
    The Add-PWUserDynamicCreationFilter cmdlet validates and appends the 
    dynamic creation filter of the current ProjectWise Datasource with the 
    provided filter settings concatenated using the AND operator.
    

PARAMETERS
    -And [<SwitchParameter>]
        Specifies that the filter statement should be appended with an AND 
        operator to the existing datasource user dynamic creation filter. 
        This flag is the default flag if the OR flag is not set.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    named
        Default value                False
        Accept pipeline input?       True (ByValue)
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -Email <String>
        The "Email" claim criterion.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    named
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       True (ByValue)
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -GivenName <String>
        The "GivenName" claim criterion.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    named
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       True (ByValue)
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -Name <String>
        The "Name" claim criterion.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    named
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       True (ByValue)
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -NameId <String>
        The "NameId" claim criterion.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    named
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       True (ByValue)
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -Or [<SwitchParameter>]
        Specifies that the provided filter statement should be appended with 
        an OR operator to the existing datasource user dynamic creation 
        filter.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    named
        Default value                False
        Accept pipeline input?       True (ByValue)
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -Organization <String>
        The "Organization" claim criterion.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    named
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       True (ByValue)
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -OrganizationId <String>
        The "OrganizationId" claim criterion.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    named
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       True (ByValue)
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -RawCondition <String>
        Any custom conditions.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    named
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       True (ByValue)
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -Surname <String>
        The "SurName" claim criterion.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    named
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       True (ByValue)
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    <CommonParameters>
        This cmdlet supports the common parameters: Verbose, Debug,
        ErrorAction, ErrorVariable, WarningAction, WarningVariable,
        OutBuffer, PipelineVariable, and OutVariable. For more information, 
    see 
        about_CommonParameters 
    (https:/go.microsoft.com/fwlink/?LinkID=113216). 
    
INPUTS
    Strings
        Separate user dynamic creation filter settings.
    
    
OUTPUTS
    None
        
    
    
NOTES
    
    
        Add-PWUserDynamicCreationFilter requires an open ProjectWise 
        Connection in the current thread. You can open a Connection with 
        Open-PWConnection before calling Add-PWUserDynamicCreationFilter.
    
    -------------------------- Example 1 --------------------------
    
    Add-PWUserDynamicCreationFilter -Organization "Bentley Systems Inc" 
    -RawCondition 'group = "PW Users"'
    Add-PWUserDynamicCreationFilter -OR -Organization "Bentley Systems Inc" 
    -RawCondition 'group = "Administrators"'
    
    Sets the user creation filter to accept only users which belong to the 
    "Bentley Systems Inc" organization AND belong to either the "PW Users" 
    OR the "Administrators" group.
    
    Note: this example assumes that the current datasource user dynamic 
    creation filter is empty.
    -------------------------- Example 2 --------------------------
    
    Add-PWUserDynamicCreationFilter -Organization "Bentley Systems Inc"
    Add-PWUserDynamicCreationFilter -RawCondition 'group = "PW Users"'
    Add-PWUserDynamicCreationFilter -OR -Organization "Bentley Systems Inc"
    Add-PWUserDynamicCreationFilter -RawCondition 'group = "Administrators"'
    
    Sets the user creation filter to accept only users which belong to the 
    "Bentley Systems Inc" organization AND belong to either the "PW Users" 
    OR the "Administrators" group.
    
    Note: this example assumes that the current datasource user dynamic 
    creation filter is empty.
    
RELATED LINKS

## Add-PWUserIdentity


NAME
    Add-PWUserIdentity
    
SYNOPSIS
    Assigns a new Federated Identity to a User in the current ProjectWise 
    Datasource.
    
    
SYNTAX
    Add-PWUserIdentity [-UserID] <Int32> [[-ProviderID] <Int32>] [-Name] 
    <String> [<CommonParameters>]
    
    Add-PWUserIdentity [-UserName] <String> [[-ProviderID] <Int32>] [-Name] 
    <String> [<CommonParameters>]
    
    Add-PWUserIdentity [-UserName] <String> [[-ProviderName] <String>] 
    [-Name] <String> [[-ProviderProtocol] <String>] [<CommonParameters>]
    
    Add-PWUserIdentity [-UserID] <Int32> [[-ProviderName] <String>] [-Name] 
    <String> [[-ProviderProtocol] <String>] [<CommonParameters>]
    
    
DESCRIPTION
    The Add-PWUserIdentity cmdlet assigns new Federated Identity belonging 
    to specified Identity Provider to a User in the current ProjectWise 
    Datasource.
    
    User and Identity Provider can be selected either by their IDs or by 
    Names.
    

PARAMETERS
    -Name <String>
        The Name aka Identity of the Federated Identity to create.
        
        Required?                    true
        Position?                    2
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       True (ByPropertyName)
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -ProviderID <Int32>
        The numerical identifier of the Identity Provider to create 
        Federated Identity for. It is possible to create Federated Identity 
        detached from Provider if neither ProviderID nor ProviderName are 
        specified.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    1
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       True (ByPropertyName)
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -ProviderName <String>
        The name/identity/URL of the Identity Provider to create Federated 
        Identity for. It can be accompanied by ProviderProtocol parameter to 
        distinguish between multiple providers served by same service. It is 
        possible to create Federated Identity detached from Provider if 
        neither ProviderID nor ProviderName are specified.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    1
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -ProviderProtocol <String>
        The protocol/type of the Identity Provider as string. Currently 
        supported options are "STS" and "OIDC".
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    3
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -UserID <Int32>
        The numerical identifier of ProjectWise user to assign new identity 
        to.
        
        Required?                    true
        Position?                    0
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       True (ByPropertyName)
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -UserName <String>
        The textual identifier of ProjectWise user to assign new identity to.
        
        Required?                    true
        Position?                    0
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    <CommonParameters>
        This cmdlet supports the common parameters: Verbose, Debug,
        ErrorAction, ErrorVariable, WarningAction, WarningVariable,
        OutBuffer, PipelineVariable, and OutVariable. For more information, 
    see 
        about_CommonParameters 
    (https:/go.microsoft.com/fwlink/?LinkID=113216). 
    
INPUTS
    Bentley.ProjectWise.PowerShell.Common.UserIdentity
        You can pipe UserIdentity objects to Add-PWUserIdentity.
    
    
OUTPUTS
    Bentley.ProjectWise.PowerShell.Common.UserIdentity
        Add-PWUserIdentity returns an object that represents the new 
        Identity.
    
    
NOTES
    
    
        Add-PWUserIdentity requires an open ProjectWise Connection in the 
        current thread.  You can open a Connection with Open-PWConnection 
        before calling Add-PWUserIdentity.
        
        Add-PWUserIdentity requires an Admin session.  To open an admin 
        session, use the -Admin flag when opening the ProjectWise Connection 
        with Open-PWConnection.
    
    --------------------------- EXAMPLE1 ---------------------------
    
    Add-PWUserIdentity -UserName "user" -ProviderName 
    "https://ims.bentley.com/" -ProviderProtocol "STS" -Name 
    "user@bentley.com"
    
    Creates a new Federated Identity for ProjectWise user linked to STS 
    Identity Provider https://ims.bentley.com/ with identity 
    user@bentley.com.
    
    UserID Name             NameID ProviderID ------ ----             ------ 
    ----------      3 user@bentley.com                11
    
RELATED LINKS
    Add-PWUserIdentityProvider 
    Get-PWUserIdentity 
    Get-PWUserIdentityProvider 
    Remove-PWUserIdentity 
    Remove-PWUserIdentityProvider 
    Update-PWUserIdentityProvider

## Add-PWUserIdentityProvider


NAME
    Add-PWUserIdentityProvider
    
SYNOPSIS
    Defines new Federated Identity Provider in the current ProjectWise 
    Datasource.
    
    
SYNTAX
    Add-PWUserIdentityProvider [-Name] <String> [-Protocol] <String> 
    [[-Description] <String>] [<CommonParameters>]
    
    
DESCRIPTION
    The Add-PWUserIdentityProvider cmdlet defines new Federated Identity 
    Provider in the current ProjectWise Datasource.
    

PARAMETERS
    -Description <String>
        The user-friendly description of the new provider.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    2
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       True (ByPropertyName)
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -Name <String>
        The name/identity/URL of the Federated Identity Provider.
        
        Required?                    true
        Position?                    0
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       True (ByPropertyName)
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -Protocol <String>
        The protocol/type of the Identity Provider as string. Currently 
        supported options are "STS" and "OIDC".
        
        Required?                    true
        Position?                    1
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       True (ByPropertyName)
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    <CommonParameters>
        This cmdlet supports the common parameters: Verbose, Debug,
        ErrorAction, ErrorVariable, WarningAction, WarningVariable,
        OutBuffer, PipelineVariable, and OutVariable. For more information, 
    see 
        about_CommonParameters 
    (https:/go.microsoft.com/fwlink/?LinkID=113216). 
    
INPUTS
    Bentley.ProjectWise.PowerShell.Common.UserIdentityProvider
        You can pipe UserIdentityProviderobjects to 
        Add-PWUserIdentityProvider.
    
    
OUTPUTS
    Bentley.ProjectWise.PowerShell.Common.UserIdentityProvider
        Add-PWUserIdentityProvider returns an object that represents the new 
        Federated Identity Provider.
    
    
NOTES
    
    
        Add-PWUserIdentityProvider requires an open ProjectWise Connection 
        in the current thread.  You can open a Connection with 
        Open-PWConnection before calling Add-PWUserIdentityProvider .
        
        Add-PWUserIdentityProvider requires an Admin session.  To open an 
        admin session, use the -Admin flag when opening the ProjectWise 
        Connection with Open-PWConnection.
    
    --------------------------- EXAMPLE1 ---------------------------
    
    Add-PWUserIdentityProvider -Name "https://oidc.bentley.com/" -Protocol 
    "OIDC"
    
    Creates OIDC identity Provider record with service URL 
    https://oidc.bentley.com/
    
    ID          : 13 Name        : https://oidc.bentley.com/ Protocol    : 
    OIDC Description :
    
RELATED LINKS
    Add-PWUserIdentity 
    Get-PWUserIdentity 
    Get-PWUserIdentityProvider 
    Remove-PWUserIdentity 
    Remove-PWUserIdentityProvider 
    Update-PWUserIdentityProvider

## Add-PWUserListMember


NAME
    Add-PWUserListMember
    
SYNOPSIS
    Adds a User, Group, or UserList to a UserList in the current ProjectWise 
    Datasource.
    
    
SYNTAX
    Add-PWUserListMember [-ID] <Int32> -GroupID <Int32> [<CommonParameters>]
    
    Add-PWUserListMember [-ID] <Int32> -UserID <Int32> [<CommonParameters>]
    
    Add-PWUserListMember [-ID] <Int32> -UserListID <Int32> 
    [<CommonParameters>]
    
    
DESCRIPTION
    The Add-PWUserListMember cmdlet adds a User, Group, or UserList to a 
    User List in the current ProjectWise Datasource.
    
    Users, Groups, and UserLists can be selected by ID.
    

PARAMETERS
    -GroupID <Int32>
        The GroupID of the Group to add.
        
        Required?                    true
        Position?                    named
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       True (ByPropertyName)
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -ID <Int32>
        The ID of the User List to add to.
        
        Required?                    true
        Position?                    0
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -UserID <Int32>
        The UserID of the User to add.
        
        Required?                    true
        Position?                    named
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       True (ByPropertyName)
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -UserListID <Int32>
        The UserListID of the UserList to add.
        
        Required?                    true
        Position?                    named
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       True (ByPropertyName)
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    <CommonParameters>
        This cmdlet supports the common parameters: Verbose, Debug,
        ErrorAction, ErrorVariable, WarningAction, WarningVariable,
        OutBuffer, PipelineVariable, and OutVariable. For more information, 
    see 
        about_CommonParameters 
    (https:/go.microsoft.com/fwlink/?LinkID=113216). 
    
INPUTS
    None
        You can optionally pipe User, Group, or UserList objects to this 
        cmdlet.
    
    
OUTPUTS
    Bentley.ProjectWise.PowerShell.Common.UserListMember
        Get-PWUserListMember returns an object that represents the new User 
        List Member. To access the actual User, Group, or UserList object, 
        use the MemberObject property of the the returned UserListMember.
    
    
NOTES
    
    
        Add-PWUserListMember requires an open ProjectWise Connection in the 
        current thread.  You can open a Connection with Open-PWConnection 
        before calling Add-PWUserListMember.
        
        Add-PWUserListMember requires an Admin session.  To open an admin 
        session, use the -Admin flag when opening the ProjectWise Connection 
        with Open-PWConnection.
    
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 1 --------------------------
    
    Add-PWUserListMember 1 -UserID 1
    
    Add a User with UserID = 1 to the UserList with UserListID = 1
    
    UserListID MemberID Name                     Description             
    MemberType ---------- -------- ----                     -----------      
           ----------     1         1     admin                              
                     User
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 2 --------------------------
    
    Add-PWUserListMember 1 -GroupID 1
    
    Add a Group with GroupID = 1 to the UserList with UserListID = 1
    
    UserListID MemberID Name                     Description             
    MemberType ---------- -------- ----                     -----------      
           ----------     1         1     Administrators           
    Administrators            Group
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 3 --------------------------
    
    Add-PWUserListMember 1 -UserListID 4
    
    Add a UserList with UserListID = 4 to the UserList with UserListID = 1
    
    UserListID MemberID Name                     Description             
    MemberType ---------- -------- ----                     -----------      
           ----------     1         4     New Access List 2                  
                   UserList
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 4 --------------------------
    
    Get-PWUser -UserName admin | Add-PWUserListMember 1
    
    Add a User to the UserList with UserListID = 1
    
    UserListID MemberID Name                     Description             
    MemberType ---------- -------- ----                     -----------      
           ----------     1         1     admin                              
                     User
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 5 --------------------------
    
    Get-PWGroup -GroupName "Restr*" | Add-PWUserListMember 1
    
    Add a Group to the UserList with UserListID = 1
    
    UserListID MemberID Name                     Description             
    MemberType ---------- -------- ----                     -----------      
           ----------     1         2     Restricted Administrator 
    Administrator restri...  Group
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 6 --------------------------
    
    Get-PWUserList -UserListName New?A*Lis??2 | Add-PWUserListMember 1
    
    Add a UserList to the UserList with UserListID = 1
    
    UserListID MemberID Name                     Description             
    MemberType ---------- -------- ----                     -----------      
           ----------     1         4     New Access List 2                  
                   UserList
    
RELATED LINKS
    Get-PWUserList 
    Get-PWUserListMember 
    Remove-PWUserListMember 
    Update-PWUserList

## Add-PWUserNativeIdentity


NAME
    Add-PWUserNativeIdentity
    
SYNOPSIS
    Assigns a new password to a User in the current ProjectWise Datasource.
    
    
SYNTAX
    Add-PWUserNativeIdentity [-UserID] <Int32> [-Password] <Object> 
    [<CommonParameters>]
    
    Add-PWUserNativeIdentity [-UserName] <String> [-Password] <Object> 
    [<CommonParameters>]
    
    
DESCRIPTION
    The Add-PWUserNativeIdentity cmdlet assigns new password to a User in 
    the current ProjectWise Datasource.
    
    User can be selected either by ID or by Name.
    

PARAMETERS
    -Password <Object>
        The password value to assign.
        
        Required?                    true
        Position?                    1
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       True (ByPropertyName)
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -UserID <Int32>
        The numerical identifier of the User to assign new password to.
        
        Required?                    true
        Position?                    0
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       True (ByPropertyName)
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -UserName <String>
        The textual identifier of the User to assign new password to.
        
        Required?                    true
        Position?                    0
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       True (ByPropertyName)
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    <CommonParameters>
        This cmdlet supports the common parameters: Verbose, Debug,
        ErrorAction, ErrorVariable, WarningAction, WarningVariable,
        OutBuffer, PipelineVariable, and OutVariable. For more information, 
    see 
        about_CommonParameters 
    (https:/go.microsoft.com/fwlink/?LinkID=113216). 
    
INPUTS
    
OUTPUTS
    Bentley.ProjectWise.PowerShell.Common.User
        Add-PWUserIdentity returns an object that represents the User which 
        password was assigned to.
    
    
NOTES

        the current thread.  You can open a Connection with 
        Open-PWConnection before calling Add-PWUserNativeIdentity.

        admin session, use the -Admin flag when opening the ProjectWise 
        Connection with Open-PWConnection.

    (ConvertTo-SecureString "password" -AsPlainText -Force)
    
    Assigns password to ProjectWise user named "user". This password then 
    can be used to perform Native login to PWDI server.

## Add-PWUsersToCloudProject


NAME
    Add-PWUsersToCloudProject
    
SYNOPSIS
    Adds users to a Bentley Infrastructure Cloud project.
    
    
SYNTAX
    Add-PWUsersToCloudProject [[-InputUsers] <User[]>] [[-FolderPath] 
    <string>] [-OIDCToken] <OIDCAuthToken> [-Roles] <string[]> 
    [[-CloudProjectId] <string>] [[-Emails] <string[]>] [<CommonParameters>]
    
    
DESCRIPTION
    Adds users to a Bentley Infrastructure Cloud project. These users can be 
    an array of user objects from the pipeline or a comma separated list of 
    emails passed in with the Emails parameter. The project itself can be 
    specified by providing a work area path that has an associated Bentley 
    Infrastructure Cloud project or the GUID id.
    

PARAMETERS
    -InputUsers <User[]>
        Array of ProjectWise User objects from the pipeline.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    0
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       True (ByValue)
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -FolderPath <string>
        Path to the rich project from which we'll find the associated 
        Bentley Infrastructure Cloud project.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    1
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -OIDCToken <OIDCAuthToken>
        Authentication token. Obtain by using Get-OIDCToken -Authority 
        https://ims.bentley.com
        
        Required?                    true
        Position?                    2
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -Roles <string[]>
        Array of roles (e.g. Engineer,"Custom Project Role 1").
        
        Required?                    true
        Position?                    3
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -CloudProjectId <string>
        Pass Bentley Infrastructure Cloud Project Guid string.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    4
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -Emails <string[]>
        Array of user emails (e.g. 
        "user.one@companytwo.com","user.two@companythree.com").
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    5
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    <CommonParameters>
        This cmdlet supports the common parameters: Verbose, Debug,
        ErrorAction, ErrorVariable, WarningAction, WarningVariable,
        OutBuffer, PipelineVariable, and OutVariable. For more information, 
    see 
        about_CommonParameters 
    (https:/go.microsoft.com/fwlink/?LinkID=113216). 
    
INPUTS
    PWPS_Cloud.Common.Users.User[]
        Array of ProjectWise User objects from the pipeline.
    
    
OUTPUTS
    
NOTES
    
    
        
    
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 1 --------------------------
    
    # Add users matching a name pattern to a Bentley Infrastructure Cloud 
    project identified by folder path.
    $OIDCToken = Get-OIDCToken -Authority https://ims.bentley.com
    Get-PWUsersByMatch -UserName 'sam.jones','john.smith','*.williams' | 
    Add-PWUsersToCloudProject -OIDCToken $OIDCToken -Roles 
    "Engineer","Custom User Role 1" -FolderPath "Projects\Project A"
    
    
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 2 --------------------------
    
    # Create a Bentley Infrastructure Cloud project and add users identified 
    by their emails.
    $OIDCToken = Get-OIDCToken -Authority https://ims.bentley.com
    $CloudProjectId = Add-CloudProject -CloudProjectName "My Cloud Project" 
    -CloudProjectNumber "PN12345" -MakeUnique -OIDCToken $OIDCToken
    Add-PWUsersToCloudProject -CloudProjectId $CloudProjectId -OIDCToken 
    $OIDCToken -Emails "fred.jones@acme.com","sam.smith@acme.com" -Roles 
    "Engineer","Custom User Role 1"
    
    
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 3 --------------------------
    
    # Add users by email to an existing Bentley Infrastructure Cloud project 
    using its GUID.
    $OIDCToken = Get-OIDCToken -Authority https://ims.bentley.com
    Add-PWUsersToCloudProject -CloudProjectId 
    "a1b2c3d4-e5f6-7890-abcd-ef1234567890" -OIDCToken $OIDCToken -Emails 
    "alice@example.com","bob@example.com" -Roles "Project Manager"
    
    
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 4 --------------------------
    
    # Combine pipeline users and explicit emails to add to a Bentley 
    Infrastructure Cloud project.
    $OIDCToken = Get-OIDCToken -Authority https://ims.bentley.com
    Get-PWUsersByMatch -UserName 'team.lead*' | Add-PWUsersToCloudProject 
    -OIDCToken $OIDCToken -Emails "external.consultant@partner.com" -Roles 
    "Engineer" -FolderPath "Projects\Highway Bridge\Phase 2"
    
    
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 5 --------------------------
    
    # Add users with multiple roles to a Bentley Infrastructure Cloud 
    project identified by folder path.
    $OIDCToken = Get-OIDCToken -Authority https://ims.bentley.com
    Add-PWUsersToCloudProject -OIDCToken $OIDCToken -Emails 
    "jane.doe@company.com","mark.taylor@company.com" -Roles 
    "Engineer","Design Reviewer","Inspector" -FolderPath "Projects\Building 
    Renovation"
    
    
    
RELATED LINKS





--

## Add-PWUsersToCloudProjectByGroup


NAME
    Add-PWUsersToCloudProjectByGroup
    
SYNOPSIS
    Takes group name(s) and associates users in the group(s) with a Bentley 
    Infrastructure Cloud project.
    
    
SYNTAX
    Add-PWUsersToCloudProjectByGroup [[-FolderPath] <string>] [[-FolderId] 
    <int>] [-Groups] <string[]> [-OIDCToken] <OIDCAuthToken> [-Roles] 
    <string[]> [[-CloudProjectId] <string>] [<CommonParameters>]
    
    
DESCRIPTION
    Takes group name(s) and associates users in the group(s) with a Bentley 
    Infrastructure Cloud Project. The Bentley Infrastructure Cloud Project 
    may be identified by its GUID ID or a ProjectWise folder path, or a 
    ProjectWise folder ID. Only users with email addresses specified will be 
    added to the Bentley Infrastructure Cloud project.
    

PARAMETERS
    -FolderPath <string>
        Path to the workarea from which we'll find the associated Bentley 
        Infrastructure Cloud project.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    0
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -FolderId <int>
        Id of workarea from which we'll find the associated Bentley 
        Infrastructure Cloud project.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    1
        Default value                0
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -Groups <string[]>
        Group name containing participants to add to the Bentley 
        Infrastructure Cloud project.
        
        Required?                    true
        Position?                    2
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -OIDCToken <OIDCAuthToken>
        Authentication token. Obtain by using Get-OIDCToken -Authority 
        https://ims.bentley.com
        
        Required?                    true
        Position?                    3
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -Roles <string[]>
        Array of roles.
        
        Required?                    true
        Position?                    4
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -CloudProjectId <string>
        Pass Bentley Infrastructure Cloud Project Guid string if you have it 
        instead of folders.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    5
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    <CommonParameters>
        This cmdlet supports the common parameters: Verbose, Debug,
        ErrorAction, ErrorVariable, WarningAction, WarningVariable,
        OutBuffer, PipelineVariable, and OutVariable. For more information, 
    see 
        about_CommonParameters 
    (https:/go.microsoft.com/fwlink/?LinkID=113216). 
    
INPUTS
    
OUTPUTS
    
NOTES
    
    
        
    
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 1 --------------------------
    
    # Add users from the "Project Managers" group to a Bentley 
    Infrastructure Cloud project
    # identified by a work area folder path.
    $OIDCToken = Get-OIDCToken -Authority https://ims.bentley.com
    Add-PWUsersToCloudProjectByGroup -OIDCToken $OIDCToken -FolderPath 
    "Projects\Parkland Division\Project A" -Groups "Project Managers" -Roles 
    "Manage"
    
    
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 2 --------------------------
    
    # Add users from multiple groups to a Bentley Infrastructure Cloud 
    project using a CloudProjectId.
    $OIDCToken = Get-OIDCToken -Authority https://ims.bentley.com
    Add-PWUsersToCloudProjectByGroup -OIDCToken $OIDCToken -CloudProjectId 
    "a1b2c3d4-e5f6-7890-abcd-ef1234567890" -Groups "Engineers","Designers" 
    -Roles "Engineer","Design Reviewer"
    
    
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 3 --------------------------
    
    # Add users from a group to a Bentley Infrastructure Cloud project 
    identified by folder ID.
    $OIDCToken = Get-OIDCToken -Authority https://ims.bentley.com
    Add-PWUsersToCloudProjectByGroup -OIDCToken $OIDCToken -FolderId 1234 
    -Groups "Site Inspectors" -Roles "Inspector"
    
    
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 4 --------------------------
    
    # Add users from a single group with multiple roles.
    $OIDCToken = Get-OIDCToken -Authority https://ims.bentley.com
    Add-PWUsersToCloudProjectByGroup -OIDCToken $OIDCToken -FolderPath 
    "Projects\Highway Bridge\Phase 1" -Groups "Bridge Team" -Roles 
    "Engineer","Project Manager"
    
    
    
RELATED LINKS





--

## Add-PWUsersToCloudProjectByUserLists


NAME
    Add-PWUsersToCloudProjectByUserLists
    
SYNOPSIS
    Adds users in userlists to Bentley Infrastructure Cloud Projects.
    
    
SYNTAX
    Add-PWUsersToCloudProjectByUserLists [[-InputFolders] 
    <ProjectWiseFolder[]>] [[-UserLists] <string[]>] [-OIDCToken] 
    <OIDCAuthToken> [[-Filter] <string>] [-Roles] <string[]> 
    [[-CloudProjectId] <string>] [<CommonParameters>]
    
    
DESCRIPTION
    Takes an array of folders and associates users in the passed user lists 
    or in all the project's user lists with the associated Bentley 
    Infrastructure Cloud project. Or takes a Bentley Infrastructure Cloud 
    project id instead of an array of folders.
    

PARAMETERS
    -InputFolders <ProjectWiseFolder[]>
        Array of ProjectWise folders from pipeline. The work areas with 
        Bentley Infrastructure Cloud projects will have users added.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    0
        Default value                
        PWPS_Cloud.CommonTypes+ProjectWiseFolder[]
        Accept pipeline input?       True (ByValue)
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -UserLists <string[]>
        Users Lists containing participants to add to the Bentley 
        Infrastructure Cloud projects. You can only leave this blank if 
        passing in work areas instead of a Bentley Infrastructure Cloud 
        project ID. If left blank, all users in the project user lists will 
        be added.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    1
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -OIDCToken <OIDCAuthToken>
        Authentication token. Obtain by using Get-OIDCToken -Authority 
        https://ims.bentley.com
        
        Required?                    true
        Position?                    2
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -Filter <string>
        Optional string wildcard filter to select the resolved user lists. 
        Originally intended for the case where input folders are passed in 
        instead of the Bentley Infrastructure Cloud project id and no user 
        lists are passed in.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    3
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -Roles <string[]>
        Array of roles.
        
        Required?                    true
        Position?                    4
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -CloudProjectId <string>
        Pass Bentley Infrastructure Cloud Project Guid string if you have it 
        instead of folders.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    5
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    <CommonParameters>
        This cmdlet supports the common parameters: Verbose, Debug,
        ErrorAction, ErrorVariable, WarningAction, WarningVariable,
        OutBuffer, PipelineVariable, and OutVariable. For more information, 
    see 
        about_CommonParameters 
    (https:/go.microsoft.com/fwlink/?LinkID=113216). 
    
INPUTS
    PWPS_Cloud.CommonTypes+ProjectWiseFolder[]
        Array of ProjectWise folders from pipeline. The work areas with 
        Bentley Infrastructure Cloud projects will have users added.
    
    
OUTPUTS
    
NOTES
    
    
        
    
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 1 --------------------------
    
    # Add all users from all user lists to each associated Bentley 
    Infrastructure Cloud Project in the entire datasource.
    New-PWLogin
    $OIDCToken = Get-OIDCToken -Authority https://ims.bentley.com
    Get-PWWorkAreas | Add-PWUsersToCloudProjectByUserLists -OIDCToken 
    $OIDCToken -Roles "Project Manager"
    
    
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 2 --------------------------
    
    # Add all users from filtered user lists to the Bentley Infrastructure 
    Cloud Projects.
    New-PWLogin
    $OIDCToken = Get-OIDCToken -Authority https://ims.bentley.com
    Get-PWFolders -FolderPath 'Projects\2020-05-19' | 
    Add-PWUsersToCloudProjectByUserLists -Filter "*Managers*" -Roles 
    "Project Manager" -OIDCToken $OIDCToken
    
    
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 3 --------------------------
    
    # Create a Bentley Infrastructure Cloud project and add users from 
    specific user lists.
    New-PWLogin
    $OIDCToken = Get-OIDCToken -Authority https://ims.bentley.com
    $CloudProjectId = Add-CloudProject -CloudProjectName MyCloudProjectName 
    -CloudProjectNumber MyCloudProjectNumber -OIDCToken $OIDCToken
    Add-PWUsersToCloudProjectByUserLists -CloudProjectId $CloudProjectId 
    -UserLists userlist1,userlist2 -Roles Engineer -OIDCToken $OIDCToken
    
    
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 4 --------------------------
    
    # Add users from specific user lists with multiple roles to a Bentley 
    Infrastructure Cloud project identified by its ID.
    New-PWLogin
    $OIDCToken = Get-OIDCToken -Authority https://ims.bentley.com
    Add-PWUsersToCloudProjectByUserLists -CloudProjectId 
    "a1b2c3d4-e5f6-7890-abcd-ef1234567890" -UserLists "Structural 
    Engineers","QA Team" -Roles "Engineer","Design Reviewer" -OIDCToken 
    $OIDCToken
    
    
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 5 --------------------------
    
    # Add users from all user lists matching a wildcard filter for a 
    specific folder and its sub-projects.
    New-PWLogin
    $OIDCToken = Get-OIDCToken -Authority https://ims.bentley.com
    Get-PWFolders -FolderPath 'Projects\Highway Bridge' | 
    Add-PWUsersToCloudProjectByUserLists -Filter "*Review*" -Roles 
    "Reviewer" -OIDCToken $OIDCToken
    
    
    
RELATED LINKS





--

## Add-PWUserWindowsIdentity


NAME
    Add-PWUserWindowsIdentity
    
SYNOPSIS
    Assigns a new Windows Domain Identity to a User in the current 
    ProjectWise Datasource.
    
    
SYNTAX
    Add-PWUserWindowsIdentity [-UserID] <Int32> [-DomainID] <Int32> [[-Name] 
    <String>] [<CommonParameters>]
    
    Add-PWUserWindowsIdentity [-UserName] <String> [-DomainID] <Int32> 
    [[-Name] <String>] [<CommonParameters>]
    
    Add-PWUserWindowsIdentity [-UserName] <String> [-DomainName] <String> 
    [[-Name] <String>] [<CommonParameters>]
    
    Add-PWUserWindowsIdentity [-UserID] <Int32> [-DomainName] <String> 
    [[-Name] <String>] [<CommonParameters>]
    
    
DESCRIPTION
    The Add-PWUserWindowsIdentity cmdlet assigns new Windows Domain Identity 
    belonging to specified Windows Domain to a User in the current 
    ProjectWise Datasource.
    
    User and Windows Domain can be selected either by their IDs or by Names.
    

PARAMETERS
    -DomainID <Int32>
        The numerical identifier of the Windows Domain to create Windows 
        Domain Identity for.
        
        Required?                    true
        Position?                    1
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       True (ByPropertyName)
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -DomainName <String>
        The name of the Windows Domain to create Windows Domain Identity for.
        
        Required?                    true
        Position?                    1
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -Name <String>
        The Windows Name of the Windows Domain Identity to create.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    2
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       True (ByPropertyName)
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -UserID <Int32>
        The numerical identifier of ProjectWise user to assign new identity 
        to.
        
        Required?                    true
        Position?                    0
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       True (ByPropertyName)
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -UserName <String>
        The textual identifier of ProjectWise user to assign new identity to.
        
        Required?                    true
        Position?                    0
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    <CommonParameters>
        This cmdlet supports the common parameters: Verbose, Debug,
        ErrorAction, ErrorVariable, WarningAction, WarningVariable,
        OutBuffer, PipelineVariable, and OutVariable. For more information, 
    see 
        about_CommonParameters 
    (https:/go.microsoft.com/fwlink/?LinkID=113216). 
    
INPUTS
    Bentley.ProjectWise.PowerShell.Common.WindowsIdentity
        You can pipe WindowsIdentity objects to Add-PWUserWindowsIdentity.
    
    
OUTPUTS
    Bentley.ProjectWise.PowerShell.Common.WindowsIdentity
        Add-PWUserWindowsIdentity returns an object that represents the new 
        Windows Domain Identity.
    
    
NOTES
    
    
        Add-PWUserWindowsIdentity requires an open ProjectWise Connection in 
        the current thread.  You can open a Connection with 
        Open-PWConnection before calling Add-PWUserWindowsIdentity.
        
        Add-PWUserWindowsIdentity requires an Admin session.  To open an 
        admin session, use the -Admin flag when opening the ProjectWise 
        Connection with Open-PWConnection.
    
    --------------------------- EXAMPLE1 ---------------------------
    
    Add-PWUserWindowsIdentity -UserID 3 -DomainID 1 -Name john.doe
    
    Creates a new Windows Domain Identity for ProjectWise user with numeric 
    id 3 linked to Windows Domain with numeric id 1 with Windows Name 
    john.doe.
    
    UserID Name     DomainID ------ ----     --------      3 john.doe        
    1
    
RELATED LINKS
    Add-PWUserWindowsDomain 
    Get-PWUserWindowsDomain 
    Get-PWUserWindowsIdentity 
    Remove-PWUserWindowsDomain 
    Remove-PWUserWindowsIdentity 
    Update-PWUserWindowsDomain

## CheckIn-PWDocumentsOrFree


NAME
    CheckIn-PWDocumentsOrFree
    
SYNOPSIS
    Check in, free or Import exported documents passed in the pipeline.
    
    
SYNTAX
    CheckIn-PWDocumentsOrFree [-InputDocument] <ProjectWiseDocument[]> 
    [-Free] [-ImportFolder <string>] [<CommonParameters>]
    
    
DESCRIPTION
    Input array of ProjectWiseDocument objects from the pipeline (Required). 
    Use Get-PWDocumentBySearch to feed pipeline. Include the -Free switch 
    parameter to free the documents. The cmdlet will determine if a document 
    is CheckedOut or Exported and either checkin or import the document. If 
    the local file is missing, the file will be freed regardless of whether 
    or not the -Free switch parameter is included.
    

PARAMETERS
    -InputDocument <ProjectWiseDocument[]>
        
        
        Required?                    true
        Position?                    0
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       True (ByValue)
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -Free [<SwitchParameter>]
        
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    named
        Default value                False
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -ImportFolder <string>
        
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    named
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    <CommonParameters>
        This cmdlet supports the common parameters: Verbose, Debug,
        ErrorAction, ErrorVariable, WarningAction, WarningVariable,
        OutBuffer, PipelineVariable, and OutVariable. For more information, 
    see 
        about_CommonParameters 
    (https:/go.microsoft.com/fwlink/?LinkID=113216). 
    
INPUTS
    PWPS_DAB.CommonTypes+ProjectWiseDocument[]
        
    
    
OUTPUTS
    
NOTES
    
    
        
    
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 1 --------------------------
    
    #Example usage for checking in document(s).
    #Get the documents in the folder where filename like *.dgn and check 
    them in.
    Get-PWDocumentsBySearch -FolderPath 'Microstation J' -FileName '%.dgn' | 
    CheckIn-PWDocumentsOrFree | Select-Object FileName
    #Should check in all the checked out documents passed to the cmdlet
    
    
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 2 --------------------------
    
    #Example usage for freeing a document(s).
    #Get the documents in the folder where filename like *.dgn and check 
    them in.
    Get-PWDocumentsBySearch -FolderPath 'Microstation J' -FileName '%.dgn' | 
    CheckIn-PWDocumentsOrFree -Free | Select-Object FileName
    #Should free all the checked out documents passed to the cmdlet
    
    
    
RELATED LINKS

## CheckOut-PWDocuments


NAME
    CheckOut-PWDocuments
    
SYNOPSIS
    Checks out, exports, or copies out ProjectWise documents.
    
    
SYNTAX
    CheckOut-PWDocuments [-InputDocument] <ProjectWiseDocument[]> 
    [[-Export]] [[-ExportFolder] <string>] [[-NoReferences]] 
    [[-RemoveCopies]] [<CommonParameters>]
    
    CheckOut-PWDocuments [-InputDocument] <ProjectWiseDocument[]> 
    [[-CopyOut]] [[-ExportFolder] <string>] [[-NoReferences]] 
    [[-RemoveCopies]] [<CommonParameters>]
    
    
DESCRIPTION
    Checks out ProjectWise documents to your working directory, or exports 
    or copies them to a local folder. Supply document objects through 
    `InputDocument`.
    
    Use `Export` or `CopyOut` to create local copies without checking 
    documents out. Use `NoReferences` when related files are not needed.
    

PARAMETERS
    -InputDocument <ProjectWiseDocument[]>
        Input array of ProjectWiseDocument objects from the pipeline 
        (Required). Use Get-PWDocumentBySearch or New-PWDocument to feed 
        pipeline.
        
        Required?                    true
        Position?                    0
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       True (ByValue)
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -Export [<SwitchParameter>]
        Use this switch to Export documents rather then checking them out. 
        Must include ExportFolder.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    1
        Default value                False
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -ExportFolder <string>
        Provide an export location for exports (will create if it does not 
        exist).
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    3
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -CopyOut [<SwitchParameter>]
        Use this switch to copy out documents rather then checking them out.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    2
        Default value                False
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -NoReferences [<SwitchParameter>]
        Use this switch to skip the references on a copy out.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    4
        Default value                False
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -RemoveCopies [<SwitchParameter>]
        Use this switch in the fetch-files scenario where it is unnecessary 
        to keep the documents in the working folder. The copy out should 
        have handled getting the files cached on the caching server. No need 
        to double the storage requirements.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    5
        Default value                False
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    <CommonParameters>
        This cmdlet supports the common parameters: Verbose, Debug,
        ErrorAction, ErrorVariable, WarningAction, WarningVariable,
        OutBuffer, PipelineVariable, and OutVariable. For more information, 
    see 
        about_CommonParameters 
    (https:/go.microsoft.com/fwlink/?LinkID=113216). 
    
INPUTS
    PWPS_DAB.CommonTypes+ProjectWiseDocument[]
        Input array of ProjectWiseDocument objects from the pipeline 
        (Required). Use Get-PWDocumentBySearch or New-PWDocument to feed 
        pipeline.
    
    
OUTPUTS
    
NOTES
    
    
        
    
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 1 --------------------------
    
    Get-PWDocumentsBySearch -FolderPath 'Microstation J' -FileName '%.dgn' | 
    CheckOut-PWDocuments | Select-Object CheckedOutLocalFileName
    
    
    
RELATED LINKS

PARAMETERS
