## Clear-PWFolderWorkflow


NAME
    Clear-PWFolderWorkflow
    
SYNOPSIS
    Removes the workflow assignment from the specified ProjectWise folder(s).
    
    
SYNTAX
    Clear-PWFolderWorkflow [-InputFolder] <ProjectWiseFolder[]> 
    [<CommonParameters>]
    
    
DESCRIPTION
    The Clear-PWFolderWorkflow cmdlet removes the workflow assignment from 
    one or more ProjectWise folders.
    
    This cmdlet only affects folders that have a workflow currently 
    assigned. Folders without a workflow are silently skipped. The cmdlet 
    does not produce any output; it performs the workflow removal action on 
    each valid folder provided.
    
    The user must have rights to change project state for the operation to 
    succeed.
    

PARAMETERS
    -InputFolder <ProjectWiseFolder[]>
        Specifies one or more ProjectWise folders from which to remove the 
        workflow assignment. You can obtain ProjectWiseFolder objects using 
        the Get-PWFolders cmdlet or the Show-PWFolderBrowserDialog cmdlet.
        
        This parameter accepts pipeline input, allowing you to pipe folder 
        objects directly from other cmdlets.
        
        Required?                    true
        Position?                    0
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       True (ByValue)
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    <CommonParameters>
        This cmdlet supports the common parameters: Verbose, Debug,
        ErrorAction, ErrorVariable, WarningAction, WarningVariable,
        OutBuffer, PipelineVariable, and OutVariable. For more information, 
    see 
        about_CommonParameters 
    (https:/go.microsoft.com/fwlink/?LinkID=113216). 
    
INPUTS
    PWPS_DAB.CommonTypes+ProjectWiseFolder[]
        You can pipe ProjectWiseFolder objects to this cmdlet. Use 
        Get-PWFolders or Show-PWFolderBrowserDialog to obtain these objects.
    
    
OUTPUTS
    None
        This cmdlet does not generate any output. It performs the workflow 
        removal action silently. Use the -Verbose switch to see progress 
        information.
    
    
NOTES
    
    
        - The user must be connected to a ProjectWise datasource before 
        running this cmdlet.
        
        - The user must have rights to change project state for the folders 
        being modified.
        
        - Folders that do not have a workflow assigned are silently skipped.
        
        - Folders with invalid ProjectID values (less than or equal to 0) 
        are silently skipped.
        
        - This cmdlet only removes the workflow assignment from the folder; 
        it does not affect documents within the folder or their workflow 
        states.
        
        
        ## RELATED LINKS
        
    
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 1 --------------------------
    
    Clear-PWFolderWorkflow -InputFolder (Show-PWFolderBrowserDialog) -Verbose
    
    Opens an interactive folder browser dialog to select a folder, then 
    removes the workflow assignment from the selected folder. The -Verbose 
    switch displays progress information.
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 2 --------------------------
    
    Clear-PWFolderWorkflow -InputFolder (Get-PWFolders -FolderPath 
    'Projects\Civil\Design' -JustOne)
    
    Retrieves the folder at the specified path and removes its workflow 
    assignment. The -JustOne switch ensures only the specified folder is 
    returned, not its subfolders.
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 3 --------------------------
    
    Get-PWFolders -FolderPath 'Projects\Civil' | Clear-PWFolderWorkflow 
    -Verbose
    
    Retrieves all folders under the 'Projects\Civil' path and pipes them to 
    Clear-PWFolderWorkflow, which removes the workflow assignment from each 
    folder that has one.
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 4 --------------------------
    
    Get-PWFolders -FolderName 'Submittals' | Clear-PWFolderWorkflow
    
    Searches for all folders named 'Submittals' in the datasource and 
    removes the workflow assignment from each one.
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 5 --------------------------
    
    Get-PWFolders -FolderPath 'Projects\Building' | Where-Object { 
    $_.WorkflowID -gt 0 } | Clear-PWFolderWorkflow -Verbose
    
    Retrieves all folders under 'Projects\Building', filters to include only 
    those with a workflow assigned (WorkflowID greater than 0), and removes 
    the workflow from each. This approach provides visibility into which 
    folders will be affected before the operation.
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 6 --------------------------
    
    $folders = Get-PWFolders -FolderPath 'Projects\Mechanical'
    Clear-PWFolderWorkflow -InputFolder $folders -Verbose
    
    Stores the retrieved folders in a variable, then passes the variable to 
    the -InputFolder parameter. This is useful when you need to inspect or 
    manipulate the folder list before clearing workflows.
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 7 --------------------------
    
    $foldersWithWorkflow = Get-PWFolders -FolderPath 'Projects\Electrical' | 
    Where-Object { $_.WorkflowID -gt 0 }
    $foldersWithWorkflow | Select-Object Name, FullPath, Workflow
    $foldersWithWorkflow | Clear-PWFolderWorkflow -Verbose
    
    First retrieves and filters folders that have workflows assigned, 
    displays their details for review, and then clears the workflows. This 
    approach allows you to verify which folders will be affected.
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 8 --------------------------
    
    Clear-PWFolderWorkflow -InputFolder (Get-PWFolders -FolderID 12345 
    -JustOne)
    
    Retrieves a specific folder by its numeric Folder ID and removes its 
    workflow assignment.
    
RELATED LINKS





--

## Convert-PWFolderToWorkArea


NAME
    Convert-PWFolderToWorkArea
    
SYNOPSIS
    Converts a ProjectWise folder to a Work Area.
    
    
SYNTAX
    Convert-PWFolderToWorkArea [-InputFolder] 
    <CommonTypes.ProjectWiseFolder> [[-ProjectType] <String>] [-SetType] 
    [<CommonParameters>]
    
    Convert-PWFolderToWorkArea [-FolderPath] <String> [[-ProjectType] 
    <String>] [-SetType] [<CommonParameters>]
    
    Convert-PWFolderToWorkArea [-FolderID] <Int32> [[-ProjectType] <String>] 
    [-SetType] [<CommonParameters>]
    
    
DESCRIPTION
    Converts the specified folder to a Work Area. A Work Area is a special 
    kind of folder in ProjectWise that can have project properties and a 
    Project Type assigned to it.
    
    The folder can be specified using an InputFolder object, a FolderPath, 
    or a FolderID. Optionally, a ProjectType can be provided to assign a 
    specific type to the Work Area during conversion.
    
    If the folder is already a Work Area and you want to update its Project 
    Type, use the `-SetType` switch parameter along with the `-ProjectType` 
    parameter.
    
    Returns `$true` on successful conversion.
    

PARAMETERS
    -InputFolder <CommonTypes.ProjectWiseFolder>
        Specifies a ProjectWise folder object to be converted to a Work 
        Area. This object can be obtained using cmdlets such as 
        `Show-PWFolderBrowserDialog` or `Get-PWFolders`.
        
        Required?                    true
        Position?                    0
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       True (ByValue)
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -FolderPath <String>
        Specifies the full folder path of the folder to be converted to a 
        Work Area.
        
        Required?                    true
        Position?                    0
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -FolderID <Int32>
        Specifies the folder ID of the folder to be converted to a Work Area.
        
        Required?                    true
        Position?                    0
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -ProjectType <String>
        Specifies the Project Type name to assign to the Work Area. The 
        Project Type must exist in the current datasource. If not specified, 
        the folder is converted to a Work Area without a specific Project 
        Type.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    1
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -SetType [<SwitchParameter>]
        When specified, allows updating the Project Type of a folder that is 
        already a Work Area. This switch must be used in combination with 
        the `-ProjectType` parameter when the target folder is already a 
        Work Area.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    named
        Default value                False
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    <CommonParameters>
        This cmdlet supports the common parameters: Verbose, Debug,
        ErrorAction, ErrorVariable, WarningAction, WarningVariable,
        OutBuffer, PipelineVariable, and OutVariable. For more information, 
    see 
        about_CommonParameters 
    (https:/go.microsoft.com/fwlink/?LinkID=113216). 
    
INPUTS
    CommonTypes.ProjectWiseFolder
        You can pipe a ProjectWiseFolder object to this cmdlet using the 
        `-InputFolder` parameter.
    
    
OUTPUTS
    System.Boolean
        Returns `$true` when the folder is successfully converted to a Work 
        Area.
    
    
NOTES
    
    
        - If the folder is already a Work Area and the `-SetType` switch is 
        not included, the cmdlet displays a warning and exits without making 
        changes.
        
        - If the specified `-ProjectType` does not exist in the current 
        datasource, an error is returned.
        
        - The folder must exist in the current datasource. If the folder is 
        not found, a warning is displayed.
    
    EXAMPLE 1: Convert a folder to a Work Area using folder browser dialog
    
    Convert-PWFolderToWorkArea -InputFolder (Show-PWFolderBrowserDialog)
    
    Converts the selected folder to a Work Area without assigning a Project 
    Type.
    - EXAMPLE 2: Convert a folder to a Work Area using folder path -
    
    Convert-PWFolderToWorkArea -FolderPath 'Projects\MyFolder'
    
    Converts the folder at the specified path to a Work Area without 
    assigning a Project Type.
    -- EXAMPLE 3: Convert a folder to a Work Area using folder ID --
    
    Convert-PWFolderToWorkArea -FolderID 70032
    
    Converts the folder with the specified ID to a Work Area without 
    assigning a Project Type.
    EXAMPLE 4: Convert a folder to a Work Area with a Project Type using 
    InputFolder
    
    Convert-PWFolderToWorkArea -InputFolder (Show-PWFolderBrowserDialog) 
    -ProjectType 'Building'
    
    Converts the selected folder to a Work Area and assigns the 'Building' 
    Project Type.
    EXAMPLE 5: Convert a folder to a Work Area with a Project Type using 
    folder path
    
    Convert-PWFolderToWorkArea -FolderPath 'Projects\MyFolder' -ProjectType 
    'Civil'
    
    Converts the folder at the specified path to a Work Area and assigns the 
    'Civil' Project Type.
    EXAMPLE 6: Convert a folder to a Work Area with a Project Type using 
    folder ID
    
    Convert-PWFolderToWorkArea -FolderID 70032 -ProjectType 'Building'
    
    Converts the folder with the specified ID to a Work Area and assigns the 
    'Building' Project Type.
    - EXAMPLE 7: Update the Project Type of an existing Work Area -
    
    Convert-PWFolderToWorkArea -FolderID 70032 -ProjectType 'Civil' -SetType
    
    Updates the Project Type of an existing Work Area to 'Civil'. The 
    `-SetType` switch is required when the folder is already a Work Area.
    EXAMPLE 8: Update the Project Type of an existing Work Area using folder 
    path
    
    Convert-PWFolderToWorkArea -FolderPath 'Projects\MyWorkArea' 
    -ProjectType 'General' -SetType
    
    Updates the Project Type of an existing Work Area at the specified path 
    to 'General'.
    
RELATED LINKS
    Get-PWWorkAreas

## Convert-PWWorkAreaToFolder


NAME
    Convert-PWWorkAreaToFolder
    
SYNOPSIS
    Converts a Work Area and sub-Work Areas to folders.
    
    
SYNTAX
    Convert-PWWorkAreaToFolder [-InputFolder] <ProjectWiseFolder> 
    [-DowngradeSubProjects] [<CommonParameters>]
    
    Convert-PWWorkAreaToFolder [-FolderPath] <String> 
    [-DowngradeSubProjects] [<CommonParameters>]
    
    
DESCRIPTION
    Converts the specified Work Area to a folder. This cmdlet downgrades a 
    ProjectWise Work Area to a regular folder. Optionally, all sub-Work 
    Areas within the specified Work Area can also be downgraded to folders 
    using the DowngradeSubProjects switch.
    

PARAMETERS
    -InputFolder <ProjectWiseFolder>
        ProjectWise folder object to downgrade to a folder.
        
        Required?                    true
        Position?                    0
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       True (ByValue)
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -FolderPath <String>
        Full folder path of Work Area to downgrade to a folder.
        
        Required?                    true
        Position?                    0
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -DowngradeSubProjects [<SwitchParameter>]
        When included, all sub-Work Areas within the specified Work Area 
        will also be downgraded to folders.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    named
        Default value                False
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    <CommonParameters>
        This cmdlet supports the common parameters: Verbose, Debug,
        ErrorAction, ErrorVariable, WarningAction, WarningVariable,
        OutBuffer, PipelineVariable, and OutVariable. For more information, 
    see 
        about_CommonParameters 
    (https:/go.microsoft.com/fwlink/?LinkID=113216). 
    
INPUTS
    PWPS_DAB.CommonTypes+ProjectWiseFolder
        ProjectWise folder object to downgrade to a folder.
    
    
OUTPUTS
    System.Boolean
        Returns True if the Work Area was successfully downgraded to a 
        folder.
    
    
NOTES
    
    
        - The specified folder must be a Work Area. If the folder is not a 
        Work Area, a warning will be displayed.
        
        - The folder must exist in the current datasource.
        
        
        ## RELATED LINKS
        
    
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 1 --------------------------
    
    Convert-PWWorkAreaToFolder -InputFolder (Show-PWFolderBrowserDialog) 
    -Verbose
    
    Converts a single Work Area selected via the folder browser dialog to a 
    folder.
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 2 --------------------------
    
    Get-PWFolders -FolderPath "Projects\MyWorkArea" | 
    Convert-PWWorkAreaToFolder -Verbose
    
    Converts a Work Area to a folder using pipeline input from Get-PWFolders.
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 3 --------------------------
    
    Convert-PWWorkAreaToFolder -FolderPath "Projects\MyWorkArea" -Verbose
    
    Converts a single Work Area specified by its folder path to a folder.
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 4 --------------------------
    
    Convert-PWWorkAreaToFolder -InputFolder (Show-PWFolderBrowserDialog) 
    -DowngradeSubProjects -Verbose
    
    Converts a Work Area and all its sub-Work Areas to folders using the 
    folder browser dialog.
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 5 --------------------------
    
    Convert-PWWorkAreaToFolder -FolderPath "Projects\MyWorkArea" 
    -DowngradeSubProjects -Verbose
    
    Converts a Work Area and all its sub-Work Areas to folders using the 
    folder path.
    
RELATED LINKS

## CopyOut-PWDocuments


NAME
    CopyOut-PWDocuments
    
SYNOPSIS
    Copies ProjectWise documents to the working directory.
    
    
SYNTAX
    CopyOut-PWDocuments [-InputDocument] <ProjectWiseDocument[]> 
    [[-Refresh]] [[-OverwriteLocalCopy]] [[-KeepUpToDateLocalCopy]] 
    [[-NoReferences]] [[-RemoveCopies]] [<CommonParameters>]
    
    
DESCRIPTION
    Copies ProjectWise documents to your working directory without checking 
    them out. Supply document objects directly or through the pipeline.
    
    Related files are included by default for DGN and DWG documents. Use 
    `NoReferences` to copy only the selected documents.
    

PARAMETERS
    -InputDocument <ProjectWiseDocument[]>
        Input array of ProjectWiseDocument objects from the pipeline 
        (Required). Use Get-PWDocumentBySearch or New-PWDocument to feed 
        pipeline.
        
        Required?                    true
        Position?                    0
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       True (ByValue)
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -Refresh [<SwitchParameter>]
        Use this switch to refresh of the local copy.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    1
        Default value                False
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -OverwriteLocalCopy [<SwitchParameter>]
        Use this switch to copy over local copy with copy from server 
        regardless of local copy has been changed. It must be used with the 
        NoReferences switch. It cannot be used with the 
        KeepUpToDateLocalCopy switch.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    2
        Default value                False
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -KeepUpToDateLocalCopy [<SwitchParameter>]
        Use this switch to copy over an up to date file in the working 
        directory. It must be used with the NoReferences switch. It cannot 
        be used with the OverwriteLocalCopy switch.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    3
        Default value                False
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -NoReferences [<SwitchParameter>]
        Use this switch to skip the references on a copy out.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    4
        Default value                False
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -RemoveCopies [<SwitchParameter>]
        Use this switch in the fetch-files scenario where it is unnecessary 
        to keep the documents in the working folder. The copy out should 
        have handled getting the files cached on the caching server. No need 
        to double the storage requirements.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    5
        Default value                False
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    <CommonParameters>
        This cmdlet supports the common parameters: Verbose, Debug,
        ErrorAction, ErrorVariable, WarningAction, WarningVariable,
        OutBuffer, PipelineVariable, and OutVariable. For more information, 
    see 
        about_CommonParameters 
    (https:/go.microsoft.com/fwlink/?LinkID=113216). 
    
INPUTS
    PWPS_DAB.CommonTypes+ProjectWiseDocument[]
        Input array of ProjectWiseDocument objects from the pipeline 
        (Required). Use Get-PWDocumentBySearch or New-PWDocument to feed 
        pipeline.
    
    
OUTPUTS
    
NOTES
    
    
        
    
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 1 --------------------------
    
    New-PWLogin -DatasourceName Server:DSN
    #Gets the documents in this folder and subfolders where the file name 
    pattern matches %.dwg
    $docs = Get-PWDocumentsBySearch -FolderPath 'BSI900 - Adelaide Tower\' 
    -FileName '%.dwg'
    #Copies out all the found DWGs 
    $docs | CopyOut-PWDocuments
    
    
    
RELATED LINKS

## Copy-PWDocument


NAME
    Copy-PWDocument
    
SYNOPSIS
    Copy-PWDocument copies input document from path specification or from 
    input document object to TargetFolderPath with optional new document 
    name and/or filename.
    
    
SYNTAX
    Copy-PWDocument [[-InputDocument] <ProjectWiseDocument>] 
    [[-SourceDocumentNamePath] <string>] [-TargetFolderPath] <string> 
    [[-NewDocumentName] <string>] [[-NewFileName] <string>] 
    [-CreateVersions] [-DoNotCopyReferenceRelations] [<CommonParameters>]
    
    
DESCRIPTION
    Copies input document with optional renaming.
    

PARAMETERS
    -InputDocument <ProjectWiseDocument>
        
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    0
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       True (ByValue)
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -SourceDocumentNamePath <string>
        Full path to document to copy.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    1
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -TargetFolderPath <string>
        Folder name to copy to (Required).
        
        Required?                    true
        Position?                    2
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -NewDocumentName <string>
        New document name (Optional).
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    3
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -NewFileName <string>
        New document file name (Optional).
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    4
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -CreateVersions [<SwitchParameter>]
        Create version if target document name/file already exists 
        (Optional).
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    named
        Default value                False
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -DoNotCopyReferenceRelations [<SwitchParameter>]
        Will copy reference relationships if this switch is not set.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    named
        Default value                False
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    <CommonParameters>
        This cmdlet supports the common parameters: Verbose, Debug,
        ErrorAction, ErrorVariable, WarningAction, WarningVariable,
        OutBuffer, PipelineVariable, and OutVariable. For more information, 
    see 
        about_CommonParameters 
    (https:/go.microsoft.com/fwlink/?LinkID=113216). 
    
INPUTS
    PWPS_DAB.CommonTypes+ProjectWiseDocument
        
    
    
OUTPUTS
    
NOTES
    
    
        
    
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 1 --------------------------
    
    Copies the ProjectWise document passed via the pipeline to 
    'Test\TargetFolder'.
    Get-PWDocumentsBySearch -FolderPath 'TEST\SourceFolder' -JustThisFolder 
    -DocumentName 'A111-View-4.dwg' -Verbose | Copy-PWDocument  
    -TargetFolderPath 'TEST\TargetFolder'-Verbose
    
    
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 2 --------------------------
    
    Makes a backup copy of a document in the same folder.  If a backup 
    already exists, it is versioned.
    $FolderPath = 'BSI10-CE - Configuration\'
    $DocumentName = 'ConfigurationSetup.cfg'
    Copy-PWDocument -SourceDocumentNamePath $FolderPath$DocumentName 
    -TargetFolderPath $FolderPath -NewDocumentName $DocumentName'.backup' 
    -CreateVersions -Verbose

## Copy-PWDocumentsBetweenDatasources


NAME
    Copy-PWDocumentsBetweenDatasources
    
SYNOPSIS
    Copies documents to a target folder in another datasource.
    
    
SYNTAX
    Copy-PWDocumentsBetweenDatasources [-InputDocuments] 
    <ProjectWiseDocument[]> [-TargetDatasource] <string> [-TargetFolderPath] 
    <string> [[-ActiveDocumentsOnly]] [[-Overwrite]] [<CommonParameters>]
    
    
DESCRIPTION
    Takes an array of document objects from the pipeline and copies them to 
    a target folder in another datasource.
    

PARAMETERS
    -InputDocuments <ProjectWiseDocument[]>
        Array of document objects from pipeline (Required).
        
        Required?                    true
        Position?                    0
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       True (ByValue)
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -TargetDatasource <string>
        Target datasource name (Required).
        
        Required?                    true
        Position?                    1
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -TargetFolderPath <string>
        Path to target folder (Required).
        
        Required?                    true
        Position?                    2
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -ActiveDocumentsOnly [<SwitchParameter>]
        Active documents only. Filters version documents from input list.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    3
        Default value                False
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -Overwrite [<SwitchParameter>]
        If a document to be copied already exists in the target folder, the 
        document and any versions it has will be deleted before the copy 
        takes place.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    4
        Default value                False
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    <CommonParameters>
        This cmdlet supports the common parameters: Verbose, Debug,
        ErrorAction, ErrorVariable, WarningAction, WarningVariable,
        OutBuffer, PipelineVariable, and OutVariable. For more information, 
    see 
        about_CommonParameters 
    (https:/go.microsoft.com/fwlink/?LinkID=113216). 
    
INPUTS
    PWPS_DAB.CommonTypes+ProjectWiseDocument[]
        Array of document objects from pipeline (Required).
    
    
OUTPUTS
    
NOTES
    
    
        
    
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 1 --------------------------
    
    #Get documents and copy them to a target folder in another datasource
    $encryptedPassword = Read-Host -Prompt "Enter Password for source 
    datasource:" -AsSecureString
    New-PWLogin ServerName:SourceDatasourceName -UserName 
    sourceDatasourceUser -Password $encryptedPassword
    $docs = Show-PWDocumentSelectionDialog
    $encryptedPassword = Read-Host -Prompt "Enter Password for target 
    datasource:" -AsSecureString
    New-PWLogin ServerName:TargetDatasourceName -UserName 
    targetDatasourceUser -Password $encryptedPassword
    $folder = Show-PWFolderBrowserDialog
    Set-PWSession ServerName:SourceDatasourceName
    Copy-PWDocumentsBetweenDatasources $docs -TargetDatasource 
    ServerName:TargetDatasourceName -TargetFolderPath $folder.FullPath
    
    
    
RELATED LINKS

## Copy-PWDocumentsByProject


NAME
    Copy-PWDocumentsByProject
    
SYNOPSIS
    Copy the contents of a folder to another folder.

    [-TargetFolderPath] <string> [[-DoNotReplicatePaths]] [[-NewStorageArea]

    new sub-folders to contain the documents.
    

PARAMETERS

        Folder path to copy from (Required).
        
        Required?                    true
        Position?                    0
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -TargetFolderPath <string>
        Folder path to copy to (Required) (Must exist).
        
        Required?                    true
        Position?                    1
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -DoNotReplicatePaths [<SwitchParameter>]

        tree to the target folder without creating subfolders.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    2
        Default value                False
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -NewStorageArea <string>
        If set, will create new folders in this storage area.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    3
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -CreateVersions [<SwitchParameter>]
        If set, will create new versions of existing documents. Default is 
        to overwrite existing.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    4
        Default value                False
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    <CommonParameters>
        This cmdlet supports the common parameters: Verbose, Debug,
        ErrorAction, ErrorVariable, WarningAction, WarningVariable,
        OutBuffer, PipelineVariable, and OutVariable. For more information, 
    see 
        about_CommonParameters 
    (https:/go.microsoft.com/fwlink/?LinkID=113216).

OUTPUTS

## Copy-PWDocumentsToFolder

    Copy-PWDocumentsToFolder

        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false

        Required?                    false
        Position?                    2

        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false

        Set this to include document versions with the copy process. If not

        Required?                    false
        Position?                    4
        Default value                False
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false

        ErrorAction, ErrorVariable, WarningAction, WarningVariable,
        OutBuffer, PipelineVariable, and OutVariable. For more information,

        about_CommonParameters

    Create versions if documents exist.

    -TargetFolderPath 'New\Folder\Path' -IncludeVersions -Verbose
    
    Get the documents in the given path and copy them to a target folder, 
    including any document versions.

## Copy-PWFolder


NAME
    Copy-PWFolder
    
SYNOPSIS
    Copy-PWFolder copies a folder to the root directory or a specified 
    target folder.
    
    
SYNTAX
    Copy-PWFolder [-FolderPath] <string> [-FolderToCopy] <string> 
    [-IncludeSubFolders] [-DontCopyWorkflow] [-IncludeDocuments] 
    [-IncludeAccessControl] [-IncludeWorkspaceProfile] [-TemplatePrefix 
    <string>] [-TargetPrefix <string>] 
    [-SkipFolderWorkflowSecurityReplication] [<CommonParameters>]
    
    Copy-PWFolder [-CreateAtRoot] [-FolderToCopy] <string> 
    [-IncludeSubFolders] [-DontCopyWorkflow] [-IncludeDocuments] 
    [-IncludeAccessControl] [-IncludeWorkspaceProfile] [-TemplatePrefix 
    <string>] [-TargetPrefix <string>] 
    [-SkipFolderWorkflowSecurityReplication] [<CommonParameters>]
    
    
DESCRIPTION
    Copy an existing folder into the root directory or another folder. Can 
    include subfolders and/or documents, as well as document attributes, 
    access control, workflow and workspace settings.
    

PARAMETERS
    -FolderPath <string>
        Full folder path of parent target folder. Required. Folder must 
        exist.
        
        Required?                    true
        Position?                    0
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -CreateAtRoot [<SwitchParameter>]
        When included, the folder will be copied into the root directory.
        
        Required?                    true
        Position?                    1
        Default value                False
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -FolderToCopy <string>
        Path Name of folder to be copied. Required. Folder must exist.
        
        Required?                    true
        Position?                    2
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -IncludeSubFolders [<SwitchParameter>]
        If included, all sub-folders will be copied.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    named
        Default value                False
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -DontCopyWorkflow [<SwitchParameter>]
        If included, the workflow setting will not be copied. By default, it 
        will.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    named
        Default value                False
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -IncludeDocuments [<SwitchParameter>]
        If included, all documents will be copied.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    named
        Default value                False
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -IncludeAccessControl [<SwitchParameter>]
        If included, all access control will be copied.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    named
        Default value                False
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -IncludeWorkspaceProfile [<SwitchParameter>]
        If included, the workspace profile will be copied.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    named
        Default value                False
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -TemplatePrefix <string>
        Defaults to '[TEMPLATE]'. If TargetPrefix is set, will process user 
        lists with that prefix into ones with the tempalte prefix 
        ([TEMPLATE]_ProjectManagers => TargetPrefix_ProjectManagers)
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    named
        Default value                [TEMPLATE]
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -TargetPrefix <string>
        If TargetPrefix is set, will process user lists with that prefix 
        into ones with the template prefix ([TEMPLATE]_ProjectManagers => 
        TargetPrefix_ProjectManagers)
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    named
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -SkipFolderWorkflowSecurityReplication [<SwitchParameter>]
        Skip the Folder + Workflow security replication which can be quite 
        time consuming. Workflow security whould probably be set globally 
        anyway...
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    named
        Default value                False
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    <CommonParameters>
        This cmdlet supports the common parameters: Verbose, Debug,
        ErrorAction, ErrorVariable, WarningAction, WarningVariable,
        OutBuffer, PipelineVariable, and OutVariable. For more information, 
    see 
        about_CommonParameters 
    (https:/go.microsoft.com/fwlink/?LinkID=113216). 
    
INPUTS
    
OUTPUTS
    
NOTES
    
    
        
    
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 1 --------------------------
    
    Example usage for Copying a folder.
    This copies the dwgs folder into the projects folder. No documents or 
    sub-folders are included.
    Copy-PWFolder -FolderPath "Projects" -FolderToCopy 'DWGS'
    
    
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 2 --------------------------
    
    This copies the DWGS folder into the Project01 folder. Sub-folders and 
    documents will be copied.
    Copy-PWFolder -FolderPath "Project Folder\Project 01" -FolderToCopy 
    'Working Folder\DWGS' -IncludeSubFolders -IncludeDocuments
    
    
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 3 --------------------------
    
    This copies the DWGS folder into the Project01 folder. Sub-folders and 
    documents will be copied. Document attribute values will also be copied.
    Copy-PWFolder -FolderPath "Project Folder\Project 01" -FolderToCopy 
    'Working Folder\DWGS' -IncludeSubFolders -IncludeDocuments 
    -IncludeDocumentAttributes
    
    
    
RELATED LINKS

## Copy-PWFoldersBetweenDatasources


NAME
    Copy-PWFoldersBetweenDatasources
    
SYNOPSIS
    Copies a folder tree from one datasource to another datasource. 
    Optionally copy documents, too.
    
    
SYNTAX
    Copy-PWFoldersBetweenDatasources [-InputFolders] <ProjectWiseFolder[]> 
    [-TargetDatasource] <string> [[-TargetParentPath] <string>] 
    [-TargetStorage] <string> [[-IgnoreMissingEnvironments]] 
    [[-IgnoreMissingWorkflows]] [[-IgnoreMissingProjectTypes]] 
    [[-IgnoreMissingViews]] [[-IncludeDocuments]] [[-ActiveDocumentsOnly]] 
    [<CommonParameters>]
    
    
DESCRIPTION
    Takes a folder object and copies the tree structue to a target parent 
    folder in another datasource. Optionally, copies documents.
    

PARAMETERS
    -InputFolders <ProjectWiseFolder[]>
        Source folder object (Required).
        
        Required?                    true
        Position?                    0
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       True (ByValue)
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -TargetDatasource <string>
        Target datasource name (Required).
        
        Required?                    true
        Position?                    1
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -TargetParentPath <string>
        Path to target parent folder.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    2
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -TargetStorage <string>
        Target storage area (Required).
        
        Required?                    true
        Position?                    3
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -IgnoreMissingEnvironments [<SwitchParameter>]
        Ignore missing environments in target. Otherwise, copy will not 
        proceed unless environment is defined.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    4
        Default value                False
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -IgnoreMissingWorkflows [<SwitchParameter>]
        Ignore missing workflows in target. Otherwise, copy will not proceed 
        unless workflow is defined.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    5
        Default value                False
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -IgnoreMissingProjectTypes [<SwitchParameter>]
        Ignore missing project types in target. Otherwise, copy will not 
        proceed unless project type is defined.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    6
        Default value                False
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -IgnoreMissingViews [<SwitchParameter>]
        Ignore missing views in target. Otherwise, copy will not proceed 
        unless views are defined.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    6
        Default value                False
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -IncludeDocuments [<SwitchParameter>]
        Include documents. Will copy ownership, create, update and file 
        update dates, state and workflow (if applicable). Preserves 
        reference relationships if master and reference included in same 
        folder tree.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    7
        Default value                False
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -ActiveDocumentsOnly [<SwitchParameter>]
        Active documents only. Ignore versions.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    8
        Default value                False
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    <CommonParameters>
        This cmdlet supports the common parameters: Verbose, Debug,
        ErrorAction, ErrorVariable, WarningAction, WarningVariable,
        OutBuffer, PipelineVariable, and OutVariable. For more information, 
    see 
        about_CommonParameters 
    (https:/go.microsoft.com/fwlink/?LinkID=113216). 
    
INPUTS
    PWPS_DAB.CommonTypes+ProjectWiseFolder[]
        Source folder object (Required).
    
    
OUTPUTS
    
NOTES
    
    
        
    
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 1 --------------------------
    
    #Note in the examples, it probably makes the most sense if 
    SourceTopeLevelName and TargetTopLevelName can be identical and 
    TargetTopLevelName does not exist.
    New-PWLogin SourceServer:SourceDatasoureName -UserName pwadmin -Password 
    (ConvertTo-SecureString pwadmin -AsPlainText -Force)
    New-PWLogin TargetServer:TargetDatasoureName -UserName pwadmin -Password 
    (ConvertTo-SecureString pwadmin -AsPlainText -Force)
    Set-PWSession  SourceServer:SourceDatasoureName 
    $folder = Get-PWFolders -JustOne -FolderPath 
    "Level1\Level2\SourceTopLevelName" -verbose
    Copy-PWFoldersBetweenDatasources $folder -TargetDatasource 
    TargetServer:TargetDatasourceName -TargetParentPath 
    'TargetTopLevelFolderLevel1\TargetTopLevelName' -TargetStorage Storage01
    
    
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 2 --------------------------
    
    New-PWLogin SourceServer:SourceDatasoureName -UserName pwadmin -Password 
    (ConvertTo-SecureString pwadmin -AsPlainText -Force)
    New-PWLogin TargetServer:TargetDatasoureName -UserName pwadmin -Password 
    (ConvertTo-SecureString pwadmin -AsPlainText -Force)
    Set-PWSession  SourceServer:SourceDatasoureName 
    Get-PWFolderByURN URNFroSourceDatasource | 
    Copy-PWFoldersBetweenDatasources -TargetDatasource 
    TargetServer:TargetDSN -TargetParentPath 
    'TargetTopLevelFolderLevel1\TargetTopLevelName' -TargetStorage Storage 
    -IgnoreMissingEnvironments -IgnoreMissingWorkflows -IgnoreMissingViews 
    -IgnoreMissingProjectTypes -IncludeDocuments
    
    
    
RELATED LINKS

## Export-PWAAMFile


NAME
    Export-PWAAMFile
    
SYNOPSIS
    Exports environments, applications, searches and workflows to an AAM 
    file.
    
    
SYNTAX
    Export-PWAAMFile [-OutputFile] <string> [[-Environments]] [[-Workflows]] 
    [[-Applications]] [[-Searches]] [[-LogFile] <string>] 
    [<CommonParameters>]
    
    
DESCRIPTION
    Exports environments, workflows, applications, or saved searches from 
    the current datasource to an AAM file. Will export environments if none 
    are selected.
    
    Provide a destination file and, if needed, a log file. The command 
    returns no output.
    
    Run it in 32-bit PowerShell with the ProjectWise Export Wizard available.
    

PARAMETERS
    -OutputFile <string>
        Full path to output AAM file. (Required).
        
        Required?                    true
        Position?                    0
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -Environments [<SwitchParameter>]
        Set to export environments.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    1
        Default value                False
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -Workflows [<SwitchParameter>]
        Set to export workflows.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    2
        Default value                False
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -Applications [<SwitchParameter>]
        Set to export applications.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    3
        Default value                False
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -Searches [<SwitchParameter>]
        Set to export searches.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    4
        Default value                False
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -LogFile <string>
        Full path to the export operation log file.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    5
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    <CommonParameters>
        This cmdlet supports the common parameters: Verbose, Debug,
        ErrorAction, ErrorVariable, WarningAction, WarningVariable,
        OutBuffer, PipelineVariable, and OutVariable. For more information, 
    see 
        about_CommonParameters 
    (https:/go.microsoft.com/fwlink/?LinkID=113216). 
    
INPUTS
    
OUTPUTS
    
NOTES
    
    
        
    
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 1 --------------------------
    
    Export-PWAAMFile "c:\temp\environments.aam"
    
    
    
RELATED LINKS

## Export-PWDocuments


NAME
    Export-PWDocuments
    
SYNOPSIS
    Despite the name, copies out files to a folder and optionally writes 
    metadata to a spreadsheet.
    
    
SYNTAX
    Export-PWDocuments [[-InputDocuments] <ProjectWiseDocument[]>] 
    [-OutputFolder] <string> [[-Personal]] [[-UseDMSFolders]] 
    [[-UseUnderscores]] [[-AltMetadataExportMethod]] [[-ProjectWiseFolder] 
    <string>] [[-Searches] <string>] [[-ExportMetadata]] 
    [[-ExportMetadataFile] <string>] [[-ExportVersions]] [[-JustOneFolder]] 
    [[-BPRCFile] <string>] [[-ServerIsUTC]] [<CommonParameters>]
    
    
DESCRIPTION
    Export metadata files is in same format as Save-PWDocumentReport. NOTE: 
    Version support assumes Document Name consistency in the version stack. 
    The export will probably work, but the import from the spreadsheet 
    assumes that document names are constant through the stack.
    
    Folder structure is preserved (even long paths so make sure you have a 
    Windows File Explorer replacement that can navigate long paths).
    
    This will not overwrite a file on the disk unless the file in 
    ProjectWise is newer. Intent is to maintain an up-to-date mirror of the 
    files from ProjectWise on-disk. So, this cmdlet would be good for use in 
    a scheduled task. Some users use this as a fetch-files replacement.
    
    The last file update date from ProjectWise and the ProjectWise document 
    create date are preserved in the copied out file.
    

PARAMETERS
    -InputDocuments <ProjectWiseDocument[]>
        Input array of ProjectWiseDocument objects from the pipeline 
        (Required). Use Get-PWDocumentFromParameters or another cmdlet that 
        returns document objects to feed pipeline.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    0
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       True (ByValue)
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -OutputFolder <string>
        Folder on disk for output.
        
        Required?                    true
        Position?                    1
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -ProjectWiseFolder <string>
        Optional ProjectWise folder path.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    2
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -Searches <string>
        Optional semi-colon delimited list of saved searches to process.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    3
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -ExportMetadata [<SwitchParameter>]
        Export metadata if set.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    4
        Default value                False
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -ExportMetadataFile <string>
        Metadata file name to use. Will use default name if not set.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    5
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -ExportVersions [<SwitchParameter>]
        If set, exports versions of files. Appends .verXXXX suffix before 
        extension. Should always be used with ExportMetadata
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    6
        Default value                False
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -JustOneFolder [<SwitchParameter>]
        If set, exports only the files in the given folder rather than 
        including subfolders.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    7
        Default value                False
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -BPRCFile <string>
        If included, will build MicroStation batch processing file.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    8
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -ServerIsUTC [<SwitchParameter>]
        Set if server is UTC. Will do file time comparison in UTC.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    9
        Default value                False
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -Personal [<SwitchParameter>]
        Set to find personal search.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    10
        Default value                False
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -UseDMSFolders [<SwitchParameter>]
        Use DMS Folders and not full paths.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    11
        Default value                False
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -UseUnderscores [<SwitchParameter>]
        Replaces illegal path characters with underscores. Otherwise, 
        replaces illegal character with name (e.g. {colon}, {pipe})
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    12
        Default value                False
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -AltMetadataExportMethod [<SwitchParameter>]
        Uses alternate method to export metadata. Slower and more accurate 
        in some cases.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    13
        Default value                False
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    <CommonParameters>
        This cmdlet supports the common parameters: Verbose, Debug,
        ErrorAction, ErrorVariable, WarningAction, WarningVariable,
        OutBuffer, PipelineVariable, and OutVariable. For more information, 
    see 
        about_CommonParameters 
    (https:/go.microsoft.com/fwlink/?LinkID=113216). 
    
INPUTS
    PWPS_DAB.CommonTypes+ProjectWiseDocument[]
        Input array of ProjectWiseDocument objects from the pipeline 
        (Required). Use Get-PWDocumentFromParameters or another cmdlet that 
        returns document objects to feed pipeline.
    
    
OUTPUTS
    
NOTES
    
    
        
    
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 1 --------------------------
    
    #Will export documents returned by the listed saved searches.
    Export-PWDocuments -OutputFolder c:\export -Searches "60% Complete;80% 
    Complete;90% Complete" -ExportMetadata -ExportMetadataFile 
    "c:\temp\output.xlsx"
    
    
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 2 --------------------------
    
    #Will export documents in given folder and write metadata file with 
    default filename and set exported (copied out) files to final status.
    Export-PWDocuments -OutputFolder c:\export -ProjectWiseFolder "Source 
    A\Subfolder 1" -ExportMetadata -ExportMetadataFile 
    "c:\temp\output2.xlsx" | Set-PWDocumentFinalStatus
    
    
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 3 --------------------------
    
    #Gets a document, exports it with metadat and sets final status.
    Get-PWDocumentFromParameters -FolderPath "Test 6\Projects 2" 
    -DocumentName 'Final Site Plan' | Export-PWDocuments -OutputFolder 
    c:\export -ExportMetadata | Set-PWDocumentFinalStatus
    
    
    
RELATED LINKS

## Export-PWDocumentsSimple


NAME
    Export-PWDocumentsSimple
    
SYNOPSIS
    Exports ProjectWise documents to a local folder.
    
    
SYNTAX
    Export-PWDocumentsSimple [-InputDocuments] <ProjectWiseDocument[]> 
    [-TargetFolder] <string> [[-ExportManagedDocs]] [<CommonParameters>]
    
    
DESCRIPTION
    Exports ProjectWise documents to the local folder you specify.
    
    By default, the command creates unmanaged copies and includes related 
    files. Use `ExportManagedDocs` for managed exports.
    
    Prepare the target folder before running the command.
    

PARAMETERS
    -InputDocuments <ProjectWiseDocument[]>
        RquiredInput array of ProjectWiseDocument objects. May by passed as 
        pipeline or parameter. Use Get-PWDocumentBySearch or New-PWDocument 
        to feed pipeline.
        
        Required?                    true
        Position?                    0
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       True (ByValue)
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -TargetFolder <string>
        Target folder for export.
        
        Required?                    true
        Position?                    1
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -ExportManagedDocs [<SwitchParameter>]
        Use to export managed copies of documents.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    2
        Default value                False
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    <CommonParameters>
        This cmdlet supports the common parameters: Verbose, Debug,
        ErrorAction, ErrorVariable, WarningAction, WarningVariable,
        OutBuffer, PipelineVariable, and OutVariable. For more information, 
    see 
        about_CommonParameters 
    (https:/go.microsoft.com/fwlink/?LinkID=113216). 
    
INPUTS
    PWPS_DAB.CommonTypes+ProjectWiseDocument[]
        RquiredInput array of ProjectWiseDocument objects. May by passed as 
        pipeline or parameter. Use Get-PWDocumentBySearch or New-PWDocument 
        to feed pipeline.
    
    
OUTPUTS
    PWPS_DAB.CommonTypes+ProjectWiseDocument
        
    
    
NOTES
    
    
        
    
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 1 --------------------------
    
    New-PWLogin -DatasourceName Server:DSN
    #Gets the documents in this folder and subfolders where the file name 
    pattern matches %.dwg
    $docs = Get-PWDocumentsBySearch -FolderPath 'BSI900 - Adelaide Tower\' 
    -FileName '%.dwg'
    #Exports all the found DWGs 
    Export-PWDocumentsSimple $docs c:\ExportedDocs
    
    
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 2 --------------------------
    
    New-PWLogin -DatasourceName Server:DSN
    #Gets the documents in this folder and subfolders where the file name 
    pattern matches %.dwg
    $docs = Get-PWDocumentsBySearch -FolderPath 'BSI900 - Adelaide Tower\' 
    -FileName '%.dwg'
    #Exports all the found DWGs 
    $docs | Export-PWDocumentsSimple -ExportManagedDocs -TargetFolder c:\temp
    
    
    
RELATED LINKS

## Export-PWEnvironments


NAME
    Export-PWEnvironments
    
SYNOPSIS
    Export environment attribute definitions to a spreadsheet suitable for 
    use with Import-PWEnvironments.
    
    
SYNTAX
    Export-PWEnvironments [[-OutputFileName] <string>] [[-Environments] 
    <string[]>] [<CommonParameters>]
    
    
DESCRIPTION
    This is intended to be a smarter and easier to edit format than the AAM 
    file currently used for environment definitions.
    
    Column types supported are Text, Int, Numeric and DateTime. All column 
    types will lumped into one of those categories.
    
    Document Code Definitions and Interfaces are also exported.
    

PARAMETERS
    -OutputFileName <string>
        Output file name. Optional. If not provided will output an array of 
        DataTables.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    1
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -Environments <string[]>
        Provide environment names to limit output. Otherwise, exports 
        definitions from all environments.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    2
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    <CommonParameters>
        This cmdlet supports the common parameters: Verbose, Debug,
        ErrorAction, ErrorVariable, WarningAction, WarningVariable,
        OutBuffer, PipelineVariable, and OutVariable. For more information, 
    see 
        about_CommonParameters 
    (https:/go.microsoft.com/fwlink/?LinkID=113216). 
    
INPUTS
    
OUTPUTS
    
NOTES
    
    
        
    
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 1 --------------------------
    
    Export-PWEnvironments c:\report\environments.xlsx -Environments 
    Simple,Complex
    Export-PWEnvironments c:\report\environments.xlsx
    
    
    
RELATED LINKS

## Get-CloudProject


NAME
    Get-CloudProject
    
SYNOPSIS
    Gets Bentley Infrastructure Cloud project from a cloud project id or 
    work area.
    
    
SYNTAX
    Get-CloudProject [[-InputFolder] <ProjectWiseFolder[]>] [-OIDCToken] 
    <OIDCAuthToken> [<CommonParameters>]
    
    Get-CloudProject [[-CloudProjectId] <string>] [-OIDCToken] 
    <OIDCAuthToken> [<CommonParameters>]
    
    
DESCRIPTION
    Takes a cloud project id or an array of ProjectWiseFolders and returns 
    Bentley Infrastructure Cloud project objects.
    

PARAMETERS
    -InputFolder <ProjectWiseFolder[]>
        Array of ProjectWise folders from pipeline or parameter value. 
        Required if no CloudProject using CloudProjectId parameter.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    0
        Default value                PWPS_DAB.CommonTypes+ProjectWiseFolder[]
        Accept pipeline input?       True (ByValue)
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -CloudProjectId <string>
        Bentley Infrastructure Cloud Project ID. Required if not using 
        InputFolder Parameter
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    0
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       True (ByValue)
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -OIDCToken <OIDCAuthToken>
        Token from Get-OIDCToken -Authority https://ims.bentley.com
        
        Required?                    true
        Position?                    1
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    <CommonParameters>
        This cmdlet supports the common parameters: Verbose, Debug,
        ErrorAction, ErrorVariable, WarningAction, WarningVariable,
        OutBuffer, PipelineVariable, and OutVariable. For more information, 
    see 
        about_CommonParameters 
    (https:/go.microsoft.com/fwlink/?LinkID=113216). 
    
INPUTS
    PWPS_DAB.CommonTypes+ProjectWiseFolder[]
        Array of ProjectWise folders from pipeline or parameter value. 
        Required if no CloudProject using CloudProjectId parameter.
    
    System.String
        Bentley Infrastructure Cloud Project ID. Required if not using 
        InputFolder Parameter
    
    
OUTPUTS
    System.Collections.Generic.List`1[[PWPS_DAB.CloudProject, PWPS, 
    Version=24.0.2.0, Culture=neutral, PublicKeyToken=null]]
        
    
    
NOTES
    
    
        
    
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 1 --------------------------
    
    #Example usage for getting a Bentley Infrastructure Cloud Project from a 
    Work Area:
    #Log into a datasource
    New-PWLogin
    #Get a work area
    $rp = Get-PWRichProjects -FolderPath "PWPS Connected Project Test 
    6\Projects 2"
    #Get an OIDC token
    $oidcToken = Get-OIDCToken -Authority https://ims.bentley.com
    #Get the Bentley Infrastructure Cloud project associated with the work 
    area
    Get-CloudProject $rp -OIDCToken $oidcToken

## Get-CloudProjectId


NAME
    Get-CloudProjectId
    
SYNOPSIS
    Gets Cloud project ID from a work area.
    
    
SYNTAX
    Get-CloudProjectId [-InputFolder] <ProjectWiseFolder[]> 
    [<CommonParameters>]
    
    
DESCRIPTION
    Takes an array of ProjectWiseFolders and returns a cloud project id.
    

PARAMETERS
    -InputFolder <ProjectWiseFolder[]>
        Array of ProjectWise folders from pipeline.
        
        Required?                    true
        Position?                    0
        Default value                
        PWPS_Cloud.CommonTypes+ProjectWiseFolder[]
        Accept pipeline input?       True (ByValue)
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    <CommonParameters>
        This cmdlet supports the common parameters: Verbose, Debug,
        ErrorAction, ErrorVariable, WarningAction, WarningVariable,
        OutBuffer, PipelineVariable, and OutVariable. For more information, 
    see 
        about_CommonParameters 
    (https:/go.microsoft.com/fwlink/?LinkID=113216). 
    
INPUTS
    PWPS_Cloud.CommonTypes+ProjectWiseFolder[]
        Array of ProjectWise folders from pipeline.
    
    
OUTPUTS
    
NOTES
    
    
        
    
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 1 --------------------------
    
    #Example usage for getting the cloud project id for each work area in a 
    tree.
    New-PWLogin Server:DSN -UseGui
    #Get all the work areas in this tree and pipe to this cmdlet
    $WorkAreas = Get-PWWorkAreas -FolderPath "PWPS cloud Project Test 
    6\Projects 2"
    #for each work area, get the cloud project ID
    foreach ($WorkArea in $WorkAreas) 
    {
    	write-host $WorkArea
    	Get-CloudProjectId $WorkArea 
    }

## Get-CloudProjectWorkAreaConnections


NAME
    Get-CloudProjectWorkAreaConnections
    
SYNOPSIS
    Retrieves Work Area Connections for a cloud project.
    
    
SYNTAX
    Get-CloudProjectWorkAreaConnections [[-InputFolder] 
    <ProjectWiseFolder[]>] [[-CloudProjectId] <string>] [-OIDCToken] 
    <OIDCAuthToken> [[-APIVersion] <string>] [<CommonParameters>]
    
    
DESCRIPTION
    Retrieves Work Area Connections for a Bentley Infrastructure Cloud 
    project. Identify the project with CloudProjectId or a connected 
    ProjectWise work area.
    
    Supply an OIDC token for access. The cmdlet returns the connections 
    available for the selected project.
    
    You do not need an active ProjectWise session when providing a 
    CloudProjectId.
    

PARAMETERS
    -InputFolder <ProjectWiseFolder[]>
        Array of ProjectWise folders from pipeline. Work Area Connections 
        from the corresponding Bentley Infrastructure Cloud Projects will be 
        shown.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    0
        Default value                PWPS_DAB.CommonTypes+ProjectWiseFolder[]
        Accept pipeline input?       True (ByValue)
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -CloudProjectId <string>
        Bentley Infrastructure Cloud Project ID to query
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    1
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -OIDCToken <OIDCAuthToken>
        Token from Get-OIDCToken -Authority https://ims.bentley.com
        
        Required?                    true
        Position?                    2
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -APIVersion <string>
        
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    3
        Default value                V1
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    <CommonParameters>
        This cmdlet supports the common parameters: Verbose, Debug,
        ErrorAction, ErrorVariable, WarningAction, WarningVariable,
        OutBuffer, PipelineVariable, and OutVariable. For more information, 
    see 
        about_CommonParameters 
    (https:/go.microsoft.com/fwlink/?LinkID=113216). 
    
INPUTS
    PWPS_DAB.CommonTypes+ProjectWiseFolder[]
        Array of ProjectWise folders from pipeline. Work Area Connections 
        from the corresponding Bentley Infrastructure Cloud Projects will be 
        shown.
    
    
OUTPUTS
    
NOTES
    
    
        
    
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 1 --------------------------
    
    $oidcToken = Get-OIDCToken -Authority https://ims.bentley.com
    $wacs = Get-CloudProjectWorkAreaConnections -CloudProjectId 
    CloudProjectIdGUID -OIDCToken $oidcToken
    
    
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 2 --------------------------
    
    $oidcToken = Get-OIDCToken -Authority https://ims.bentley.com
    New-PWLogin
    $folder = Get-PWFolderByURN 
    URNofWorkAreaConnectedToBentleyInrastructureCloudProject
    $wacs = Get-CloudProjectWorkAreaConnections $folder -OIDCToken $oidcToken

## Get-OIDCToken


NAME
    Get-OIDCToken
    
SYNOPSIS
    Gets an OIDC token for Bentley services.
    
    
SYNTAX
    Get-OIDCToken [[-Scopes] <string>] [[-ClientID] <string>] 
    [[-ForceError]] [[-RedirectPort] <int>] [[-Authority] <string>] 
    [[-UseWebPage]] [[-UseCacheIfAvailable]] [[-NewToken]] 
    [[-SuppressLoginPrompt]] [[-UnattendedMode]] [[-PreserveRegistryValues]] 
    [<CommonParameters>]
    
    
DESCRIPTION
    Gets an OIDC token for the authority, client, and scopes you specify. An 
    active ProjectWise session is not required.
    
    Use the browser sign-in option when interactive sign-in is appropriate. 
    Use UnattendedMode only when a non-interactive sign-in is available.
    

PARAMETERS
    -Scopes <string>
        Access Scopes. Defaults to "global_roles email openid profile 
        organization feature_tracking connections:modify connections:read 
        context-registry-service imodel-bridge-service-api-admin 
        imodel-bridge-service-api-general imodelhub imodelhub:webview 
        imodels:modify imodels:read insights:read library:read 
        product-settings-service projects:modify projects:read 
        projectwise-deliverables-management-service 
        projectwise-drive-settings-api projectwise-share 
        projectwise-web-connections-service projectwise-web-view-service 
        projectwise365:modify projectwise365:read pwconnections-read 
        pwdi-read pwprojects-read rbac-user:external-client realitydata:read 
        storage:modify storage:read synchronization:modify 
        synchronization:read users:read offline_access"
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    0
        Default value                Itwins:read itwins:modify global_roles 
        email openid profile organization feature_tracking 
        connections:modify connections:read context-registry-service 
        enterprise imodel-bridge-service-api-admin 
        imodel-bridge-service-api-general imodelhub imodelhub:webview 
        imodels:modify imodels:read insights:read library:read 
        product-settings-service projects:modify projects:read 
        projectwise-deliverables-management-service 
        projectwise-drive-settings-api projectwise-share 
        projectwise-web-connections-service projectwise-web-view-service 
        projectwise365:modify projectwise365:read pwconnections-read 
        pwdi-read pwprojects-read rbac-user:external-client realitydata:read 
        storage:modify storage:read synchronization:modify 
        synchronization:read users:read offline_access 
        projectwise-user-sync-api
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -ClientID <string>
        ClientID. Defaults to "powershelltokengen"
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    1
        Default value                Powershelltokengen
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -RedirectPort <int>
        RedirectPort. Defaults to 4998. Needs to match the Redirect URL as 
        defined in the app definition.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    2
        Default value                4998
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -Authority <string>
        Authority. Defaults to "https://ims.bentley.com"
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    3
        Default value                Https://ims.bentley.com
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -UseWebPage [<SwitchParameter>]
        Use web page instead of built-in dialog.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    4
        Default value                True
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -UseCacheIfAvailable [<SwitchParameter>]
        Use cached token if available.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    5
        Default value                False
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -NewToken [<SwitchParameter>]
        Bypass values cached in registry and force new token generation.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    6
        Default value                False
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -SuppressLoginPrompt [<SwitchParameter>]
        Changes the login address.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    7
        Default value                False
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -UnattendedMode [<SwitchParameter>]
        Suppresses interactive form and throws exception on failure.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    8
        Default value                False
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -PreserveRegistryValues [<SwitchParameter>]
        Don't overwrite registry entries when refresh fails.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    9
        Default value                False
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -ForceError [<SwitchParameter>]
        For testing unattended scripts.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    10
        Default value                False
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    <CommonParameters>
        This cmdlet supports the common parameters: Verbose, Debug,
        ErrorAction, ErrorVariable, WarningAction, WarningVariable,
        OutBuffer, PipelineVariable, and OutVariable. For more information, 
    see

## Get-PWAsyncExtractionSchedule


NAME
    Get-PWAsyncExtractionSchedule
    
SYNOPSIS
    Gets the Schedule for an Asynchronous Extraction Component in the 
    current ProjectWise Datasource.
    
    
SYNTAX
    Get-PWAsyncExtractionSchedule -FilePropertyExtraction 
    [<CommonParameters>]
    
    Get-PWAsyncExtractionSchedule -FullTextIndexing [<CommonParameters>]
    
    Get-PWAsyncExtractionSchedule -ThumbnailExtraction [<CommonParameters>]
    
    
DESCRIPTION
    The Get-PWAsyncExtractionSchedule cmdlet gets the Schedule for an 
    Asynchronous Extraction Component in the current ProjectWise Datasource.
    
    The component to retrieve the Schedule for must be specified by one of 
    the following flags: -FilePropertyExtraction, -FullTextIndexing, or 
    -ThumbnailExtraction.
    

PARAMETERS
    -FilePropertyExtraction [<SwitchParameter>]
        If set, retrieves the Schedule for the File Property Extraction 
        Component.
        
        Required?                    true
        Position?                    named
        Default value                False
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -FullTextIndexing [<SwitchParameter>]
        If set, retrieves the Schedule for the Full Text Indexing Component.
        
        Required?                    true
        Position?                    named
        Default value                False
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -ThumbnailExtraction [<SwitchParameter>]
        If set, retrieves the Schedule for the Thumbnail Extraction 
        Component.
        
        Required?                    true
        Position?                    named
        Default value                False
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    <CommonParameters>
        This cmdlet supports the common parameters: Verbose, Debug,
        ErrorAction, ErrorVariable, WarningAction, WarningVariable,
        OutBuffer, PipelineVariable, and OutVariable. For more information, 
    see 
        about_CommonParameters 
    (https:/go.microsoft.com/fwlink/?LinkID=113216). 
    
INPUTS
    None
        You cannot pipe objects to this cmdlet.
    
    
OUTPUTS
    Bentley.ProjectWise.PowerShell.Common.ExtractSchedule
        Get-PWAsyncExtractionSchedule returns an object that represents the 
        Schedule for the given Asynchronous Extraction Component.
    
    
NOTES
    
    
        Get-PWAsyncExtractionSchedule requires an open ProjectWise 
        Connection in the current thread.  You can open a Connection with 
        Open-PWConnection before calling Get-PWAsyncExtractionSchedule.
        
        Get-PWAsyncExtractionSchedule requires an Admin session.  To open an 
        admin session, use the -Admin flag when opening the ProjectWise 
        Connection with Open-PWConnection.
