## Get-PWAuditTrailRecordsFromPreviousDays


NAME
    Get-PWAuditTrailRecordsFromPreviousDays
    
SYNOPSIS
    Returns audit trail records from previous days.
    
    
SYNTAX
    Get-PWAuditTrailRecordsFromPreviousDays -DaysAgo <Int32> 
    [-IncludeIntermediateDays] [-AddDatasourceInformation] 
    [<CommonParameters>]
    
    
DESCRIPTION
    Uses audit trail criteria to return records from previous days.
    
    - `-DaysAgo 1` returns records from yesterday.
    
    - By default, only the target day window is queried.
    
    - If `-IncludeIntermediateDays` is set, records from the target day up 
    to now are queried.
    
    - If `-AddDatasourceInformation` is set, datasource columns are added to 
    the output table.
    

PARAMETERS
    -DaysAgo <Int32>
        Integer value to specify the number of days ago to return records 
        for. A value of `1` is equal to yesterday. Must be `0` or greater.
        
        Required?                    true
        Position?                    named
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -IncludeIntermediateDays [<SwitchParameter>]
        Switch to include records from intermediate results. If `DaysAgo` is 
        `5` and this switch is activated, records from 5 days ago up to now 
        are returned.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    named
        Default value                False
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -AddDatasourceInformation [<SwitchParameter>]
        Switch to add datasource information (`DatasourceString`, 
        `DatasourceName`, `ServerName`) to the returned `DataTable`.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    named
        Default value                False
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    <CommonParameters>
        This cmdlet supports the common parameters: Verbose, Debug,
        ErrorAction, ErrorVariable, WarningAction, WarningVariable,
        OutBuffer, PipelineVariable, and OutVariable. For more information, 
    see 
        about_CommonParameters 
    (https:/go.microsoft.com/fwlink/?LinkID=113216). 
    
INPUTS
    None
        
    
    
OUTPUTS
    System.Data.DataTable
        Audit trail records. If no records are found, a warning is written.
    
    
NOTES
    
    
        
    
    -------------------------- Example 1 --------------------------
    
    PS C:\> Get-PWAuditTrailRecordsFromPreviousDays -DaysAgo 1
    
    Returns audit trail records from yesterday.
    -------------------------- Example 2 --------------------------
    
    PS C:\> Get-PWAuditTrailRecordsFromPreviousDays -DaysAgo 5 
    -IncludeIntermediateDays
    
    Returns audit trail records from 5 days ago through now.
    -------------------------- Example 3 --------------------------
    
    PS C:\> Get-PWAuditTrailRecordsFromPreviousDays -DaysAgo 2 
    -AddDatasourceInformation
    
    Returns audit trail records and appends datasource metadata columns.
    
RELATED LINKS





--

## Get-PWAuditTrailSettings


NAME
    Get-PWAuditTrailSettings
    
SYNOPSIS
    Get-PWAuditTrailSettings retrieves the audit trail settings for the 
    current ProjectWise datasource.
    
    
SYNTAX
    Get-PWAuditTrailSettings [[-AuditTrailType] <string>] 
    [<CommonParameters>]
    
    
DESCRIPTION
    The `Get-PWAuditTrailSettings` cmdlet retrieves the audit trail settings 
    for the current ProjectWise datasource. It returns the enabled or 
    disabled status of audit logging for various object types, including 
    projects (folders), documents, document sets, users, groups, and user 
    lists.
    
    You can optionally specify the `-AuditTrailType` parameter to filter the 
    results to a specific type: `project`, `document`, `set`, `user`, 
    `group`, or `userlist`. If no type is specified, the cmdlet returns 
    settings for all supported types.
    
    The cmdlet also checks for the presence of a secondary audit trail table 
    and includes its information if available. The output is a sorted list 
    of audit trail settings, with each entry indicating whether logging is 
    enabled for a specific action and object type.
    

PARAMETERS
    -AuditTrailType <string>
        
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    0
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    <CommonParameters>
        This cmdlet supports the common parameters: Verbose, Debug,
        ErrorAction, ErrorVariable, WarningAction, WarningVariable,
        OutBuffer, PipelineVariable, and OutVariable. For more information, 
    see 
        about_CommonParameters 
    (https:/go.microsoft.com/fwlink/?LinkID=113216). 
    
INPUTS
    
OUTPUTS
    
NOTES
    
    
        
    
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 1 --------------------------
    
    Example usage for getting Audit Trail data.
    Get-PWAuditTrailSettings
    Returns all audit trail settings for current datasource.
    
    
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 2 --------------------------
    
    Example usage for getting only the project/folder Audit Trail data.
    Get-PWAuditTrailSettings -AuditTrailType 'project'
    Returns only the project/folder audit trail settings for current 
    datasource.
    
    
    
RELATED LINKS

## Get-PWCelLibraryExpression


NAME
    Get-PWCelLibraryExpression
    
SYNOPSIS
    Retrieves CEL Library Expressions.
    
    
SYNTAX
    Get-PWCelLibraryExpression [-Name] <String> [-Raw] [<CommonParameters>]
    
    
DESCRIPTION
    The Get-PWCelLibraryExpression cmdlet retrieves CEL library expressions 
    from the ProjectWise datasource.
    
    When called without parameters, it returns a list of all available CEL 
    library expression names and descriptions.
    
    When called with the -Name parameter, it returns the full details of a 
    specific CEL library expression in a formatted display showing the name, 
    expression, and description.
    
    When called with both -Name and -Raw, it returns only the raw expression 
    string without any formatting.
    

PARAMETERS
    -Name <String>
        The name of the CEL library expression to retrieve. If not 
        specified, returns a list of all available CEL library expression 
        names and descriptions.
        
        Required?                    true
        Position?                    0
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -Raw [<SwitchParameter>]
        When specified with -Name, returns only the raw expression string 
        without any formatting.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    named
        Default value                False
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    <CommonParameters>
        This cmdlet supports the common parameters: Verbose, Debug,
        ErrorAction, ErrorVariable, WarningAction, WarningVariable,
        OutBuffer, PipelineVariable, and OutVariable. For more information, 
    see 
        about_CommonParameters 
    (https:/go.microsoft.com/fwlink/?LinkID=113216). 
    
INPUTS
    
OUTPUTS
    
NOTES
    
    
        
    
    --------- Example 1: List all CEL library expressions ---------
    
    PS C:\> Get-PWCelLibraryExpression
    
    Returns a list of all CEL library expression names and descriptions.
    ------- Example 2: Get a specific CEL library expression -------
    
    PS C:\> Get-PWCelLibraryExpression -Name "MyExpression"
    
    Returns the full details of the specified CEL library expression in a 
    formatted display showing the name, expression content, and description.
    ----------- Example 3: Get the raw expression string -----------
    
    PS C:\> Get-PWCelLibraryExpression -Name "MyExpression" -Raw
    
    Returns only the raw expression string without any formatting or labels. 
    Useful when you need to pass the expression content to another command.
    
RELATED LINKS

## Get-PWDatasource


NAME
    Get-PWDatasource
    
SYNOPSIS
    Gets all available ProjectWise Datasources.
    
    
SYNTAX
    Get-PWDatasource [-Active] [<CommonParameters>]
    
    
DESCRIPTION
    Get-PWDatasource simply lists all available ProjectWise Datasources.
    

PARAMETERS
    -Active [<SwitchParameter>]
        Returns the active datasource.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    named
        Default value                False
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    <CommonParameters>
        This cmdlet supports the common parameters: Verbose, Debug,
        ErrorAction, ErrorVariable, WarningAction, WarningVariable,
        OutBuffer, PipelineVariable, and OutVariable. For more information, 
    see 
        about_CommonParameters 
    (https:/go.microsoft.com/fwlink/?LinkID=113216). 
    
INPUTS
    None
        You cannot pipe objects to this cmdlet.
    
    
OUTPUTS
    Bentley.ProjectWise.PowerShell.Common.Datasource
        Get-PWDatasource returns an object that represents each ProjectWise 
        Datasource.
    
    
NOTES
    
    
        
    
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 1 --------------------------
    
    Get-PWDatasource
    
    InternalName      HostName               ServerAddress    InterfaceType 
    DatabaseType IsSSO IsSecure
    ------------      --------               -------------    ------------- 
    ------------ ----- --------
    sampleDS01        pcName.domain.com      192.168.0.1          ODBC       
    SQLServer     Y      N
    
    Description

## Get-PWDatasourceConfigEntry


NAME
    Get-PWDatasourceConfigEntry
    
SYNOPSIS
    Gets the DatasourceConfigEntries on the specified host.
    
    
SYNTAX
    Get-PWDatasourceConfigEntry [-HostName] <String> [[-DBID] <String>] 
    [<CommonParameters>]
    
    
DESCRIPTION
    The Get-PWDatasourceConfigEntry cmdlet gets the datasource config 
    entries in the specified host's dmskrnl.cfg.
    
    By default, Get-PWDatasourceConfigEntry lists all entries, but when you 
    use the DBID parameter, Get-PWDatasourceConfigEntry searches for an 
    entry with specific DBID field.
    

PARAMETERS
    -DBID <String>
        Retrieves datasource config entry with the specific DBID.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    1
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -HostName <String>
        Specifies the hostname with ProjectWise Server configured.
        
        Required?                    true
        Position?                    0
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    <CommonParameters>
        This cmdlet supports the common parameters: Verbose, Debug,
        ErrorAction, ErrorVariable, WarningAction, WarningVariable,
        OutBuffer, PipelineVariable, and OutVariable. For more information, 
    see 
        about_CommonParameters 
    (https:/go.microsoft.com/fwlink/?LinkID=113216). 
    
INPUTS
    
OUTPUTS
    Bentley.ProjectWise.PowerShell.Common.DatasourceDonfigEntry
        Get-PWDatasourceConfigEntry returns an object(s) which represents 
        each datasource config entry presented in dmskrnl.cfg.
    
    
NOTES
    
    
        Get-PWDatasourceDonfigEntry does not need opened PW connection.
    
    -------------------------- Example 1 --------------------------
    
    get-PWDatasourceConfigEntry -HostName HOSTNAME
    
    Lists all entries on the specified host.
    
    DBID                 : db0 Description          : ds1 Type               
      : SQLServer DisplayName          : ds1 InterfaceType        : ODBC 
    Name                 : ds1 DBUserName           : sa SSO                 
     : 0 STS                  : 0 ImsServiceIdentities : {}
    
    DBID                 : db1 Description          : ds2 Type               
      : SQLServer DisplayName          : InterfaceType        : ODBC Name    
                 : ds2 DBUserName           : sa SSO                  : 0 
    STS                  : 0 ImsServiceIdentities : {}
    -------------------------- Example 2 --------------------------
    
    get-PWDatasourceConfigEntry HOSTNAME db1
    
    Lists entry with specific DBID on the specified host.
    
    DBID          : db1 Description   : ds2 Type          : SQLServer 
    DisplayName   : InterfaceType : ODBC Name          : ds2 DBUserName    : 
    sa SSO           : 0
    
RELATED LINKS
    Initialize-PWDatasourceConfigEntry 
    Remove-PWDatasourceConfigEntry 
    Update-PWDatasourceConfigEntry

## Get-PWDatasourceSetting


NAME

    <DatasourceSetting+Category>] [<CommonParameters>]
    
    
DESCRIPTION

    setting in the current ProjectWise datasource.
    

PARAMETERS

        The category (group) of the setting to retrieve.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    named
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       True (ByPropertyName)
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    <CommonParameters>
        This cmdlet supports the common parameters: Verbose, Debug,
        ErrorAction, ErrorVariable, WarningAction, WarningVariable,
        OutBuffer, PipelineVariable, and OutVariable. For more information, 
    see 
        about_CommonParameters 
    (https:/go.microsoft.com/fwlink/?LinkID=113216). 
    
INPUTS
    
OUTPUTS
    
        Get-PWDatasourceSetting returns a Datasource Settings object.
    
    
NOTES

        Open-PWConnection before calling Get-PWDatasourceSetting.

    FederatedIdentity         PromptUserToAssociateIdentity       0

## Get-PWDatasourceStatistics


NAME
    Get-PWDatasourceStatistics
    
SYNOPSIS
    Gets the statistics of the current ProjectWise Datasource.
    
    
SYNTAX
    Get-PWDatasourceStatistics [-Refresh] [<CommonParameters>]
    
    
DESCRIPTION
    The Get-PWDatasourceStatistics cmdlet gets the statistics of the current 
    ProjectWise Datasource. The -Refresh flag triggers a database refresh 
    before retrieving the information.
    

PARAMETERS
    -Refresh [<SwitchParameter>]
        Refreshes the Datasource statistics before retrieving them.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    named
        Default value                False
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    <CommonParameters>
        This cmdlet supports the common parameters: Verbose, Debug,
        ErrorAction, ErrorVariable, WarningAction, WarningVariable,
        OutBuffer, PipelineVariable, and OutVariable. For more information, 
    see 
        about_CommonParameters 
    (https:/go.microsoft.com/fwlink/?LinkID=113216). 
    
INPUTS
    None
        You cannot pipe objects to this cmdlet.
    
    
OUTPUTS
    Bentley.ProjectWise.PowerShell.Common.StatisticRecord
        Get-PWDatasourceStatistics returns a list of objects that represent 
        statistical records.
    
    
NOTES
    
    
        Get-PWDatasourceStatistics requires an open ProjectWise Connection 
        in the current thread. You can open a Connection with 
        Open-PWConnection before calling Get-PWDatasourceStatistics.
        
        Get-PWDatasourceStatistics -Refresh requires an open Admin session. 
        You can open an Admin session with Open-PWConnection -Admin before 
        calling Get-PWDatasourceStatistics -Refresh.
    
    -------------------------- Example 1 --------------------------
    
    Get-PWDatasourceStatistics
    
    Retrieves the current Datasource statistics without refreshing the 
    database and outputs them.
    
    CategoryID CategoryDescription PropertyID PropertyDescription            
            PropertyValue
    ---------- ------------------- ---------- -------------------            
            -------------
    0 SystemProperty               0 Last Updated                           
    2016-05-24 12:24:26
    0 SystemProperty               0 Current Time                           
    2016-05-24 12:31:03
    0 SystemProperty               0 Datasource Name                        
    // Active Datasource          1 Audit                       10 Items 
    with Audit Trail                 1
    1 Audit                       11 Minimum Audit Trail Records Per Folder 0
    1 Audit                       12 Maximum Audit Trail Records Per Folder 1
    1 Audit                       13 Average Audit Trail Records Per Folder 0
    1 Audit                       25 Audit Trail Records                    
    16
    2 Datasource                  20 Number of Storage Areas                1
    2 Datasource                  27 Number of User View Records            0
    
    (further output truncated)
    -------------------------- Example 2 --------------------------
    
    Get-PWDatasourceStatistics -Refresh
    
    Retrieves the current Datasource statistics after refreshing the 
    database and outputs them.
    
    CategoryID CategoryDescription PropertyID PropertyDescription            
            PropertyValue
    ---------- ------------------- ---------- -------------------            
            -------------
    0 SystemProperty               0 Last Updated                           
    2016-05-24 12:34:25
    0 SystemProperty               0 Current Time                           
    2016-05-24 12:34:25
    0 SystemProperty               0 Datasource Name                        
    // Active Datasource          1 Audit                       10 Items 
    with Audit Trail                 1
    1 Audit                       11 Minimum Audit Trail Records Per Folder 0
    1 Audit                       12 Maximum Audit Trail Records Per Folder 1
    1 Audit                       13 Average Audit Trail Records Per Folder 0
    1 Audit                       25 Audit Trail Records                    
    16
    2 Datasource                  20 Number of Storage Areas                1
    2 Datasource                  27 Number of User View Records            0
    
    (further output truncated)
    
    Note: After setting the -Refresh flag the "Last Updated" and "Current 
    Time" properties become the same.
    -------------------------- Example 3 --------------------------
    
    Get-PWDatasourceStatistics -Refresh | Export-Csv "c:\output.csv"
    
    Retrieves the current Datasource statistics after refreshing the 
    database. The output is then exported to a CSV file.
    
RELATED LINKS

## Get-PWDocumentAuditTrailRecords


NAME
    Get-PWDocumentAuditTrailRecords
    
SYNOPSIS
    Retrieves audit records for selected ProjectWise documents.
    
    
SYNTAX
    Get-PWDocumentAuditTrailRecords [-InputDocuments] 
    <ProjectWiseDocument[]> [[-OutputFilePathName] <string>] 
    [[-OutputFormat] <string>] -StartDate <DateTime> -EndDate <DateTime> 
    [<CommonParameters>]
    
    Get-PWDocumentAuditTrailRecords [-FolderPath] <string> [-DocumentName] 
    <string> [[-OutputFilePathName] <string>] [[-OutputFormat] <string>] 
    -StartDate <DateTime> -EndDate <DateTime> [<CommonParameters>]
    
    
DESCRIPTION
    Retrieves audit trail records for one or more ProjectWise documents 
    within a specified date range.
    
    You can identify documents in the following ways: - By piping 
    `ProjectWiseDocument` objects to `-InputDocuments`
    
    - By specifying `-FolderPath` and `-DocumentName`
    
    
    The `-StartDate` and `-EndDate` parameters are required and used to 
    filter audit trail entries.
    If `-OutputFilePathName` is provided, `-OutputFormat` is required and 
    must be either `Excel` or `SQLite`. When no output file is specified, 
    results are written to the pipeline as a `DataTable`.
    
    

PARAMETERS
    -InputDocuments <ProjectWiseDocument[]>
        Target document object(s) from pipeline.
        
        Required?                    true
        Position?                    0
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       True (ByValue)
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -FolderPath <string>
        Full folder path to folder containing document of retrieve audit 
        trail records of.
        
        Required?                    true
        Position?                    0
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -DocumentName <string>
        Document to retrieve audit trail records for.
        
        Required?                    true
        Position?                    1
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -OutputFilePathName <string>
        Provide file path and name if you want to write data to a 
        spreadsheet.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    2
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -OutputFormat <string>
        HelpMessage = "If OutputFilePathName is provided, the OutputFormat 
        is required. Specifies export type. Valid entries are Excel and 
        SQLite."
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    3
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -StartDate <DateTime>
        Start date to filter audit trail records. Format: 'YYYY-MM-DD' or 
        'YYYY-MM-DD HH:mm:ss'. Example: '2024-01-01'.
        
        Required?                    true
        Position?                    named
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -EndDate <DateTime>
        End date to filter audit trail records. Format: 'YYYY-MM-DD' or 
        'YYYY-MM-DD HH:mm:ss'. Example: '2024-12-31'. When only a date is 
        provided, time defaults to 23:59:59.
        
        Required?                    true
        Position?                    named
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    <CommonParameters>
        This cmdlet supports the common parameters: Verbose, Debug,
        ErrorAction, ErrorVariable, WarningAction, WarningVariable,
        OutBuffer, PipelineVariable, and OutVariable. For more information, 
    see 
        about_CommonParameters 
    (https:/go.microsoft.com/fwlink/?LinkID=113216). 
    
INPUTS
    PWPS_DAB.CommonTypes+ProjectWiseDocument[]
        Target document object(s) from pipeline.
    
    System.DateTime
        Start and end dates used to filter audit trail records.
    
    
OUTPUTS
    
NOTES
    
    
        
    
    -------------------------- Example 1 --------------------------
    
    PS C:\> Get-PWDocumentAuditTrailRecords -FolderPath "Documents\ProjectA" 
    -DocumentName "Spec.docx" -StartDate "2024-01-01" -EndDate "2024-01-31"
    
    Returns audit trail records for `Spec.docx` in `Documents\ProjectA` 
    between January 1 and January 31, 2024.
    -------------------------- Example 2 --------------------------
    
    PS C:\> Get-PWDocumentsByGUIDs -DocumentGUIDs "d0bc426a-973e-4ebe-8764-77
    39e81e9182","31050aa0-bf0c-4804-a9f4-236c54496f33" | 
    Get-PWDocumentAuditTrailRecords -StartDate "2024-01-01" -EndDate 
    "2024-01-31"
    
    Pipes document objects resolved from GUIDs into the cmdlet and returns 
    audit trail records for the specified date range.
    -------------------------- Example 3 --------------------------
    
    PS C:\> Get-PWDocumentAuditTrailRecords -FolderPath "Documents\ProjectA" 
    -DocumentName "Spec.docx" -StartDate "2024-01-01" -EndDate "2024-01-31" 
    -OutputFilePathName "C:\Temp\DocumentAudit.xlsx" -OutputFormat Excel
    
    Exports the filtered audit trail records to an Excel file.
    -------------------------- Example 4 --------------------------
    
    PS C:\> Get-PWDocumentAuditTrailRecords -FolderPath "Documents\ProjectA" 
    -DocumentName "Spec.docx" -StartDate "2024-01-01" -EndDate "2024-01-31" 
    -OutputFilePathName "C:\Temp\DocumentAudit.sqlite" -OutputFormat SQLite
    
    Exports the filtered audit trail records to a SQLite file.
    
RELATED LINKS

## Get-PWDocumentFromParameters


NAME
    Get-PWDocumentFromParameters
    
SYNOPSIS
    Gets a ProjectWise document(s) by searching within a specified folder 
    using either the document name or file name.
    
    
SYNTAX
    Get-PWDocumentFromParameters [-InputFolder] <ProjectWiseFolder> 
    [-DocumentName] <String> [[-Version] <String>] [<CommonParameters>]
    
    Get-PWDocumentFromParameters [-InputFolder] <ProjectWiseFolder> 
    [-FileName] <String> [[-Version] <String>] [<CommonParameters>]
    
    Get-PWDocumentFromParameters [-FolderPath] <String> [-DocumentName] 
    <String> [[-Version] <String>] [<CommonParameters>]
    
    Get-PWDocumentFromParameters [-FolderPath] <String> [-FileName] <String> 
    [[-Version] <String>] [<CommonParameters>]
    
    
DESCRIPTION
    The Get-PWDocumentFromParameters cmdlet retrieves a ProjectWise 
    document(s) from a specific folder. You can specify the folder using 
    either a folder path string or a folder object, and search for the 
    document using either its document name or file name. Optionally, you 
    can specify a version to retrieve a specific document version.
    
    IMPORTANT: When specifying nested folders (e.g., Top-Level\Sub-Folder), 
    provide only the folder path without including the document or file 
    name. The document/file name must be provided separately using the 
    -DocumentName or -FileName parameter.
    

PARAMETERS
    -InputFolder <ProjectWiseFolder>
        A ProjectWise folder object that specifies where to search for the 
        document. Use Get-PWFolders to retrieve a folder object. For nested 
        folders (e.g., Parent\Child), the folder object should represent the 
        complete folder path, but should NOT include the document or file 
        name.
        
        Required?                    true
        Position?                    0
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       True (ByValue)
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -FolderPath <String>
        The folder path string that specifies where to search for the 
        document. For nested folders, use backslash-separated paths (e.g., 
        'Parent\Child\GrandChild'). The path should include only the folder 
        structure and should NOT include the document or file name you want 
        to retrieve.
        
        Required?                    true
        Position?                    0
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -DocumentName <String>
        The document name to search for within the specified folder. This 
        parameter cannot be used together with FileName. Use this when you 
        want to search by the ProjectWise document name property.
        
        Required?                    true
        Position?                    1
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -FileName <String>
        The file name to search for within the specified folder. This 
        parameter cannot be used together with DocumentName. Use this when 
        you want to search by the file name property of the document.
        
        Required?                    true
        Position?                    1
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -Version <String>
        Optional. The version of the document to retrieve. If not specified, 
        the cmdlet returns the document without version filtering. When 
        specified, only the document with the matching version will be 
        returned.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    2
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    <CommonParameters>
        This cmdlet supports the common parameters: Verbose, Debug,
        ErrorAction, ErrorVariable, WarningAction, WarningVariable,
        OutBuffer, PipelineVariable, and OutVariable. For more information, 
    see 
        about_CommonParameters 
    (https:/go.microsoft.com/fwlink/?LinkID=113216). 
    
INPUTS
    PWPS_DAB.CommonTypes+ProjectWiseFolder
        A ProjectWise folder object that specifies where to search for the 
        document. Required when using InputFolderWithDocumentName or 
        InputFolderWithFileName parameter sets.
    
    System.String (FolderPath)
        The folder path string that specifies where to search for the 
        document. Required when using FolderPathWithDocumentName or 
        FolderPathWithFileName parameter sets.
    
    System.String (DocumentName)
        The document name to search for within the specified folder. 
        Required when using InputFolderWithDocumentName or 
        FolderPathWithDocumentName parameter sets.
    
    System.String (FileName)
        The file name to search for within the specified folder. Required 
        when using InputFolderWithFileName or FolderPathWithFileName 
        parameter sets.
    
    System.String (Version)
        The version of the document to retrieve. Optional in all parameter 
        sets.
    
    
OUTPUTS
    PWPS_DAB.CommonTypes+ProjectWiseDocument
        Returns one or more ProjectWise document objects that match the 
        search criteria.
    
    
NOTES
    
    
        - You cannot use both DocumentName and FileName parameters in the 
        same command.
        
        - You cannot use both FolderPath and InputFolder parameters in the 
        same command.
        
        - When multiple documents match the search criteria, all matching 
        documents are returned sorted by DocumentID in ascending order.
        
        - The folder path should only contain the folder structure, not the 
        document or file name.
    
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 1 --------------------------
    
    Get-PWDocumentFromParameters -FolderPath 'Top-Level\Sub-Folder' 
    -DocumentName 'TestDocument.dgn'
    
    Retrieves the document named 'TestDocument.dgn' from the folder 
    'Top-Level\Sub-Folder'.
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 2 --------------------------
    
    Get-PWDocumentFromParameters -FolderPath 'Top-Level\Sub-Folder' 
    -DocumentName 'TestDocument.dgn' -Version 'v1'
    
    Retrieves version 'v1' of the document named 'TestDocument.dgn' from the 
    folder 'Top-Level\Sub-Folder'.
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 3 --------------------------
    
    Get-PWDocumentFromParameters -FolderPath 'Top-Level\Sub-Folder' 
    -FileName 'TestFile.dwg'
    
    Retrieves the document with file name 'TestFile.dwg' from the folder 
    'Top-Level\Sub-Folder'.
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 4 --------------------------
    
    $folder = Get-PWFolders -FolderPath 'Top-Level\Sub-Folder' -JustOne
    Get-PWDocumentFromParameters -InputFolder $folder -DocumentName 
    'TestDocument.dgn'
    
    Retrieves the document named 'TestDocument.dgn' from the folder object 
    stored in $folder. The JustOne parameter ensures only the specified 
    folder is returned, not its subfolders.
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 5 --------------------------
    
    $folder = Get-PWFolders -FolderPath 'Top-Level\Sub-Folder' -JustOne
    Get-PWDocumentFromParameters -InputFolder $folder -FileName 
    'TestFile.dwg' -Version 'v2'
    
    Retrieves the 'v2' version of the file named 'TestFile.dwg' from the 
    folder object. The JustOne parameter ensures only the specified folder 
    is returned, not its subfolders.
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 6 --------------------------
    
    Get-PWDocumentFromParameters -FolderPath 
    'Top-Level\Sub-Folder\Nested-Folder' -DocumentName 'TestDocument.dgn'
    
    Retrieves the document 'TestDocument.dgn' from the nested folder path 
    'Top-Level\Sub-Folder\Nested-Folder'. Note that the folder path does not 
    include the document name.
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 7 --------------------------
    
    Get-PWFolders -FolderPath 'Top-Level\Sub-Folder' -JustOne | 
    Get-PWDocumentFromParameters -DocumentName 'TestDocument.dgn'
    
    Retrieves the document 'TestDocument.dgn' by piping a folder object to 
    the cmdlet. The JustOne parameter ensures only the specified folder is 
    piped, not its subfolders.
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 8 --------------------------
    
    Get-PWFolders -FolderPath 'Top-Level\Sub-Folder' -JustOne | 
    Get-PWDocumentFromParameters -FileName 'TestFile.dwg'
    
    Retrieves the document 'TestFile.dwg' by piping a folder object to the 
    cmdlet. The JustOne parameter ensures only the specified folder is 
    piped, not its subfolders.
    
RELATED LINKS





--

## Get-PWDocumentsBySearch


NAME
    Get-PWDocumentsBySearch
    
SYNOPSIS
    Searches for ProjectWise documents.
    
    
SYNTAX
    Get-PWDocumentsBySearch [[-SearchName] <string>] [[-FolderPath] 
    <string>] [[-Attributes] <Hashtable>] [[-Slow]] [[-Personal]] 
    [[-ReturnDataTable]] [[-Version] <string>] [[-PopulatePath]] 
    [[-LatitudeMin] <double>] [[-LongitudeMin] <double>] [[-LatitudeMax] 
    <double>] [[-LongitudeMax] <double>] [[-FolderID] <int>] 
    [[-FullTextSearchMatchAllWords]] [[-FullTextSearchMatchWholePhrase]] 
    [[-FullTextSearchAttributeValues]] [[-PathOfSearchProject] <string>] 
    [[-GetVersionsToo]] [[-GetAttributes]] [[-JustThisFolder]] [[-FileName] 
    <string>] [[-DocumentName] <string>] [[-SearchString] <string>] 
    [[-Environment] <string>] [<CommonParameters>]
    
    
DESCRIPTION
    Searches the current ProjectWise datasource by saved search, folder, 
    document criteria, text, attributes, and location. A saved search takes 
    precedence when you provide `SearchName`.
    
    Folder searches include subfolders by default. Use `JustThisFolder` to 
    limit the search.
    
    Broad searches can take longer to return results.
    

PARAMETERS
    -PathOfSearchProject <string>
        If search belongs to a rich project, send the path to the rich 
        project.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    23
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -SearchName <string>
        Search name to execute. Send full path of search if nested. 
        SearchName will supersede any other search parameters.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    0
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -FolderPath <string>
        Folder path to search.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    1
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -FolderID <int>
        Folder ID to search.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    2
        Default value                0
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -GetVersionsToo [<SwitchParameter>]
        Return document versions.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    3
        Default value                False
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -GetAttributes [<SwitchParameter>]
        Populate attributes in document object. Will degrade performance. It 
        is much faster to use Get-PWDocumentsBySearchWithReturnColumns.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    4
        Default value                False
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -JustThisFolder [<SwitchParameter>]
        Only search this folder and not its sub-folders.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    5
        Default value                False
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -FileName <string>
        Filename to search for. Supports wildcards like '%.pdf'
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    6
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -DocumentName <string>
        Document Name to search for. Supports wildcards like '%.pdf'
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    7
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -SearchString <string>
        Full text search string.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    8
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -Environment <string>
        Environment name.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    9
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -Attributes <Hashtable>
        Hashtable of attributes to search for. Hashtable in PowerShell looks 
        like this @{Name = 'Value'; Name2 = 'Value2' }
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    10
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -Slow [<SwitchParameter>]
        Set to fill in document name path and attributes in document object 
        (same as -GetAttributes). It is much faster to use 
        Get-PWDocumentsBySearchWithReturnColumns.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    11
        Default value                False
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -Personal [<SwitchParameter>]
        Set to use a personal search.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    12
        Default value                False
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -ReturnDataTable [<SwitchParameter>]
        Return DataTable object rather than array of Document objects.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    13
        Default value                False
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -Version <string>
        Version to filter on.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    14
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -PopulatePath [<SwitchParameter>]
        Populate path on return objects/table. Will degrade performance, but 
        not as much as -GetAttributes or -Slow.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    15
        Default value                False
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -LatitudeMin <double>
        Minimum latitude in decimal degrees. Range -90.00 to 90.00.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    16
        Default value                1.79769313486232E+308
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -LongitudeMin <double>
        Minimum longitude in decimal degrees. Range -180.00 to 180.00.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    17
        Default value                1.79769313486232E+308
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -LatitudeMax <double>
        Maximum latitude in decimal degrees. Range -90.00 to 90.00.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    18
        Default value                1.79769313486232E+308
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -LongitudeMax <double>
        Maximum longitude in decimal degrees. Range -180.00 to 180.00.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    19
        Default value                1.79769313486232E+308
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -FullTextSearchMatchAllWords [<SwitchParameter>]
        If SearchString is provided, this will split the string into words 
        at the spaces and require the content to contain all of the words in 
        the string. Otherwise, will match on any word.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    20
        Default value                False
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -FullTextSearchMatchWholePhrase [<SwitchParameter>]
        If SearchString is provided, this searches for the whole phrase and 
        does not split the string into words.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    21
        Default value                False
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -FullTextSearchAttributeValues [<SwitchParameter>]
        If SearchString is provided, this searches in the attribute values 
        rather than in the file content.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    22
        Default value                False
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    <CommonParameters>
        This cmdlet supports the common parameters: Verbose, Debug,
        ErrorAction, ErrorVariable, WarningAction, WarningVariable,
        OutBuffer, PipelineVariable, and OutVariable. For more information, 
    see 
        about_CommonParameters 
    (https:/go.microsoft.com/fwlink/?LinkID=113216). 
    
INPUTS
    
OUTPUTS
    
NOTES
    
    
        
    
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 1 --------------------------
    
    Get-PWDocumentsBySearch -FolderPath "Test 6\Projects 2" -GetVersionsToo 
    -JustThisFolder -Filename '%.pdf'
    
    
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 2 --------------------------
    
    Get-PWDocumentsBySearch -FolderPath "Test 6\Projects 2" -GetVersionsToo 
    -SearchString 'Primary' -Filename '%.pdf'
    
    
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 3 --------------------------
    
    Get-PWDocumentsBySearch -FolderPath "Test 6\Projects 2" -Attributes 
    @{Name = 'Value'; Name2 = 'Value2' }
    
    
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 4 --------------------------
    
    Get-PWDocumentsBySearch -SearchName "PDFs in Project"
    
    
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 5 --------------------------
    
    Get-PWDocumentsBySearch -SearchName "Rich Project From 
    PowerShell\Project 02\PDF Search made by PowerShell" -GetAttributes
    
    
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 6 --------------------------
    
    Get-PWDocumentsBySearch -SearchName "Project Searches\PDF Search made by 
    PowerShell" -PathOfSearchProject "Projects\Project 01"

## Get-PWDocumentsBySearchExtended


NAME
    Get-PWDocumentsBySearchExtended
    
SYNOPSIS
    Get an array of ProjectWiseDocument objects based on a saved search or 
    from a folder tree.
    
    
SYNTAX
    Get-PWDocumentsBySearchExtended [[-FolderPath] <string>] 
    [[-GetVersionsToo]] [[-FileUpdatedBefore] <DateTime>] 
    [[-ColumnsToReturn] <string[]>] [[-ReturnDataTable]] [[-PopulatePath]] 
    [[-FullTextSearchMatchAllWords]] [[-FullTextSearchMatchWholePhrase]] 
    [[-FullTextSearchAttributeValues]] [[-DocumentUpdatedAfter] <DateTime>] 
    [[-DocumentUpdatedBefore] <DateTime>] [[-Workflows] <string[]>] 
    [[-JustThisFolder]] [[-Storages] <string[]>] [[-ItemTypes] 
    <eItemTypes[]>] [[-DocumentCreatedAfter] <DateTime>] 
    [[-DocumentCreatedBefore] <DateTime>] [[-Status] {CheckedIn | CheckedOut 
    | ComingIn | GoingOut | Exported | FinalStatus | Moving | None}] 
    [[-Applications] <string[]>] [[-Creators] <string[]>] [[-Updaters] 
    <string[]>] [[-CheckOutUsers] <string[]>] [[-LatitudeMiniumum] <double>] 
    [[-FileName] <string>] [[-LatitudeMaximum] <double>] 
    [[-LongitudeMiniumum] <double>] [[-LongitudeMaximum] <double>] 
    [[-SaveQueryName] <string>] [[-DocumentName] <string>] [[-SearchString] 
    <string>] [[-Environments] <string[]>] [[-Attributes] <Hashtable>] 
    [[-States] <string[]>] [[-FileUpdatedAfter] <DateTime>] 
    [<CommonParameters>]
    
    
DESCRIPTION
    Takes a folder path, full-text search string, document name or pattern, 
    file name or pattern along with a lot of other parameters and returns 
    the matching documents.
    

PARAMETERS
    -FolderPath <string>
        Folder path to search.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    0
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -GetVersionsToo [<SwitchParameter>]
        Return document versions.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    1
        Default value                False
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -JustThisFolder [<SwitchParameter>]
        Only search this folder and not its sub-folders.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    2
        Default value                False
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -FileName <string>
        Filename to search for. Supports wildcards like '%.pdf'
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    3
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -DocumentName <string>
        Document Name to search for. Supports wildcards like '%.pdf'
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    4
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -SearchString <string>
        Full text search string.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    5
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -Environments <string[]>
        Array of Environment names to search for (e.g. 
        Simple,Complex,"Project Environment").
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    6
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -Attributes <Hashtable>
        Hashtable of attribute search parameters. Hashtable in PowerShell 
        looks like this @{Name = 'Value'; Name2 = 'Value2' }
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    7
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -States <string[]>
        List of states to search for (e.g. Draft,Final,"Ready for Publish").
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    8
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -FileUpdatedAfter <DateTime>
        File updated after this date.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    9
        Default value                1/1/0001 12:00:00 AM
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -FileUpdatedBefore <DateTime>
        File updated before this date.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    10
        Default value                1/1/0001 12:00:00 AM
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -ColumnsToReturn <string[]>
        List of columns to populate in search results (not validated) (e.g. 
        ProjectName,ProjectNumber,ProjectLocation).
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    11
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -ReturnDataTable [<SwitchParameter>]
        If set, will return a data table. Default is to return an arrary of 
        document objects.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    12
        Default value                False
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -PopulatePath [<SwitchParameter>]
        Populate path on return objects/table. Will degrade performance 
        somewhat.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    13
        Default value                False
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -FullTextSearchMatchAllWords [<SwitchParameter>]
        If SearchString is provided, this will split the string into words 
        at the spaces and require the content to contain all of the words in 
        the string. Otherwise, will match on any word.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    14
        Default value                False
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -FullTextSearchMatchWholePhrase [<SwitchParameter>]
        If SearchString is provided, this searches for the whole phrase and 
        does not split the string into words.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    15
        Default value                False
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -FullTextSearchAttributeValues [<SwitchParameter>]
        If SearchString is provided, this searches in the attribute values 
        rather than in the file content.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    16
        Default value                False
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -DocumentUpdatedAfter <DateTime>
        Document updated after this date.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    17
        Default value                1/1/0001 12:00:00 AM
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -DocumentUpdatedBefore <DateTime>
        Document updated before this date.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    18
        Default value                1/1/0001 12:00:00 AM
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false

        Required?                    false
        Position?                    19

        Accept pipeline input?       False

        Accept wildcard characters?  false

        Position?                    21

        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false

        Position?                    22
        Default value                1/1/0001 12:00:00 AM
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false

        Required?                    false
        Position?                    23
        Default value                1/1/0001 12:00:00 AM
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -Status <eDmsStatus>
        Document status to search for (CheckedIn, CheckedOut, Exported, 
        Final Status etc.).
        
        Possible values: CheckedIn, CheckedOut, ComingIn, GoingOut, 
        Exported, FinalStatus, Moving, None
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    24
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -Applications <string[]>
        List of applications to search for.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    25
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -Creators <string[]>
        List of creator user names to search for.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    26
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -Updaters <string[]>
        List of updater user names to search for.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    27
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -CheckOutUsers <string[]>
        List of check out user names to search for.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    28
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -LatitudeMiniumum <double>
        Bounding box Minimum Latitude (-90 to 90).
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    29
        Default value                0
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -LatitudeMaximum <double>
        Bounding box Maximum Latitude (-90 to 90).
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    30
        Default value                0
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -LongitudeMiniumum <double>
        Bounding box Minimum Longitude (-180 to 180).
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    31
        Default value                0
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -LongitudeMaximum <double>
        Bounding box Maximum Longitude (-180 to 180).
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    32
        Default value                0
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -SaveQueryName <string>
        Optionally save this search as a global search. Useful for debugging.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    33
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false

        This cmdlet supports the common parameters: Verbose, Debug,

OUTPUTS

    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 1 --------------------------
    
    Get-PWDocumentsBySearchExtended -FolderPath "Test 6\Projects 2" 
    -Filename '%.pdf' -States "Draft","Pending Approval" -ColumnsToReturn 
    "PercentComplete","DrawingNumber" -Updater "user 2" -Status Exported
    
    
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 2 --------------------------

    -Filename '%.pdf' -GetVersionsToo -States "Draft","Pending Approval" 
    -SearchString "Primary" -ColumnsToReturn 
    "PercentComplete","DrawingNumber" -ItemTypes Set,Abstract
    
    
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 3 --------------------------
    
    Get-PWDocumentsBySearchExtended -FolderPath "Test 6\Projects 2"

## Get-PWEmptyLeafFolders


NAME
    Get-PWEmptyLeafFolders
    
SYNOPSIS
    Retrieves empty leaf folders in ProjectWise.
    
    
SYNTAX
    Get-PWEmptyLeafFolders [[-InputFolder] <ProjectWiseFolder[]>] 
    [[-FolderPath] <string>] [[-FillPaths]] [<CommonParameters>]
    
    
DESCRIPTION
    Retrieves ProjectWise folders that have no child folders and no 
    documents. Search supplied folders, a folder path, or the datasource 
    root.
    

PARAMETERS
    -InputFolder <ProjectWiseFolder[]>
        Folders from pipeline.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    0
        Default value                PWPS_DAB.CommonTypes+ProjectWiseFolder[]
        Accept pipeline input?       True (ByValue)
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -FolderPath <string>
        Folder path. Will search whole datasource if not provided and no 
        folders passed in pipeline.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    1
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -FillPaths [<SwitchParameter>]
        Fill paths (Slow).
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    2
        Default value                False
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    <CommonParameters>
        This cmdlet supports the common parameters: Verbose, Debug,
        ErrorAction, ErrorVariable, WarningAction, WarningVariable,
        OutBuffer, PipelineVariable, and OutVariable. For more information, 
    see 
        about_CommonParameters 
    (https:/go.microsoft.com/fwlink/?LinkID=113216). 
    
INPUTS
    PWPS_DAB.CommonTypes+ProjectWiseFolder[]
        Folders from pipeline.
    
    
OUTPUTS
    
NOTES
    
    
        
    
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 1 --------------------------
    
    $folders=Get-PWEmptyLeafFolders
    Get-PWEmptyLeafFolders -FolderPath "Top Level Folder\Sub-Folder"
    
    
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 2 --------------------------
    
    Get-PWFolders -FolderPath "Top Level Folder\Sub-Folder" -JustOne | 
    Get-PWEmptyLeafFolders

## Get-PWFileEAttributes


NAME
    Get-PWFileEAttributes
    
SYNOPSIS
    Retrieves attributes embedded in a ProjectWise document file.
    
    
SYNTAX
    Get-PWFileEAttributes [-DocumentID <Int32>] [-ProjectID <Int32>] 
    [<CommonParameters>]
    
    Get-PWFileEAttributes [-GUID <String>] [<CommonParameters>]
    
    
DESCRIPTION
    Retrieves environment attribute values embedded in a ProjectWise 
    document file. Select the document by GUID or by project and document 
    IDs.
    
    The cmdlet returns an attribute value for each matching attribute. Use 
    Get-PWDocumentEAttributes to read document attributes instead.
    

PARAMETERS
    -DocumentID <Int32>
        @{Text=}
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    named
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       True (ByPropertyName)
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -GUID <String>
        @{Text=}
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    named
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       True (ByPropertyName)
        Accept wildcard characters?  false

## Get-PWFlatSetsBySearch


NAME
    Get-PWFlatSetsBySearch
    
SYNOPSIS
    Get an array of flat set document objects based the search criteria.
    
    
SYNTAX
    Get-PWFlatSetsBySearch [[-FolderPath] <string>] [[-Attributes] 
    <Hashtable>] [[-ReturnDataTable]] [[-PopulatePath]] [[-FolderID] <int>] 
    [[-JustThisFolder]] [[-FileName] <string>] [[-DocumentName] <string>] 
    [[-Environment] <string>] [<CommonParameters>]
    
    
DESCRIPTION
    Get an array of flat set document objects based the search criteria.
    

PARAMETERS
    -FolderPath <string>

        Required?                    false

## Get-PWFolderAuditTrailRecords


NAME
    Get-PWFolderAuditTrailRecords
    
SYNOPSIS
    Retrieves audit trail records for one or more ProjectWise folders within 
    a specified date range, with optional inclusion of subfolder and 
    document audit trail records.
    
    
SYNTAX
    Get-PWFolderAuditTrailRecords [[-InputFolder] <ProjectWiseFolder[]>] 
    [[-OutputFilePathName] <string>] [[-OutputFormat] <string>] -StartDate 
    <DateTime> -EndDate <DateTime> [-IncludeSubFoldersOnly] 
    [-IncludeAllChildObjects] [<CommonParameters>]
    
    Get-PWFolderAuditTrailRecords [-FolderPath] <string> 
    [[-OutputFilePathName] <string>] [[-OutputFormat] <string>] -StartDate 
    <DateTime> -EndDate <DateTime> [-IncludeSubFoldersOnly] 
    [-IncludeAllChildObjects] [<CommonParameters>]
    
    
DESCRIPTION
    Get-PWFolderAuditTrailRecords retrieves audit trail records for the 
    specified ProjectWise folder(s) within a given date range. Audit trail 
    records include information such as the object type, object name, action 
    performed, timestamp, and the user who performed the action.
    
    The -StartDate and -EndDate parameters are required to define the date 
    range for retrieval. When only a date is provided for -EndDate without a 
    time component (e.g., '2024-12-31'), the time is automatically set to 
    23:59:59 to include the entire day.
    
    By default, only folder-level audit trail records are returned. Use the 
    -IncludeSubFoldersOnly switch to include audit trail records for all 
    subfolders within the hierarchy. Use the -IncludeAllChildObjects switch 
    to include audit trail records for documents contained within the 
    folder(s).
    
    Results can be output directly to the PowerShell pipeline or exported to 
    an Excel spreadsheet or SQLite database file.
    

PARAMETERS
    -InputFolder <ProjectWiseFolder[]>
        An array of ProjectWise folder objects, typically received from the 
        pipeline. Use this parameter to pass folder objects from cmdlets 
        like Get-PWFolders.
        
        This parameter cannot be used together with -FolderPath.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    0
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       True (ByValue)
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -FolderPath <string>
        The full folder path of the ProjectWise folder to retrieve audit 
        trail records for.
        
        This parameter cannot be used together with -InputFolder.
        
        Required?                    true
        Position?                    0
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       True (ByValue)
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -StartDate <DateTime>
        The start date for filtering audit trail records. Only records on or 
        after this date will be returned. Accepts date strings like 
        '2026-01-01' or '2026-01-01 14:30:00'.
        
        Required?                    true
        Position?                    named
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -EndDate <DateTime>
        The end date for filtering audit trail records. Only records on or 
        before this date will be returned. Accepts date strings like 
        '2026-12-31' or '2026-12-31 23:59:59'. When only a date is provided 
        without a time, the time is automatically set to 23:59:59 to include 
        the entire day.
        
        Required?                    true
        Position?                    named
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -IncludeSubFoldersOnly [<SwitchParameter>]
        When included, audit trail records for all subfolders within the 
        folder hierarchy are also returned.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    named
        Default value                False
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -IncludeAllChildObjects [<SwitchParameter>]
        When included, audit trail records for all documents contained 
        within the specified folder(s) are also returned.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    named
        Default value                False
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -OutputFilePathName <string>
        The full file path and name for exporting audit trail records. When 
        this parameter is provided, the -OutputFormat parameter is also 
        required.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    2
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -OutputFormat <string>
        Specifies the export format when -OutputFilePathName is provided. 
        Valid values are Excel and SQLite.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    3
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    <CommonParameters>
        This cmdlet supports the common parameters: Verbose, Debug,
        ErrorAction, ErrorVariable, WarningAction, WarningVariable,
        OutBuffer, PipelineVariable, and OutVariable. For more information, 
    see 
        about_CommonParameters 
    (https:/go.microsoft.com/fwlink/?LinkID=113216). 
    
INPUTS
    PWPS_DAB.CommonTypes+ProjectWiseFolder[]
        An array of ProjectWise folder objects passed via the pipeline.
    
    System.String
        A folder path string identifying the ProjectWise folder.
    
    
OUTPUTS
    System.Data.DataRow
        When no output file is specified, the cmdlet outputs DataRow objects 
        to the pipeline containing audit trail record details such as object 
        type, object name, action, timestamp, user information, and 
        additional action parameters.
    
    System.Boolean
        When an output file is specified, returns True if the export was 
        successful, or False if it failed.
    
    
NOTES
    
    
        - The -InputFolder and -FolderPath parameters cannot be used 
        together. Use one or the other.
        
        - The -StartDate and -EndDate parameters are required to prevent 
        retrieval of excessively large result sets.
        
        - When only a date is provided for -EndDate (e.g., '2026-03-10'), 
        the time is automatically set to 23:59:59 to include the entire day.
        
        - When -OutputFilePathName is specified, -OutputFormat must also be 
        provided. If omitted, a warning is displayed.
    
    EXAMPLE 1: Get audit trail records for a folder within a date range
    
    PS C:\> Get-PWFolderAuditTrailRecords -FolderPath 'Projects' -StartDate 
    '2026-01-01' -EndDate '2026-03-31'
    
    Returns all audit trail records for the specified folder 'Projects' 
    between January 1, 2026 and March 31, 2026.
    ----- EXAMPLE 2: Get audit trail records for a single day -----
    
    PS C:\> Get-PWFolderAuditTrailRecords -FolderPath 'Projects' -StartDate 
    '2026-03-10' -EndDate '2026-03-10'
    
    Returns all audit trail records for March 10, 2026. When only a date is 
    provided for -EndDate, the time defaults to 23:59:59 to include the 
    entire day.
    --- EXAMPLE 3: Get audit trail records including subfolders ---
    
    PS C:\> Get-PWFolderAuditTrailRecords -FolderPath 'Projects' -StartDate 
    '2026-01-01' -EndDate '2026-12-31' -IncludeSubFoldersOnly
    
    Returns audit trail records for the 'Projects' folder and all of its 
    subfolders for the year 2026.
    ---- EXAMPLE 4: Get audit trail records including documents ----
    
    PS C:\> Get-PWFolderAuditTrailRecords -FolderPath 'Projects' -StartDate 
    '2026-01-01' -EndDate '2026-12-31' -IncludeAllChildObjects
    
    Returns audit trail records for the 'Projects' folder and all documents 
    contained within it for the year 2026.
    EXAMPLE 5: Get audit trail records for folder, subfolders, and documents
    
    PS C:\> Get-PWFolderAuditTrailRecords -FolderPath 'Projects' -StartDate 
    '2026-01-01' -EndDate '2026-12-31' -IncludeSubFoldersOnly 
    -IncludeAllChildObjects
    
    Returns all audit trail records for the 'Projects' folder, all 
    subfolders, and all documents within the folder hierarchy for the year 
    2026.
    ------ EXAMPLE 6: Use pipeline input with folder objects ------
    
    PS C:\> Get-PWFolders -FolderName 'MyProjects' | 
    Get-PWFolderAuditTrailRecords -StartDate '2026-01-01' -EndDate 
    '2026-12-31' -IncludeAllChildObjects
    
    Pipes folder objects from Get-PWFolders and retrieves their audit trail 
    records including document records for the year 2026.
    ---- EXAMPLE 7: Export audit trail records to an Excel file ----
    
    PS C:\> $folders = Get-PWFolders -FolderPath 'MyProjects'
    PS C:\> Get-PWFolderAuditTrailRecords -InputFolder $folders -StartDate 
    '2026-01-01' -EndDate '2026-12-31' -IncludeSubFoldersOnly 
    -IncludeAllChildObjects -OutputFilePathName 
    'D:\Export\AuditTrailRecords.xlsx' -OutputFormat Excel
    
    Exports all audit trail records for the specified folder, subfolders, 
    and documents to an Excel spreadsheet for the year 2026.
    -- EXAMPLE 8: Export audit trail records to a SQLite database --
    
    PS C:\> Get-PWFolders -FolderPath 'MyProjects' | 
    Get-PWFolderAuditTrailRecords -StartDate '2026-01-01' -EndDate 
    '2026-12-31' -IncludeSubFoldersOnly -IncludeAllChildObjects 
    -OutputFilePathName 'D:\Export\AuditTrailRecords.sqlite' -OutputFormat 
    SQLite
    
    Exports all audit trail records for the piped folder objects, including 
    subfolders and documents, to a SQLite database file for the year 2026.
    
RELATED LINKS

## Get-PWFolderPathAndProperties


NAME
    Get-PWFolderPathAndProperties
    
SYNOPSIS
    Given a folder object, populate the path and the project properties if 
    available.
    
    
SYNTAX
    Get-PWFolderPathAndProperties [-InputFolder] <ProjectWiseFolder[]> 
    [<CommonParameters>]
    
    
DESCRIPTION
    Designed to take a folder from the pipeline and fill in the full path 
    and project properties if the folder is also a rich project.
    

PARAMETERS
    -InputFolder <ProjectWiseFolder[]>
        Folder object from pipeline.
        
        Required?                    true
        Position?                    0
        Default value                PWPS_DAB.CommonTypes+ProjectWiseFolder[]
        Accept pipeline input?       True (ByValue)
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    <CommonParameters>
        This cmdlet supports the common parameters: Verbose, Debug,
        ErrorAction, ErrorVariable, WarningAction, WarningVariable,
        OutBuffer, PipelineVariable, and OutVariable. For more information, 
    see 
        about_CommonParameters 
    (https:/go.microsoft.com/fwlink/?LinkID=113216). 
    
INPUTS
    PWPS_DAB.CommonTypes+ProjectWiseFolder[]
        Folder object from pipeline.
    
    
OUTPUTS
    
NOTES
    
    
        
    
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 1 --------------------------
    
    #Example usage getting folders and populating path.
    #Get the path and all properties for folder number 20315
    Get-PWFolders 20315 | Get-PWFolderPathAndProperties
    #Get the folder number and all properties for the given folder path
    Get-PWFolders -FolderPath 'Level 1\Level 2\Level 3\Level 4' -JustOne | 
    Get-PWFolderPathAndProperties

## Get-PWFolders


NAME
    Get-PWFolders
    
SYNOPSIS
    Gets a list of the folders in a given path or under a given folder ID.
    
    
SYNTAX
    Get-PWFolders [[-FolderPath] <string>] [[-FolderID] <int>] 
    [[-FolderName] <string>] [[-JustOne]] [[-BypassSearchAssembly]] 
    [[-OutputFileName] <string>] [[-Slow]] [[-PopulatePaths]] 
    [<CommonParameters>]
    
    
DESCRIPTION
    Takes a folder ID or folder path and enumerates all the folders beneath 
    it in the tree.
    

PARAMETERS
    -FolderID <int>
        Folder ID for enumerating sub-folders.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    1
        Default value                0
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -FolderPath <string>
        Folder path for enumerating sub-folders.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    0
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -FolderName <string>
        Folder name for enumerating folders. Instructs to search for 
        matching folder names.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    2
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -JustOne [<SwitchParameter>]
        Just return specified folder.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    3
        Default value                False
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -BypassSearchAssembly [<SwitchParameter>]
        OBSOLETE. Not used.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    4
        Default value                False
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -OutputFileName <string>
        Provide if you desire a report.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    5
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -Slow [<SwitchParameter>]
        Specify to populate folder path and project properties. Can take a 
        long time for a dense folder tree.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    6
        Default value                False
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -PopulatePaths [<SwitchParameter>]
        Populate paths. Should be more performant than -Slow though somewhat 
        less data is returned.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    7
        Default value                False
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    <CommonParameters>
        This cmdlet supports the common parameters: Verbose, Debug,
        ErrorAction, ErrorVariable, WarningAction, WarningVariable,
        OutBuffer, PipelineVariable, and OutVariable. For more information, 
    see 
        about_CommonParameters 
    (https:/go.microsoft.com/fwlink/?LinkID=113216). 
    
INPUTS
    
OUTPUTS
    
NOTES
    
    
        
    
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 1 --------------------------
    
    #Example usage for getting all the folders in a given folder path.
    #Get all the folders in this tree
    $folders = Get-PWFolders -FolderPath "PWPS Connected Project Test 
    6\Projects 2"
    #Get all the folders in this tree with paths populated
    $folders = Get-PWFolders -FolderPath "PWPS Connected Project Test 
    6\Projects 2" -PopulatePaths
    #Get all the folders in this tree with paths and all data populated
    $folders = Get-PWFolders -FolderPath "PWPS Connected Project Test 
    6\Projects 2" -Slow
    #Get all folders with name matching the pattern (fast)
    $folders = Get-PWFolders -FolderName "%project%"
    #to get the full paths populated, do this (slow)
    $folders.GetFullPath()
    
    
    
RELATED LINKS
