## Get-PWFolderTreeDocumentSize


NAME
    Get-PWFolderTreeDocumentSize
    
SYNOPSIS
    Retrieves the total document size for folder trees.
    
    
SYNTAX
    Get-PWFolderTreeDocumentSize [-InputFolder] <ProjectWiseFolder[]> 
    [[-OutputFileName] <string>] [<CommonParameters>]
    
    
DESCRIPTION
    Retrieves document counts, file-size totals, and latest update 
    information for each supplied folder and its subfolders.
    
    By default, it returns a result for each non-empty folder tree. Provide 
    OutputFileName if you want to write the report to an XLSX file.
    

PARAMETERS
    -InputFolder <ProjectWiseFolder[]>
        
        
        Required?                    true
        Position?                    0
        Default value                PWPS_DAB.CommonTypes+ProjectWiseFolder[]
        Accept pipeline input?       True (ByValue)
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -OutputFileName <string>
        Full path to output XLSX file.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    0
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    <CommonParameters>
        This cmdlet supports the common parameters: Verbose, Debug,
        ErrorAction, ErrorVariable, WarningAction, WarningVariable,
        OutBuffer, PipelineVariable, and OutVariable. For more information, 
    see 
        about_CommonParameters 
    (https:/go.microsoft.com/fwlink/?LinkID=113216). 
    
INPUTS
    PWPS_DAB.CommonTypes+ProjectWiseFolder[]
        
    
    
OUTPUTS
    
NOTES
    
    
        
    
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 1 --------------------------
    
    Get-PWFolders -FolderPath "Top Level Folder\Sub-Folder" | 
    Get-PWFolderTreeSize"
    
    
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 2 --------------------------
    
    Get-PWFolders -FolderPath "Top Level Folder\Sub-Folder" | 
    Get-PWFolderTreeSize -OutputFileName "D:\TEMP\FolderDocSizeReport.xlsx"

DESCRIPTION

    information for folder trees at the selected depth.
    
    The default depth is 1. Provide a folder to make the depth relative to 
    that folder. You can provide an output file name to write an XLSX report.

        Provide a folder to limit report.

        Position?                    0
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       True (ByValue)
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -OutputFileName <string>
        Full path to output XLSX file.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    2
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -FolderDepth <int>
        Use this to set the folder depth you want to report on (Defaults to 
        1 which is all top level folders).
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    1
        Default value                1
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    <CommonParameters>
        This cmdlet supports the common parameters: Verbose, Debug,
        ErrorAction, ErrorVariable, WarningAction, WarningVariable,
        OutBuffer, PipelineVariable, and OutVariable. For more information, 
    see 
        about_CommonParameters 
    (https:/go.microsoft.com/fwlink/?LinkID=113216). 
    
INPUTS
    PWPS_DAB.CommonTypes+ProjectWiseFolder
        Provide a folder to limit report.
    
    
OUTPUTS
    
NOTES
    
    
        
    
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 1 --------------------------
    
    Get-PWFolderTreeDocumentSizeByDepth -Depth 2"
    
    
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 2 --------------------------

    -b6c2-42a1-b83f-4cfd3b18c9ce}/' | Get-PWFolderTreeDocumentSizeByDepth

## Get-PWGroup


NAME
    Get-PWGroup
    
SYNOPSIS
    Gets the User Groups in the current ProjectWise Datasource.
    
    
SYNTAX
    Get-PWGroup [-Exclude] -UserID <Int32> [<CommonParameters>]
    
    Get-PWGroup [[-GroupID] <Int32>] [<CommonParameters>]
    
    Get-PWGroup [[-GroupName] <String>] [<CommonParameters>]
    
    
DESCRIPTION
    The Get-PWGroup cmdlet gets the User Groups in the current ProjectWise 
    Datasource.
    
    By default, Get-PWGroup lists all Groups in the Datasource.  When you 
    use the GroupID parameter, Get-PWGroup searches for a specific Group.  
    When you use the UserID parameter, Get-PWGroup searches and only returns 
    Groups that have the given User as a member.
    

PARAMETERS
    -Exclude [<SwitchParameter>]
        If set, retrieves only User Groups that do NOT have the User with 
        the given UserID as a member.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    named
        Default value                False
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -GroupID <Int32>
        Retrieves the User Group with the given GroupID, if it exists.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    0
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       True (ByPropertyName)
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -GroupName <String>
        Retrieves only User Groups that match this GroupName. Some wildcards 
        (*, ?) are allowed.
        
        The two wildcard characters supported are:  * (matches zero or more 
        characters, starting at the specified position)  ? (matches any 
        character at the specified position)
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    0
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -UserID <Int32>
        Retrieves only User Groups that have the User with this ID as a 
        member, unless -Exclude is set, in which case Get-PWGroup will 
        return only User Groups that do NOT have the User with this ID as a 
        member.
        
        Required?                    true
        Position?                    named
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       True (ByPropertyName)
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    <CommonParameters>
        This cmdlet supports the common parameters: Verbose, Debug,
        ErrorAction, ErrorVariable, WarningAction, WarningVariable,
        OutBuffer, PipelineVariable, and OutVariable. For more information, 
    see 
        about_CommonParameters 
    (https:/go.microsoft.com/fwlink/?LinkID=113216). 
    
INPUTS
    System.Int32
        You can pipe an integer representing the UserID to search for to 
        Get-PWGroup.
    
    
OUTPUTS
    Bentley.ProjectWise.PowerShell.Common.Group
        Get-PWGroup returns an object that represents each User Group.
    
    
NOTES
    
    
        Get-PWGroup requires an open ProjectWise Connection in the current 
        thread.  You can open a Connection with Open-PWConnection before 
        calling Get-PWUser.
    
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 1 --------------------------
    
    Get-PWGroup
    
    Get all ProjectWise Groups
    
    GroupID GroupName             Description          Type    SecProvider 
    ------- ---------             -----------          ----    -----------   
     1    Administrators        Administrators       Logical    2    
    Restricted Adminis... Administrator res... Logical    3    ANewGroup     
            This parameter is... Logical    4    DomainGroup           This 
    parameter is... Windows DOMAIN
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 2 --------------------------
    
    Get-PWGroup -GroupID 1
    
    Get a Group by GroupID
    
    GroupID GroupName             Description          Type    SecProvider 
    ------- ---------             -----------          ----    -----------   
     1    Administrators        Administrators       Logical
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 3 --------------------------
    
    Get-PWGroup -GroupName *?dministrator*
    
    Get Groups by GroupName (note that wildcards are supported)
    
    GroupID GroupName             Description          Type    SecProvider 
    ------- ---------             -----------          ----    -----------   
     1    Administrators        Administrators       Logical    2    
    Restricted Adminis... Administrator res... Logical
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 4 --------------------------
    
    Get-PWGroup -UserID 1
    
    Get Groups that a User (by UserID) is in
    
    GroupID GroupName             Description          Type    SecProvider 
    ------- ---------             -----------          ----    -----------   
     1    Administrators        Administrators       Logical    3    
    ANewGroup             This parameter is... Logical    4    DomainGroup   
            This parameter is... Windows DOMAIN
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 5 --------------------------
    
    Get-PWGroup -UserID 1 -Exclude
    
    Get Groups that a User (by UserID) is NOT in
    
    GroupID GroupName             Description          Type    SecProvider 
    ------- ---------             -----------          ----    -----------   
     2    Restricted Adminis... Administrator res... Logical
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 6 --------------------------
    
    Get-PWUser -UserName admin | Get-PWGroup
    
    Get Groups that a User (from the pipeline) is in
    
    GroupID GroupName             Description          Type    SecProvider 
    ------- ---------             -----------          ----    -----------   
     1    Administrators        Administrators       Logical    3    
    ANewGroup             This parameter is... Logical    4    DomainGroup   
            This parameter is... Windows DOMAIN
    
RELATED LINKS
    New-PWGroup 
    Remove-PWGroup 
    Update-PWGroup

## Get-PWGroupMember


NAME
    Get-PWGroupMember
    
SYNOPSIS
    Gets all Members of a given User Group in the current ProjectWise 
    Datasource.
    
    
SYNTAX
    Get-PWGroupMember [-GroupID] <Int32> [<CommonParameters>]
    
    
DESCRIPTION
    The Get-PWGroupMember cmdlet gets all Users that are currently members 
    of a given User Group in the current ProjectWise Datasource.
    
    Groups can be selected by ID.
    

PARAMETERS
    -GroupID <Int32>
        Retrieves all Users in the User Group with the given GroupID, if it 
        exists.
        
        Required?                    true
        Position?                    0
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       True (ByPropertyName)
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    <CommonParameters>
        This cmdlet supports the common parameters: Verbose, Debug,
        ErrorAction, ErrorVariable, WarningAction, WarningVariable,
        OutBuffer, PipelineVariable, and OutVariable. For more information, 
    see 
        about_CommonParameters 
    (https:/go.microsoft.com/fwlink/?LinkID=113216). 
    
INPUTS
    Bentley.ProjectWise.PowerShell.Common.Group
        You can optionally pipe Group objects to this cmdlet.
    
    
OUTPUTS
    Bentley.ProjectWise.PowerShell.Common.User
        Get-PWGroupMember returns a User object that represents each Group 
        Member.
    
    
NOTES
    
    
        Get-PWGroupMember requires an open ProjectWise Connection in the 
        current thread.  You can open a Connection with Open-PWConnection 
        before calling Get-PWGroupMember.
        
        Get-PWGroupMember will work without an Admin session, but some 
        information in the User objects can only be retrieved in an Admin 
        session.  These fields are simply set to null for a non-Admin 
        session.
    
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 1 --------------------------
    
    Get-PWGroupMember -GroupID 1
    
    Get all the Users in a Group (by GroupID)
    
    UserID UserName      Description   Type    SecProvider  IsConnected 
    IsDisabled ------ --------      -----------   ----    -----------  
    ----------- ----------   1    admin                       Logical        
               Y           N
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 2 --------------------------
    
    Get-PWGroup | %{ Write-Host $_.GroupName:; $_ } | Get-PWGroupMember
    
    List all the Users in each ProjectWise Group
    
    Administrators :
    
    UserID UserName      Description   Type    SecProvider  IsConnected 
    IsDisabled ------ --------      -----------   ----    -----------  
    ----------- ----------   1    admin                       Logical        
               Y           N Restricted Administrator : ANewGroup :   1    
    admin                       Logical                   Y           N   2  
      user                        Logical                   N           N 
    DomainGroup :   1    admin                       Logical                 
      Y           N   2    user                        Logical               
        N           N
    
RELATED LINKS
    Add-PWGroupMember

## Get-PWLookup


NAME
    Get-PWLookup
    
SYNOPSIS
    Retrieves ProjectWise Lookup registrations.
    
    
SYNTAX
    Get-PWLookup [-LookupName] <String> [<CommonParameters>]
    
    Get-PWLookup [-All] [-EnvironmentTable] [-StandardLookupTable] 
    [-DmsBuiltIn] [<CommonParameters>]
    
    
DESCRIPTION
    The Get-PWLookup cmdlet retrieves ProjectWise Lookup registrations from 
    the datasource.
    
    When called without a LookupName, it lists all available lookups showing 
    their name and type. You can optionally filter the list by type using 
    the -All, -EnvironmentTable, -StandardLookupTable, or -DmsBuiltIn 
    switches. Only one type filter switch can be specified at a time.
    
    When called with the -LookupName parameter, it returns the full 
    registration details of a specific lookup. For Standard Lookup Table and 
    Environment Table types, this includes columns, default value and 
    description columns, sort columns, and partition filter. For Environment 
    Table types, the environment name is also returned. For DMS Built-In 
    types, only the name and type are returned.
    

PARAMETERS
    -LookupName <String>
        The name of the lookup to retrieve. If not specified, lists all 
        available lookups.
        
        Required?                    true
        Position?                    0
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -All [<SwitchParameter>]
        Lists all lookups regardless of type. This is the default behavior 
        when no type filter switch is specified.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    named
        Default value                False
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -EnvironmentTable [<SwitchParameter>]
        Lists only lookups of type "Environment Table".
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    named
        Default value                False
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -StandardLookupTable [<SwitchParameter>]
        Lists only lookups of type "Standard Lookup Table".
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    named
        Default value                False
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -DmsBuiltIn [<SwitchParameter>]
        Lists only lookups of type "DMS Built-In".
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    named
        Default value                False
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    <CommonParameters>
        This cmdlet supports the common parameters: Verbose, Debug,
        ErrorAction, ErrorVariable, WarningAction, WarningVariable,
        OutBuffer, PipelineVariable, and OutVariable. For more information, 
    see 
        about_CommonParameters 
    (https:/go.microsoft.com/fwlink/?LinkID=113216). 
    
INPUTS
    
OUTPUTS
    
NOTES
    
    
        Only one type filter switch (-All, -EnvironmentTable, 
        -StandardLookupTable, -DmsBuiltIn) can be specified at a time.
    
    ----------------- Example 1: List all lookups -----------------
    
    PS C:\> Get-PWLookup
    
    Returns a list of all available lookups with their name and type.
    ---------- Example 2: List only DMS Built-In lookups ----------
    
    PS C:\> Get-PWLookup -DmsBuiltIn
    
    Returns only lookups of type "DMS Built-In".
    ------ Example 3: List only Standard Lookup Table lookups ------
    
    PS C:\> Get-PWLookup -StandardLookupTable
    
    Returns only lookups of type "Standard Lookup Table".
    -------- Example 4: List only Environment Table lookups --------
    
    PS C:\> Get-PWLookup -EnvironmentTable
    
    Returns only lookups of type "Environment Table".
    ---- Example 5: Get a specific standard lookup registration ----
    
    PS C:\> Get-PWLookup -LookupName "my.towns"
    
    Returns the full registration details of the specified standard lookup, 
    including columns, default value column, default description column, 
    sort columns, and partition filter.
    -- Example 6: Get a specific environment lookup registration --
    
    PS C:\> Get-PWLookup -LookupName "LKP.Status"
    
    Returns the full registration details of the specified environment 
    lookup, including environment name, columns, default value column, 
    default description column, sort columns, and partition filter.
    -------- Example 7: Get a specific DMS Built-In lookup --------
    
    PS C:\> Get-PWLookup -LookupName "DMS.UserGroups"
    
    Returns the name and type of the specified DMS Built-In lookup.
    
RELATED LINKS





--

## Get-PWLookupData


NAME
    Get-PWLookupData
    
SYNOPSIS
    Retrieves data rows from a ProjectWise Standard Lookup Table.
    
    
SYNTAX
    Get-PWLookupData [-LookupName] <String> [<CommonParameters>]
    
    
DESCRIPTION
    The Get-PWLookupData cmdlet retrieves data rows from a ProjectWise 
    Standard Lookup Table.
    
    It reads the lookup registration to determine the columns, then fetches 
    all data rows from the lookup table. Each row is returned as a PSObject 
    with properties matching the registered column names.
    
    This cmdlet works only with Standard Lookup Tables. It is not applicable 
    for DMS Built-In or Environment Table lookups.
    
    The output can be piped to Set-PWLookupData to copy data between 
    lookups, or piped through Where-Object to filter data before piping to 
    Set-PWLookupData.
    

PARAMETERS
    -LookupName <String>
        The name of the standard lookup to retrieve data from.
        
        Required?                    true
        Position?                    0
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    <CommonParameters>
        This cmdlet supports the common parameters: Verbose, Debug,
        ErrorAction, ErrorVariable, WarningAction, WarningVariable,
        OutBuffer, PipelineVariable, and OutVariable. For more information, 
    see 
        about_CommonParameters 
    (https:/go.microsoft.com/fwlink/?LinkID=113216). 
    
INPUTS
    
OUTPUTS
    System.Management.Automation.PSObject
        Each row is returned as a PSObject with properties matching the 
        registered column names of the lookup. If the lookup has no data, no 
        output is returned.
    
    
NOTES
    
    
        This cmdlet is only applicable for Standard Lookup Tables. Using it 
        with DMS Built-In or Environment Table lookups will result in an 
        error.
    
    -------- Example 1: Get all data from a standard lookup --------
    
    PS C:\> Get-PWLookupData -LookupName "my.towns"
    
    Returns all data rows from the specified standard lookup table, with 
    columns matching the registered column names.
    ------- Example 2: Copy data from one lookup to another -------
    
    PS C:\> Get-PWLookupData -LookupName "source.lookup" | Set-PWLookupData 
    -LookupName "target.lookup"
    
    Retrieves all data from the source lookup and pipes it to 
    Set-PWLookupData to populate the target lookup.
    ------- Example 3: Copy filtered data to another lookup -------
    
    PS C:\> Get-PWLookupData -LookupName "source.lookup" | Where-Object { 
    $_.status -eq 'Active' } | Set-PWLookupData -LookupName "target.lookup"
    
    Retrieves data from the source lookup, filters to only rows where the 
    status column equals "Active", and pipes the filtered results to the 
    target lookup.
    
RELATED LINKS





--

## Get-PWSavedSearches


NAME
    Get-PWSavedSearches
    
SYNOPSIS
    Retrieves saved searches from within the current datasource.
    
    
SYNTAX
    Get-PWSavedSearches [[-InputFolder] <ProjectWiseFolder[]>] [[-Personal]] 
    [<CommonParameters>]
    
    Get-PWSavedSearches [[-FolderPath] <string>] [[-Personal]] 
    [<CommonParameters>]
    
    
DESCRIPTION
    Retrieves saved searches from the current ProjectWise datasource. By 
    default, it returns global saved searches; use Personal for your 
    personal saved searches.
    
    Provide a Work Area folder or path to limit results to that Work Area. 
    Omit the folder selection to return datasource-wide searches.
    

PARAMETERS
    -InputFolder <ProjectWiseFolder[]>
        ProjectWise Folder (Work Area) object(s) to search for saved 
        searches.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    0
        Default value                PWPS_DAB.CommonTypes+ProjectWiseFolder[]
        Accept pipeline input?       True (ByValue)
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -FolderPath <string>
        Folder path to Work Area to search for saved searches.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    0
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -Personal [<SwitchParameter>]
        Set this to return the current user's personal searches, otherwise 
        global searches will be returned for the datasource or for the 
        project if one is defined.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    1
        Default value                False
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    <CommonParameters>
        This cmdlet supports the common parameters: Verbose, Debug,
        ErrorAction, ErrorVariable, WarningAction, WarningVariable,
        OutBuffer, PipelineVariable, and OutVariable. For more information, 
    see 
        about_CommonParameters 
    (https:/go.microsoft.com/fwlink/?LinkID=113216). 
    
INPUTS
    PWPS_DAB.CommonTypes+ProjectWiseFolder[]
        ProjectWise Folder (Work Area) object(s) to search for saved 
        searches.
    
    
OUTPUTS
    
NOTES
    
    
        
    
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 1 --------------------------
    
    Get-PWSavedSearches
    
    
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 2 --------------------------
    
    Get-PWSavedSearches -Personal
    
    
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 3 --------------------------
    
    Get-PWSavedSearches -FolderPath 'workArea1\workarea2'
    
    
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 4 --------------------------
    
    Get-PWSavedSearches -InputFolder (Get-PWFolderByURN -URN "FOLDER_URN") 
    -Personal

## Get-PWStorage


NAME
    Get-PWStorage
    
SYNOPSIS
    Gets the Storage Areas in the current ProjectWise Datasource.
    
    
SYNTAX
    Get-PWStorage [-Name <String>] [<CommonParameters>]
    
    Get-PWStorage [-ProjectID <Int32>] [<CommonParameters>]
    
    Get-PWStorage [-StorageID <Int32>] [<CommonParameters>]
    
    
DESCRIPTION
    The Get-PWStorage cmdlet gets the Storage Areas in the current 
    ProjectWise Datasource.
    
    By default, Get-PWStorage lists all Storage Areas in the Datasource.  
    When you use the StorageID parameter, Get-PWStorage searches for a 
    specific Storage Area.  When you use the ProjectID parameter, 
    Get-PWStorage returns all Storage Areas available for the specified 
    project.
    

PARAMETERS
    -Name <String>
        Retrieves only Storage Areas that match this Name. Some wildcards 
        (*, ?) are allowed.
        
        The two wildcard characters supported are:  * (matches zero or more 
        characters, starting at the specified position)  ? (matches any 
        character at the specified position)
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    named
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -ProjectID <Int32>
        Retrieves all Storage Areas available for the project with this 
        ProjectID.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    named
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       True (ByPropertyName)
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -StorageID <Int32>
        Retrieves the Storage Area with this StorageID, if it exists.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    named
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       True (ByPropertyName)
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    <CommonParameters>
        This cmdlet supports the common parameters: Verbose, Debug,
        ErrorAction, ErrorVariable, WarningAction, WarningVariable,
        OutBuffer, PipelineVariable, and OutVariable. For more information, 
    see 
        about_CommonParameters 
    (https:/go.microsoft.com/fwlink/?LinkID=113216). 
    
INPUTS
    None
        You cannot pipe objects to this cmdlet.
    
    
OUTPUTS
    Bentley.ProjectWise.PowerShell.Common.Storage
        Get-PWStorage returns an object that represents each Storage Area.
    
    
NOTES
    
    
        Get-PWStorage requires an open ProjectWise Connection in the current 
        thread.  You can open a Connection with Open-PWConnection before 
        calling Get-PWStorage.
    
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 1 --------------------------
    
    Get-PWStorage
    
    Get all ProjectWise Storage Areas
    
    StorageID Name              Description       Host             Path 
    --------- ----              -----------       ----             ----     
    1     New Storage Name  This parameter... PWHOST           
    C:\ProjectWis...     2     New Storage Na...  PWHOST           
    C:\ProjectWis...     3     Storage1          Storage1          PWHOST    
           C:\Storage1     4     Storage2          Storage2          PWHOST  
             C:\Storage2
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 2 --------------------------
    
    Get-PWStorage -StorageID 1
    
    Get a Storage Area by StorageID
    
    StorageID Name              Description       Host             Path 
    --------- ----              -----------       ----             ----     
    1     New Storage Name  This parameter... PWHOST           
    C:\ProjectWis...
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 3 --------------------------
    
    Get-PWStorage -Name new?storage*
    
    Get Storage Areas by Name (note that wildcards are supported)
    
    StorageID Name              Description       Host             Path 
    --------- ----              -----------       ----             ----     
    1     New Storage Name  This parameter... PWHOST           
    C:\ProjectWis...     2     New Storage Na...  PWHOST           
    C:\ProjectWis...
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 4 --------------------------
    
    Get-PWStorage -ProjectID 1
    
    Get Storage Areas available for a Project (by ProjectID)
    
    StorageID Name              Description       Host             Path 
    --------- ----              -----------       ----             ----     
    1     New Storage Name  This parameter... PWHOST           
    C:\ProjectWis...     2     New Storage Na...  PWHOST           
    C:\ProjectWis...     3     Storage1          Storage1          PWHOST    
           C:\Storage1     4     Storage2          Storage2          PWHOST  
             C:\Storage2
    
RELATED LINKS
    New-PWStorage

## Get-PWUser


NAME
    Get-PWUser
    
SYNOPSIS
    Gets the Users in the current ProjectWise Datasource.
    
    
SYNTAX
    Get-PWUser -CurrentUser [-ShowLastLoginDate] [-IncludeIdentities] 
    [<CommonParameters>]
    
    Get-PWUser [[-UserName] <String>] [[-EMail] <String>] [[-Description] 
    <String>] [-ShowLastLoginDate] [-IncludeIdentities] [<CommonParameters>]
    
    Get-PWUser [-ShowLastLoginDate] [-IncludeIdentities] [-UserID <Int32>] 
    [<CommonParameters>]
    
    
DESCRIPTION
    The Get-PWUser cmdlet gets the Users in the current ProjectWise 
    Datasource.
    
    By default, Get-PWUser lists all Users in the Datasource. When you use 
    the UserID parameter, Get-PWUser searches for a specific User. When you 
    use the UserName, Email, or Description parameters, Get-PWUser searches 
    and only returns Users that match the given parameter(s).
    

PARAMETERS
    -CurrentUser [<SwitchParameter>]
        Retrieves the currently logged in User.
        
        Required?                    true
        Position?                    named
        Default value                False
        Accept pipeline input?       True (ByPropertyName)
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -Description <String>
        Retrieves only users that match this Description. Some wildcards (*, 
        ?) are allowed.
        
        The two wildcard characters supported are:  * (matches zero or more 
        characters, starting at the specified position)  ? (matches any 
        character at the specified position)
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    2
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -EMail <String>
        Retrieves only users that match this Email address. Some wildcards 
        (*, ?) are allowed.
        
        The two wildcard characters supported are:  * (matches zero or more 
        characters, starting at the specified position)  ? (matches any 
        character at the specified position)
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    1
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -ShowLastLoginDate [<SwitchParameter>]
        Shows the last login date for each retrieved User.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    named
        Default value                False
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -IncludeIdentities [<SwitchParameter>]
        Returned user information will include identities.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    named
        Default value                False
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -UserID <Int32>
        Retrieves the User with the given UserID, if it exists.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    named
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       True (ByPropertyName)
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -UserName <String>
        Retrieves only users that match this UserName. Some wildcards (*, ?) 
        are allowed.
        
        The two wildcard characters supported are:  * (matches zero or more 
        characters, starting at the specified position)  ? (matches any 
        character at the specified position)
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    0
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    <CommonParameters>
        This cmdlet supports the common parameters: Verbose, Debug,
        ErrorAction, ErrorVariable, WarningAction, WarningVariable,
        OutBuffer, PipelineVariable, and OutVariable. For more information, 
    see 
        about_CommonParameters 
    (https:/go.microsoft.com/fwlink/?LinkID=113216). 
    
INPUTS
    None
        You cannot pipe objects to this cmdlet.
    
    
OUTPUTS
    Bentley.ProjectWise.PowerShell.Common.User
        Get-PWUser returns an object that represents each User.
    
    
NOTES
    
    
        Get-PWUser requires an open ProjectWise Connection in the current 
        thread.  You can open a Connection with Open-PWConnection before 
        calling Get-PWUser.
        
        Get-PWUser will work without an Admin session, but some information 
        in the User objects can only be retrieved in an Admin session.  
        These fields are simply set to null for a non-Admin session.
    
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 1 --------------------------
    
    Get-PWUser
    
    UserID UserName      Description               Type     SecProvider  
    IsConnected IsDisabled
    ------ --------      -----------               ----     -----------  
    ----------- ----------
      1    admin         An admin user             Logical                   
     Y           N
      2    Domain.User   Windows domain user       Windows  DOMAIN           
     N           N
      3    Synced.User   Synchronized domain user  WinSync  DOMAIN           
     Y           N
      4    disabledUser  A disabled user           Logical                   
     N           Y
      5    IMS.User      Bentley IMS user          STS                       
     N           N
    
    Description
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 2 --------------------------
    
    Get-PWUser -UserID 3
    
    UserID UserName      Description               Type     SecProvider  
    IsConnected IsDisabled
    ------ --------      -----------               ----     -----------  
    ----------- ----------
      3    Synced.User   Synchronized domain user  WinSync  DOMAIN           
     Y           N
    
    Description
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 3 --------------------------
    
    Get-PWUser -UserName d*Us?r
    
    UserID UserName      Description           Type     SecProvider  
    IsConnected IsDisabled
    ------ --------      -----------           ----     -----------  
    ----------- ----------
      2    Domain.User   Windows domain user   Windows  DOMAIN            N  
             N
      4    disabledUser  A disabled user       Logical                    N  
             Y
    
    Description
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 4 --------------------------
    
    Get-PWUser -Description "A* uSe?"
    
    UserID UserName      Description           Type     SecProvider  
    IsConnected IsDisabled
    ------ --------      -----------           ----     -----------  
    ----------- ----------
      1    admin         An admin user         Logical                    Y  
             N
      4    disabledUser  A disabled user       Logical                    N  
             Y
    
    Description
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 5 --------------------------
    
    Get-PWUser -EMail aDmin@*.c?m
    
    UserID UserName      Description           Type     SecProvider  
    IsConnected IsDisabled
    ------ --------      -----------           ----     -----------  
    ----------- ----------
      1    admin         An admin user         Logical                    Y  
             N
    
    Description
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 6 --------------------------
    
    Get-PWUser -UserName *u?er -Description *uS?r
    
    UserID UserName      Description               Type     SecProvider  
    IsConnected IsDisabled
    ------ --------      -----------               ----     -----------  
    ----------- ----------
      3    Synced.User   Synchronized domain user  WinSync  DOMAIN           
     Y           N
      4    disabledUser  A disabled user           Logical                   
     N           Y
    
    Description
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 7 --------------------------
    
    Get-PWUser | Where-Object { $_.IsConnected }
    
    UserID UserName      Description               Type     SecProvider  
    IsConnected IsDisabled
    ------ --------      -----------               ----     -----------  
    ----------- ----------
      1    admin         An admin user             Logical                   
     Y           N
      3    Synced.User   Synchronized domain user  WinSync  DOMAIN           
     Y           N
    
    Description
    
RELATED LINKS

## Get-PWUserAuditTrailRecords


NAME
    Get-PWUserAuditTrailRecords
    
SYNOPSIS
    Reports on user activity from the audit trail, including actions such as 
    login, logout, user creation, modification, and other user-related 
    events.
    
    
SYNTAX
    Get-PWUserAuditTrailRecords [[-Users] <User[]>] [-StartDate] <DateTime> 
    [-EndDate] <DateTime> [<CommonParameters>]
    
    
DESCRIPTION
    Get-PWUserAuditTrailRecords retrieves user-related audit trail records 
    from the ProjectWise datasource. The cmdlet returns records for user 
    actions such as login, logout, user creation, modification, deletion, 
    renaming, enabling, disabling, and settings changes (action codes 
    3001-3009).
    
    By default, all user audit trail records are returned. Use the -Users 
    parameter to filter by specific users who performed the actions. The 
    -StartDate and -EndDate parameters are required to define the date range 
    for retrieval.
    
    When only a date is provided without a time component for -EndDate 
    (e.g., '2024-12-31'), the time is automatically set to 23:59:59 to 
    include the entire day.
    
    Results are sorted by action date in ascending order and output as a 
    DataTable.
    

PARAMETERS
    -Users <User[]>
        An optional array of User objects to filter audit trail records by. 
        Only records for actions performed by the specified users will be 
        returned.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    0
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       True (ByValue)
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -StartDate <DateTime>
        The start date for filtering audit trail records. Only records on or 
        after this date will be returned. Accepts date strings like 
        '2026-01-01' or '2026-01-01 14:30:00'.
        
        Required?                    true
        Position?                    1
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -EndDate <DateTime>
        The end date for filtering audit trail records. Only records on or 
        before this date will be returned. Accepts date strings like 
        '2026-12-31' or '2026-12-31 23:59:59'. When only a date is provided 
        without a time, the time is automatically set to 23:59:59 to include 
        the entire day.
        
        Required?                    true
        Position?                    2
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    <CommonParameters>
        This cmdlet supports the common parameters: Verbose, Debug,
        ErrorAction, ErrorVariable, WarningAction, WarningVariable,
        OutBuffer, PipelineVariable, and OutVariable. For more information, 
    see 
        about_CommonParameters 
    (https:/go.microsoft.com/fwlink/?LinkID=113216). 
    
INPUTS
    PWPS_Cloud.Common.Users.User[]
        An optional array of User objects to filter by. Pass User objects 
        from cmdlets like Get-PWUser or Get-PWUsersByMatch.
    
    
OUTPUTS
    System.Data.DataTable
        A DataTable containing user audit trail records with the following 
        columns: Action, UserName, UserDescription, UserEmail, ActionDate, 
        Comments, and ActedOn.
    
    
NOTES
    
    
        - The -StartDate and -EndDate parameters are required to prevent 
        retrieval of excessively large result sets.
        
        - The -Users parameter filters by the user who performed the action, 
        not the user who was acted on. For example, if admin creates a user 
        named TestUser, the record shows admin as UserName and TestUser as 
        ActedOn. - The cmdlet retrieves records for the following user 
        actions: User Login (3001), User Logout (3002), User Create (3003), 
        User Modified (3004), Change User Settings (3005), User Renamed 
        (3006), User Deleted (3007), User Disabled (3008), User Enabled 
        (3009).
        
        - Results are sorted by ActionDate in ascending order.
    
    - EXAMPLE 1: Get all user audit trail records for a date range -
    
    PS C:\> Get-PWUserAuditTrailRecords -StartDate '2026-01-01' -EndDate 
    '2026-12-31'
    
    Returns all user audit trail records from the current datasource for the 
    year 2026.
    ---- EXAMPLE 2: Get audit trail records for a specific user ----
    
    PS C:\> $user = Get-PWUser -UserName 'admin'
    PS C:\> Get-PWUserAuditTrailRecords -Users $user -StartDate '2026-01-01' 
    -EndDate '2026-12-31'
    
    Returns user audit trail records for the admin user for the year 2026.
    ---- EXAMPLE 3: Get audit trail records within a date range ----
    
    PS C:\> Get-PWUserAuditTrailRecords -StartDate '2026-01-01' -EndDate 
    '2026-03-31'
    
    Returns all user audit trail records between January 1, 2026 and March 
    31, 2026.
    EXAMPLE 4: Get audit trail records for a user within a specific date 
    range
    
    PS C:\> $user = Get-PWUser -UserName 'admin'
    PS C:\> Get-PWUserAuditTrailRecords -Users $user -StartDate '2026-03-01' 
    -EndDate '2026-03-31'
    
    Returns user audit trail records for the admin user for March 2026.
    ------------- EXAMPLE 5: Pipeline to Excel export -------------
    
    PS C:\> $user = Get-PWUser -UserName 'admin'
    PS C:\> Get-PWUserAuditTrailRecords -Users $user -StartDate '2026-01-01' 
    -EndDate '2026-12-31' | New-XLSXWorkbook -OutputFileName 
    C:\temp\useraudittrail.xlsx
    
    Exports user audit trail records for the admin user to an Excel workbook.
    ----- EXAMPLE 6: Get audit trail records for a single day -----
    
    PS C:\> Get-PWUserAuditTrailRecords -StartDate '2026-03-10' -EndDate 
    '2026-03-10'
    
    Returns all user audit trail records for March 10, 2026. When only a 
    date is provided for -EndDate, the time is automatically set to 23:59:59 
    to include the entire day.
    
RELATED LINKS





--

SYNOPSIS
    Gets the User Dynamic Creation Filter of the current ProjectWise

## Get-PWUserIdentity


NAME
    Get-PWUserIdentity
    
SYNOPSIS
    Retrieves Federated User Identities from the current ProjectWise 
    Datasource.
    
    
SYNTAX
    Get-PWUserIdentity [[-UserID] <Int32>] [[-UserName] <String>] 
    [[-ProviderID] <Int32>] [[-ProviderName] <String>] [[-Name] <String>] 
    [[-ProviderProtocol] <String>] [<CommonParameters>]
    
    
DESCRIPTION
    The Get-PWUserIdentity cmdlet retrieves Federated User Identities 
    according to specified parameters from the current ProjectWise 
    Datasource.
    

PARAMETERS
    -Name <String>
        The Name aka Identity of the Federated Identity.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    2
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       True (ByPropertyName)
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -ProviderID <Int32>
        The numerical identifier of the Identity Provider the Federated 
        Identity belongs to.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    1
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       True (ByPropertyName)
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -ProviderName <String>
        The name/identity/URL of the Identity Provider the Federated 
        Identity belongs to. It can be accompanied by ProviderProtocol 
        parameter to distinguish between multiple providers served by same 
        service.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    1
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -ProviderProtocol <String>
        The protocol/type of the Identity Provider as string. Currently 
        supported options are "STS" and "OIDC".
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    3
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -UserID <Int32>
        The numerical identifier of the ProjectWise user the Federated 
        Identity is assigned to.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    0
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       True (ByPropertyName)
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -UserName <String>
        The User Name of the ProjectWise user the Federated Identity is 
        assigned to.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    0
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    <CommonParameters>
        This cmdlet supports the common parameters: Verbose, Debug,
        ErrorAction, ErrorVariable, WarningAction, WarningVariable,
        OutBuffer, PipelineVariable, and OutVariable. For more information, 
    see 
        about_CommonParameters 
    (https:/go.microsoft.com/fwlink/?LinkID=113216). 
    
INPUTS
    Bentley.ProjectWise.PowerShell.Common.UserIdentity
        You can pipe UserIdentity objects to Add-PWUserIdentity.
    
    
OUTPUTS
    Bentley.ProjectWise.PowerShell.Common.UserIdentity
        Get-PWUserIdentity returns objects representing the Federated User 
        Identities which match specified parameters.
    
    
NOTES
    
    
        Get-PWUserIdentity requires an open ProjectWise Connection in the 
        current thread.  You can open a Connection with Open-PWConnection 
        before calling Get-PWUserIdentity.
        
        Get-PWUserIdentity requires an Admin session.  To open an admin 
        session, use the -Admin flag when opening the ProjectWise Connection 
        with Open-PWConnection.
    
    --------------------------- EXAMPLE1 ---------------------------
    
    Get-PWUserIdentity -UserName "user"
    
    Retrieves Federated User Identities for ProjectWise user with username 
    "user".
    
    UserID Name                   ProviderID ------ ----                   
    ----------      3 John Doe                        4      3 
    user@bentley.com               11
    --------------------------- EXAMPLE2 ---------------------------
    
    Get-PWUserIdentity -ProviderProtocol OIDC
    
    Retrieves Federated Identities which belong to Identity Providers of 
    OIDC type.
    
    UserID Name                               ProviderID ------ ----         
                          ----------      3 John Doe                         
               4     19 jane.doe@bentley.com                        1     19 
    jane.doe@bentley.com                       11      3 user@bentley.com    
                           11
    
RELATED LINKS
    Add-PWUserIdentity 
    Add-PWUserIdentityProvider 
    Get-PWUserIdentityProvider 
    Remove-PWUserIdentity 
    Remove-PWUserIdentityProvider 
    Update-PWUserIdentityProvider

    [[-Protocol] <String>] [<CommonParameters>]

    Identities Providers according to specified parameters from the current 
    ProjectWise Datasource.
    

PARAMETERS

        The numerical identifier of the Identity Provider.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    0
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       True (ByPropertyName)
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -Name <String>
        The name/identity/URL of the Identity Provider. It can be 
        accompanied by Protocol parameter to distinguish between multiple 
        providers served by same service.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    0
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       True (ByPropertyName)
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -Protocol <String>
        The protocol/type of the Identity Provider as string. Currently 
        supported options are "STS" and "OIDC".
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    1
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       True (ByPropertyName)
        Accept wildcard characters?  false

        Get-PWUserIdentity returns objects representing the Federated User

## Get-PWUserList


NAME
    Get-PWUserList
    
SYNOPSIS
    Gets the User Lists in the current ProjectWise Datasource.
    
    
SYNTAX
    Get-PWUserList [-Exclude] -GroupID <Int32> [<CommonParameters>]
    
    Get-PWUserList [-Exclude] -UserID <Int32> [<CommonParameters>]
    
    Get-PWUserList [-Owner <User>] [<CommonParameters>]
    
    Get-PWUserList [[-UserListID] <Int32>] [<CommonParameters>]
    
    Get-PWUserList [[-UserListName] <String>] [<CommonParameters>]
    
    
DESCRIPTION
    The Get-PWUserList cmdlet gets the User Lists in the current ProjectWise 
    Datasource.
    
    By default, Get-PWUserList lists all Users in the Datasource. When you 
    use the UserListID parameter, Get-PWUserList searches for a specific 
    UserList. When you use the UserListName parameter, Get-PWUserList 
    searches and only returns UserLists that match the given UserListName.
    
    When you use the UserID or GroupID parameters, Get-PWUserList searches 
    and only returns UserLists that have the given User or Group as a member.
    

PARAMETERS
    -Exclude [<SwitchParameter>]
        If set, retrieves only User Lists that do NOT have the User or Group 
        with the given UserID or GroupID as a member.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    named
        Default value                False
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -GroupID <Int32>
        Retrieves only User Lists that have the Group with this ID as a 
        member, unless -Exclude is set, in which case Get-PWUserList will 
        return only User Lists that do NOT have the Group with this ID as a 
        member.
        
        Required?                    true
        Position?                    named
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -Owner <User>
        Retrieves only User Lists that are owned by this User.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    named
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       True (ByValue)
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -UserID <Int32>
        Retrieves only User Lists that have the User with this ID as a 
        member, unless -Exclude is set, in which case Get-PWUserList will 
        return only User Lists that do NOT have the User with this ID as a 
        member.
        
        Required?                    true
        Position?                    named
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -UserListID <Int32>
        Retrieves the User List with the given UserListID, if it exists.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    0
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       True (ByPropertyName)
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -UserListName <String>
        Retrieves only User Lists that match this UserListName. Some 
        wildcards (*, ?) are allowed.
        
        The two wildcard characters supported are:  * (matches zero or more 
        characters, starting at the specified position)  ? (matches any 
        character at the specified position).
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    0
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    <CommonParameters>
        This cmdlet supports the common parameters: Verbose, Debug,
        ErrorAction, ErrorVariable, WarningAction, WarningVariable,
        OutBuffer, PipelineVariable, and OutVariable. For more information, 
    see 
        about_CommonParameters 
    (https:/go.microsoft.com/fwlink/?LinkID=113216). 
    
INPUTS
    Bentley.ProjectWise.PowerShell.Common.User
        You can pipe a User object to Get-PWUserList to search for UserLists 
        owned by a particular User.
    
    
OUTPUTS
    Bentley.ProjectWise.PowerShell.Common.UserList
        Get-PWUserList returns an object that represents each User List.
    
    
NOTES
    
    
        Get-PWUserList requires an open ProjectWise Connection in the 
        current thread.  You can open a Connection with Open-PWConnection 
        before calling Get-PWUserList.
    
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 1 --------------------------
    
    Get-PWUserList
    
    Get all ProjectWise User Lists
    
    UserListID UserListName       Description       Type          Owner 
    ---------- ------------       -----------       ----          -----     
    1      New Access List    This parameter... AccessControl [global]     2 
         New Global Mail... This parameter... AddressBook   [global]     3   
       List With Owner                      AddressBook   admin
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 2 --------------------------
    
    Get-PWUserList -UserListID 1
    
    Get a UserList by UserListID
    
    UserListID UserListName       Description       Type          Owner 
    ---------- ------------       -----------       ----          -----     
    1      New Access List    This parameter... AccessControl [global]
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 3 --------------------------
    
    Get-PWUserList -UserListName New*Lis?
    
    Get UserLists by UserListName (note that wildcards are supported)
    
    UserListID UserListName       Description       Type          Owner 
    ---------- ------------       -----------       ----          -----     
    1      New Access List    This parameter... AccessControl [global]     2 
         New Global Mail... This parameter... AddressBook   [global]
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 4 --------------------------
    
    Get-PWUserList -UserID 1
    
    Get UserLists that a User (by UserID) is in
    
    UserListID UserListName       Description       Type          Owner 
    ---------- ------------       -----------       ----          -----     
    1      New Access List    This parameter... AccessControl [global]
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 5 --------------------------
    
    Get-PWUserList -UserID 1 -Exclude
    
    Get UserLists that a User (by UserID) is NOT in
    
    UserListID UserListName       Description       Type          Owner 
    ---------- ------------       -----------       ----          -----     
    2      New Global Mail... This parameter... AddressBook   [global]     3 
         List With Owner                      AddressBook   admin
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 6 --------------------------
    
    Get-PWUserList -GroupID 1
    
    Get UserLists that a Group (by GroupID) is in
    
    UserListID UserListName       Description       Type          Owner 
    ---------- ------------       -----------       ----          -----     
    2      New Global Mail... This parameter... AddressBook   [global]
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 7 --------------------------
    
    Get-PWUserList -GroupID 3 -Exclude
    
    Get UserLists that a Group (by GroupID) is NOT in
    
    UserListID UserListName       Description       Type          Owner 
    ---------- ------------       -----------       ----          -----     
    1      New Access List    This parameter... AccessControl [global]     3 
         List With Owner                      AddressBook   admin
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 8 --------------------------
    
    Get-PWUser -UserName admin | Get-PWUserList
    
    Get UserLists that a User (from the pipeline) is owner of
    
    UserListID UserListName       Description       Type          Owner 
    ---------- ------------       -----------       ----          -----     
    3      List With Owner                      AddressBook   admin
    
RELATED LINKS
    New-PWUserList 
    Remove-PWUserList 
    Update-PWUserList

## Get-PWUserListMember


NAME
    Get-PWUserListMember
    
SYNOPSIS
    Gets all Members of a given User List in the current ProjectWise 
    Datasource.
    
    
SYNTAX
    Get-PWUserListMember [-UserListID] <Int32> [<CommonParameters>]
    
    
DESCRIPTION
    The Get-PWUserListMember cmdlet gets all Users, Groups, and UserLists 
    that are currently members of a given User List in the current 
    ProjectWise Datasource.
    
    User Lists can be selected by ID.
    

PARAMETERS
    -UserListID <Int32>
        Retrieves all Users, Groups, and UserLists in the User List with the 
        given UserListID, if it exists.
        
        Required?                    true
        Position?                    0
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       True (ByPropertyName)
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    <CommonParameters>
        This cmdlet supports the common parameters: Verbose, Debug,
        ErrorAction, ErrorVariable, WarningAction, WarningVariable,
        OutBuffer, PipelineVariable, and OutVariable. For more information, 
    see 
        about_CommonParameters 
    (https:/go.microsoft.com/fwlink/?LinkID=113216). 
    
INPUTS
    Bentley.ProjectWise.PowerShell.Common.UserList
        You can optionally pipe UserList objects to this cmdlet.
    
    
OUTPUTS
    Bentley.ProjectWise.PowerShell.Common.UserListMember
        Get-PWUserListMember returns an object that represents each User 
        List Member. To access the actual User, Group, and UserList objects, 
        use the MemberObject property of the the returned UserListMembers.
    
    
NOTES
    
    
        Get-PWUserListMember requires an open ProjectWise Connection in the 
        current thread.  You can open a Connection with Open-PWConnection 
        before calling Get-PWUserListMember.
        
        Get-PWUserListMember will work without an Admin session, but some 
        information in the User objects can only be retrieved in an Admin 
        session.  These fields are simply set to null for a non-Admin 
        session.
    
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 1 --------------------------
    
    Get-PWUserListMember -UserListID 1
    
    Get all the members of a UserList (by UserListID)
    
RELATED LINKS
    Add-PWUserListMember 
    Get-PWUserList 
    New-PWUserList 
    Remove-PWUserListMember 
    Update-PWUserList

## Get-PWUserSetting


NAME
    Get-PWUserSetting
    
SYNOPSIS
    Gets the user settings of a specified User.
    
    
SYNTAX
    Get-PWUserSetting -Default [-UserSettingCategory <UserSetting+Category>] 
    [<CommonParameters>]
    
    Get-PWUserSetting [-UserID <Int32>] [-UserSettingCategory 
    <UserSetting+Category>] [<CommonParameters>]
    
    
DESCRIPTION
    The Get-PWUserSetting cmdlet gets the settings of a specified User or 
    the datasource's default User settings.
    

PARAMETERS
    -Default [<SwitchParameter>]
        Retrieve default setting value.
        
        Required?                    true
        Position?                    named
        Default value                False
        Accept pipeline input?       True (ByPropertyName)
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -UserID <Int32>
        The UserID of the user whose settings should be retrieved.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    named
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       True (ByPropertyName)
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -UserSettingCategory <UserSetting+Category>
        The category (group) of the setting to retrieve.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    named
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       True (ByPropertyName)
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    <CommonParameters>
        This cmdlet supports the common parameters: Verbose, Debug,
        ErrorAction, ErrorVariable, WarningAction, WarningVariable,
        OutBuffer, PipelineVariable, and OutVariable. For more information, 
    see 
        about_CommonParameters 
    (https:/go.microsoft.com/fwlink/?LinkID=113216). 
    
INPUTS
    
OUTPUTS
    Bentley.ProjectWise.PowerShell.Common.UserSetting
        
    
    
NOTES
    
    
        This cmdlet requires an open administrative ProjectWise Connection 
        in the current thread. You can open an administrative Connection 
        using the Open-PWConnection cmdlet and setting the -Admin flag.
    
    -------------------------- Example 1 --------------------------
    
    Get-PWUserSetting -UserID 2
    
    Gets all user (with UserID = 2) settings.
    -------------------------- Example 2 --------------------------
    
    Get-PWUserSetting -Default
    
    Gets default user settings of the active datasource.
    -------------------------- Example 3 --------------------------
    
    Get-PWUserSetting -UserID 2 | Export-Csv "c:\userSettings.txt"
    
    Export all user (with ID = 2) settings to a file.
    -------------------------- Example 4 --------------------------
    
    Get-PWUserSetting -UserID 2 -UserSettingCategory UI
    
    Gets all user (with UserID = 2) settings of the UserInterface category.
    -------------------------- Example 5 --------------------------
    
    Get-PWUserSetting -UserID 2 -UserSettingCategory UI -UserSettingName 
    CanEditSets
    
    Gets the "CanEditSets" setting value of the user with UserID = 2.
    
RELATED LINKS
    Set-PWUserSetting

## Get-PWUserWindowsDomain


NAME
    Get-PWUserWindowsDomain
    
SYNOPSIS
    Retrieves Windows Domains according to specified parameters from the 
    current ProjectWise Datasource.
    
    
SYNTAX
    Get-PWUserWindowsDomain [[-ID] <Int32>] [[-Name] <String>] 
    [<CommonParameters>]
    
    
DESCRIPTION
    The Get-PWUserWindowsDomain cmdlet retrieves Windows Domain record(s) 
    according to specified parameters from the current ProjectWise 
    Datasource.
    

PARAMETERS
    -ID <Int32>
        The numerical identifier of the Windows Domain.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    0
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       True (ByPropertyName)
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -Name <String>
        The name of the Windows Domain.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    0
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       True (ByPropertyName)
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    <CommonParameters>
        This cmdlet supports the common parameters: Verbose, Debug,
        ErrorAction, ErrorVariable, WarningAction, WarningVariable,
        OutBuffer, PipelineVariable, and OutVariable. For more information, 
    see 
        about_CommonParameters 
    (https:/go.microsoft.com/fwlink/?LinkID=113216). 
    
INPUTS
    Bentley.ProjectWise.PowerShell.Common.WindowsDomain
        You can pipe WindowsDomain objects to Get-PWUserWindowsDomain.
    
    
OUTPUTS
    Bentley.ProjectWise.PowerShell.Common.WindowsDomain
        Get-PWUserWindowsDomain returns objects representing the Windows 
        Domains which match specified parameters.
    
    
NOTES
    
    
        Get-PWUserWindowsDomain requires an open ProjectWise Connection in 
        the current thread.  You can open a Connection with 
        Open-PWConnection before calling Get-PWUserWindowsDomain.
        
        Get-PWUserWindowsDomain requires an Admin session.  To open an admin 
        session, use the -Admin flag when opening the ProjectWise Connection 
        with Open-PWConnection.
