## Import-PWDocumentsBySpreadsheet


NAME
    Import-PWDocumentsBySpreadsheet
    
SYNOPSIS
    Imports documents and their attributes from a spreadsheet into 
    ProjectWise. The spreadsheet format should match the output from 
    Export-PWDocuments cmdlets. DateTime attribute values must be in the 
    format: yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss. NOTE: Does not support version stack 
    imports where the document name varies through the stack. Will handle 
    file name variation within a version stack.
    
    
SYNTAX
    Import-PWDocumentsBySpreadsheet [-InputFile] <string> 
    [[-TargetProjectWiseFolder] <string>] [[-DefaultStorage] <string>] 
    [[-DefaultWorkflow] <string>] [[-DefaultEnvironment] <string>] 
    [[-VersionIfExists]] [[-OnlyUpdateAttributes]] [[-WriteBackLinks]] 
    [[-CreateMissingFiles]] [[-Force]] [<CommonParameters>]
    
    
DESCRIPTION
    This cmdlet imports documents from a spreadsheet (XLSX format) into 
    ProjectWise, creating folder structures as needed. It supports importing 
    document files with their metadata, attributes, workflow states, and 
    version information. Spreadsheet Requirements: The spreadsheet must 
    contain a 'General' sheet with the following columns: - ProjectWisePath 
    : Full path including document name in ProjectWise (e.g., 
    "Folder1\Subfolder\Document.pdf") - FileName : Physical file name on 
    disk (can differ from document name for versions) - FileSystemPath : 
    Full path to the source file on the local file system - Description : 
    Document description - ProjectWiseDocumentVersion : Version identifier 
    string - Environment : Environment name for the document - Workflow : 
    Workflow name to assign - State : Workflow state to set after import - 
    Creator , Updater , FileUpdater : User names for audit fields - 
    CreateDate , UpdateDate , FileUpdateDate : Timestamps in yyyy-MM-dd 
    HH:mm:ss format Attribute Sheets: For attribute data, create additional 
    sheets named after the Environment name (truncated to 30 characters if 
    longer). These sheets must contain: - ProjectWisePath : Must match the 
    General sheet for document matching - ProjectWiseDocumentVersion : Must 
    match for version-specific attribute assignment - Additional columns for 
    each attribute field Key Behaviors: - If a document already exists and 
    `-VersionIfExists` is specified, a new version is created with 
    attributes copied from the previous version
    
    - If a document already exists and `-VersionIfExists` is NOT specified, 
    the existing file is overwritten
    
    - If a document does not exist, a new document is created
    
    - Folders are created automatically as needed when a valid storage area 
    is provided
    
    - Abstract documents (no physical file) are supported when 
    FileSystemPath and FileName are empty
    
    - The cmdlet outputs ProjectWiseDocument objects that can be piped to 
    other cmdlets
    

PARAMETERS
    -InputFile <string>
        Path to the Excel spreadsheet (XLSX format) containing document 
        information to import. The spreadsheet must contain a 'General' 
        sheet with document metadata. Additional sheets named after 
        Environment names can contain attribute data.
        
        Required?                    true
        Position?                    0
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -TargetProjectWiseFolder <string>
        Target parent folder in ProjectWise where documents will be 
        imported. The ProjectWisePath values from the spreadsheet are 
        appended to this path. If not specified, documents are created using 
        only the ProjectWisePath from the spreadsheet. Folders are created 
        as needed if they don't exist (requires valid DefaultStorage).
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    1
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -DefaultStorage <string>
        Storage area name for newly created folders. Required when creating 
        new folders. If importing to an existing folder, the storage is 
        inherited from that folder. If no storage can be determined and new 
        folders need to be created, the cmdlet will display a warning and 
        exit.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    2
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -DefaultWorkflow <string>
        Default workflow name to assign to newly created folders. Documents 
        inherit the workflow from their parent folder. The workflow can be 
        overridden per-document using the Workflow column in the spreadsheet.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    3
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -DefaultEnvironment <string>
        Default environment name to assign to newly created folders. The 
        environment determines which attribute schema is available for 
        documents. Can be overridden per-document using the Environment 
        column in the spreadsheet.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    4
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -VersionIfExists [<SwitchParameter>]
        When specified, creates a new version if a document with the same 
        name already exists in the target folder. The new version copies 
        attributes and preserves relations from the previous version. When 
        not specified, existing documents have their files overwritten 
        without creating a new version.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    5
        Default value                False
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -OnlyUpdateAttributes [<SwitchParameter>]
        When specified, only updates document attributes without modifying 
        or creating document files. The cmdlet locates existing documents by 
        matching the document name and version, then applies attribute 
        values from the spreadsheet. Useful for bulk metadata updates.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    6
        Default value                False
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -WriteBackLinks [<SwitchParameter>]
        When specified, writes ProjectWise URN links back to the 
        ProjectWiseURNLink column in the input spreadsheet after import. 
        Creates a reference between spreadsheet rows and imported documents. 
        Warning: Do not use with very large files as the entire spreadsheet 
        is rewritten.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    7
        Default value                False
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -CreateMissingFiles [<SwitchParameter>]
        When specified, creates placeholder files (1KB) for documents where 
        the source file specified in FileSystemPath does not exist on disk. 
        Without this switch, rows with missing source files are skipped with 
        a warning. Useful for creating document stubs.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    8
        Default value                False
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -Force [<SwitchParameter>]
        When specified, ignores any WRE restrictions for state / version 
        changes
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    9
        Default value                False
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    <CommonParameters>
        This cmdlet supports the common parameters: Verbose, Debug,
        ErrorAction, ErrorVariable, WarningAction, WarningVariable,
        OutBuffer, PipelineVariable, and OutVariable. For more information, 
    see 
        about_CommonParameters 
    (https:/go.microsoft.com/fwlink/?LinkID=113216). 
    
INPUTS
    None
        This cmdlet does not accept pipeline input.
    
    
OUTPUTS
    CommonTypes.ProjectWiseDocument[]
        Returns an array of ProjectWiseDocument objects representing the 
        documents that were created or updated.
    
    
NOTES
    
    
        Spreadsheet Format: The input spreadsheet should match the format 
        produced by Save-PWDocumentReport or Export-PWDocuments cmdlets. The 
        'General' sheet contains core document metadata, while 
        environment-specific sheets contain attribute data. Folder Creation: 
        Folders are created automatically when they don't exist, provided a 
        valid storage area is available (either from DefaultStorage 
        parameter or inherited from an existing parent folder). Folder names 
        longer than 63 characters are truncated. Version Stacks: This cmdlet 
        does not support importing version stacks where the document name 
        varies between versions. However, it does handle file name 
        variations within a version stack (where the document name stays the 
        same but the physical file name differs). Attribute Updates: When 
        updating attributes, the cmdlet first attempts to update all 
        attributes at once. If this fails (due to data type mismatches or 
        other issues), it falls back to updating attributes one at a time 
        and reports which specific attributes failed. Workflow States: 
        Document states are set after import using the State column. If the 
        state cannot be set - most commonly because Workflow Rule Engine 
        (WRE) rules block the transition - the cmdlet emits a warning and 
        continues. The document is still imported, but its state is not 
        changed. Performance Considerations: - Use -WriteBackLinks only with 
        smaller spreadsheets as it rewrites the entire file
        
        - Large imports benefit from using -Verbose to monitor progress
        
        - Attribute update failures are reported individually to help 
        identify data issues
    
    ---------- EXAMPLE 1: Basic Import with Storage Area ----------
    
    Import-PWDocumentsBySpreadsheet -InputFile "C:\Import\documents.xlsx" 
    -DefaultStorage "Storage01"
    
    Imports documents using paths specified in the ProjectWisePath column of 
    the spreadsheet. Documents are created in ProjectWise folders as defined 
    in the spreadsheet. If documents already exist, their files are 
    overwritten. Folders are created as needed using the specified storage 
    area.
    ----------- EXAMPLE 2: Import Under a Target Folder -----------
    
    Import-PWDocumentsBySpreadsheet -InputFile "C:\Import\documents.xlsx" 
    -TargetProjectWiseFolder "Projects\2024\Engineering" -DefaultStorage 
    "Storage01"
    
    Imports documents under a specific target folder. The ProjectWisePath 
    from the spreadsheet is appended to the TargetProjectWiseFolder. For 
    example, if the spreadsheet contains ProjectWisePath 
    "Drawings\Floor1.dwg", the document is created at 
    "Projects\2024\Engineering\Drawings\Floor1.dwg". All necessary folders 
    are created automatically.
    -------------- EXAMPLE 3: Import with Versioning --------------
    
    Import-PWDocumentsBySpreadsheet -InputFile "C:\Import\documents.xlsx" 
    -TargetProjectWiseFolder "Projects\Archive" -DefaultStorage "Storage01" 
    -VersionIfExists
    
    Imports documents and creates new versions when documents with the same 
    name already exist in the target folder. Without the `-VersionIfExists` 
    switch, existing documents would have their files overwritten. When 
    versioning, attributes are copied from the previous version and 
    relations are preserved.
    --- EXAMPLE 4: Import with Default Workflow and Environment ---
    
    Import-PWDocumentsBySpreadsheet -InputFile "C:\Import\documents.xlsx" 
    -TargetProjectWiseFolder "Projects\NewProject" -DefaultStorage 
    "Storage01" -DefaultWorkflow "Document Review" -DefaultEnvironment 
    "Engineering Docs"
    
    Imports documents and applies the specified default workflow and 
    environment to newly created folders. Documents inherit the workflow 
    from their folder and can have their state set based on the State column 
    in the spreadsheet. If the API rejects the state transition, a warning 
    is emitted and the document keeps it default state.
    ----- EXAMPLE 5: Update Attributes Only (No File Changes) -----
    
    Import-PWDocumentsBySpreadsheet -InputFile 
    "C:\Import\attribute_updates.xlsx" -TargetProjectWiseFolder 
    "Projects\Existing" -DefaultStorage "Storage01" -OnlyUpdateAttributes
    
    Updates only the attributes of existing documents without modifying the 
    document files. Useful for bulk metadata updates. The cmdlet locates 
    existing documents by name and version, then applies attribute values 
    from the environment-named sheets in the spreadsheet.
    ----------- EXAMPLE 6: Import with Write-Back Links -----------
    
    Import-PWDocumentsBySpreadsheet -InputFile "C:\Import\documents.xlsx" 
    -TargetProjectWiseFolder "Projects\Import" -DefaultStorage "Storage01" 
    -WriteBackLinks
    
    Imports documents and writes ProjectWise URN links back to the 
    ProjectWiseURNLink column in the input spreadsheet. This creates a 
    reference between the spreadsheet rows and the imported ProjectWise 
    documents. Note: Do not use this option with very large spreadsheets as 
    it rewrites the entire file after import.
    --------- EXAMPLE 7: Import with Missing File Creation ---------
    
    Import-PWDocumentsBySpreadsheet -InputFile "C:\Import\documents.xlsx" 
    -TargetProjectWiseFolder "Projects\Placeholder" -DefaultStorage 
    "Storage01" -CreateMissingFiles
    
    Imports documents and creates placeholder files when source files 
    specified in FileSystemPath do not exist on disk. Without this switch, 
    rows with missing source files are skipped. Placeholder files are 
    created with minimal content (1KB). Useful for creating document stubs 
    that will be replaced later.
    ---- EXAMPLE 8: Import and Set Document State via Pipeline ----
    
    Import-PWDocumentsBySpreadsheet -InputFile "C:\Import\documents.xlsx" 
    -TargetProjectWiseFolder "Projects\Review" -DefaultStorage "Storage01" 
    -DefaultWorkflow "Approval" | Set-PWDocumentState -State "Pending Review"
    
    Imports documents and pipes the resulting ProjectWiseDocument objects to 
    Set-PWDocumentState to set all imported documents to a specific workflow 
    state. The import cmdlet outputs an array of ProjectWiseDocument objects 
    representing all successfully created or updated documents.
    EXAMPLE 9: Import with Versioning and Pipeline to Update State
    
    Import-PWDocumentsBySpreadsheet -InputFile "C:\Import\revisions.xlsx" 
    -TargetProjectWiseFolder "Projects\Active" -DefaultStorage "Storage01" 
    -DefaultWorkflow "Engineering" -VersionIfExists | Set-PWDocumentState 
    -State "In Review"
    
    Imports document revisions, creating new versions for existing 
    documents, and then sets all imported documents to the "In Review" 
    state. Combines versioning with workflow state management in a single 
    pipeline.
    - EXAMPLE 10: Import to Existing Folder (No Storage Required) -
    
    Import-PWDocumentsBySpreadsheet -InputFile "C:\Import\documents.xlsx" 
    -TargetProjectWiseFolder "Projects\ExistingFolder"
    
    Imports documents to an existing ProjectWise folder. When the target 
    folder already exists, the storage area is inherited from that folder, 
    so DefaultStorage is not required. If the folder does not exist and no 
    DefaultStorage is provided, the cmdlet will display a warning and exit.
    ----------- EXAMPLE 11: Full Import with All Options -----------
    
    Import-PWDocumentsBySpreadsheet -InputFile "C:\Import\full_import.xlsx" 
    -TargetProjectWiseFolder "Projects\Complete" -DefaultStorage "Storage01" 
    -DefaultWorkflow "Standard" -DefaultEnvironment "General" 
    -VersionIfExists -WriteBackLinks
    
    Comprehensive import with all configuration options. Creates new 
    versions for existing documents, applies default workflow and 
    environment to new folders, and writes links back to the spreadsheet for 
    tracking.
    -- EXAMPLE 12: Import Abstract Documents (No Physical Files) --
    
    Import-PWDocumentsBySpreadsheet -InputFile "C:\Import\abstracts.xlsx" 
    -TargetProjectWiseFolder "Projects\References" -DefaultStorage 
    "Storage01"
    
    Imports abstract documents (documents without physical files). In the 
    spreadsheet, leave the FileSystemPath and FileName columns empty for 
    abstract documents. Abstract documents are useful for placeholder 
    entries, references, or document sets.
    
RELATED LINKS





--

## Import-PWEnvironments


NAME
    Import-PWEnvironments
    
SYNOPSIS
    Import and update environments from a spreadsheet generated by 
    Export-PWEnvironments.
    
    
SYNTAX
    Import-PWEnvironments [-InputFileName] <string> [[-Environments] 
    <string[]>] [[-Interfaces] <string[]>] [[-ApplyDocumentCodeDefinition]] 
    [<CommonParameters>]
    
    
DESCRIPTION
    A ProjectWise environment defines the custom attributes (metadata 
    fields) available on documents, how those fields are laid out on 
    interface tabs, and any document code rules that apply. 
    Import-PWEnvironments reads those definitions from an Excel workbook and 
    creates or updates the matching environments in the connected datasource.
    
    The typical workflow is:
    
    1. Run Export-PWEnvironments to produce a workbook that describes one or 
    more existing environments (or start from a workbook in the same 
    format). 2. Edit the workbook - add rows for new environments or 
    attributes, or change the values in existing rows. 3. Run 
    Import-PWEnvironments to apply those changes back to ProjectWise.
    
    This is a more flexible alternative to maintaining environments with AAM 
    files. An environment is created when its name does not already exist; 
    otherwise the existing environment is updated in place. Supported column 
    type keywords are text, int, numeric and datetime. Workbook structure: 
    The input workbook uses the format produced by Export-PWEnvironments and 
    may contain up to three sheets:
    
    - Environments (required): one row per environment, with the Name, 
    TableName, Description and AutoCreateRecords columns. This sheet drives 
    the import, so it must be present. - EnvironmentAttributes (optional): 
    the attribute (column) definitions for each environment - data type, 
    length, default values, value lists, triggers and document code 
    settings. If this sheet is omitted, no attributes are created or 
    modified. - Interfaces (optional): the interface (GUI) layout for each 
    attribute. If this sheet is omitted, no interfaces are created or 
    modified.
    
    Only the Environments sheet is mandatory. A missing 
    EnvironmentAttributes or Interfaces sheet is treated the same as an 
    empty sheet, so an existing environment can be re-imported even when it 
    has no attribute or interface rows.
    
    Note: creating a new environment always creates a new backing table, and 
    a table must have at least one column. A workbook that contains only the 
    Environments sheet (no attribute rows for a new environment) therefore 
    cannot create that environment; the cmdlet writes a warning explaining 
    that the table would have no columns and skips it. What you can change: 
    - Add new environments, or add attributes to existing ones, by adding 
    rows to the spreadsheet.
    
    - Change the properties of an existing attribute - such as its default 
    value, value list, trigger, edit control type or format string - by 
    editing the matching row.
    
    - Add, replace or move interface elements by editing the Interfaces 
    sheet.
    
    - Modify or remove document code definitions by using the 
    -ApplyDocumentCodeDefinition switch.
    

PARAMETERS
    -InputFileName <string>
        Path to the Excel workbook (.xlsx) to import. The workbook must be 
        in the format generated by Export-PWEnvironments and must contain an 
        'Environments' sheet. This is the only required parameter.
        
        Required?                    true
        Position?                    0
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -Environments <string[]>
        One or more environment names to import. Only matching rows on the 
        Environments sheet are processed; all other environments in the 
        workbook are skipped. Omit this parameter to import every 
        environment in the workbook.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    2
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -Interfaces <string[]>
        One or more interface names to import. When supplied, only the named 
        interfaces on the Interfaces sheet are created or updated. Omit this 
        parameter to import every interface definition in the workbook.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    3
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -ApplyDocumentCodeDefinition [<SwitchParameter>]
        When set, replaces the existing document code definition of each 
        environment with the document code rows defined in the workbook. Use 
        with caution: the previous definition is removed first. When not 
        set, document code definitions are left unchanged.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    4
        Default value                False
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    <CommonParameters>
        This cmdlet supports the common parameters: Verbose, Debug,
        ErrorAction, ErrorVariable, WarningAction, WarningVariable,
        OutBuffer, PipelineVariable, and OutVariable. For more information, 
    see 
        about_CommonParameters 
    (https:/go.microsoft.com/fwlink/?LinkID=113216). 
    
INPUTS
    None
        This cmdlet does not accept pipeline input. Provide the workbook 
        path with the -InputFileName parameter.
    
    
OUTPUTS
    None
        This cmdlet does not return objects to the pipeline. Use -Verbose to 
        follow per-environment progress; problems are reported as warnings 
        and validation failures as errors.
    
    
NOTES
    
    
        Spreadsheet Format: The input spreadsheet should match the format 
        produced by Export-PWEnvironments. Only the 'Environments' sheet is 
        required; the 'EnvironmentAttributes' and 'Interfaces' sheets are 
        optional and a missing optional sheet is treated the same as an 
        empty one. Error Handling: The cmdlet validates its input before 
        processing and writes a terminating error (without throwing an 
        unhandled exception) in the following cases: - The file specified by 
        -InputFileName does not exist (InputFileNotFound).
        
        - The file cannot be loaded or parsed as a spreadsheet 
        (InvalidSpreadsheetFile).
        
        - The workbook is missing the required 'Environments' sheet 
        (MissingRequiredSheet). Document Code Definitions: Document code 
        definitions are only modified when the -ApplyDocumentCodeDefinition 
        switch is supplied and the EnvironmentAttributes sheet contains rows 
        flagged as document code elements. Troubleshooting Table or Column 
        Creation: Tables and columns are created and modified through the 
        ProjectWise API. If creation fails, the cause is usually a reserved 
        word, an invalid column name or an invalid column width. The cmdlet 
        reports the underlying ProjectWise error as a warning - correct the 
        offending value in the spreadsheet and run the import again.
        
        ## RELATED LINKS
    
    ------ EXAMPLE 1: Import every environment in a workbook ------
    
    Import-PWEnvironments -InputFileName "C:\report\environments.xlsx"
    
    Creates or updates every environment listed on the Environments sheet, 
    together with the attributes and interfaces defined on the other sheets.
    --------- EXAMPLE 2: Import only specific environments ---------
    
    Import-PWEnvironments -InputFileName "C:\report\environments.xlsx" 
    -Environments Simple,Complex
    
    Processes only the "Simple" and "Complex" environments and ignores the 
    other rows on the Environments sheet.
    -------- EXAMPLE 3: Limit which interfaces are imported --------
    
    Import-PWEnvironments -InputFileName "C:\report\environments.xlsx" 
    -Interfaces "Title Block","Review"
    
    Imports the environments but only creates or updates the named 
    interfaces; other interface definitions in the workbook are skipped.
    ---- EXAMPLE 4: Replace existing document code definitions ----
    
    Import-PWEnvironments -InputFileName "C:\report\environments.xlsx" 
    -ApplyDocumentCodeDefinition
    
    Imports the workbook and replaces the document code definition of each 
    environment using the attribute rows flagged as document code elements. 
    Without this switch, existing document code definitions are left 
    unchanged.
    
RELATED LINKS

## Invoke-PWCelExpression


NAME
    Invoke-PWCelExpression
    
SYNOPSIS
    Executes a CEL expression from PowerShell.
    
    
SYNTAX
    Invoke-PWCelExpression [-Expression] <String> -CurrentVersion <String> 
    [<CommonParameters>]
    
    Invoke-PWCelExpression [-Expression] <String> -DocumentGUID <String> 
    [-FormFields <Hashtable>] [-LoadFormFields <Boolean>] 
    [<CommonParameters>]
    
    
DESCRIPTION
    The Invoke-PWCelExpression cmdlet executes a CEL expression from 
    PowerShell.
    
    It supports executing expression in version and document form contexts, 
    or contextless. See examples for more details.
    

PARAMETERS
    -Expression <String>
        CEL expression to execute
        
        Required?                    true
        Position?                    0
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -CurrentVersion <String>
        Version string for executing the expression in version context
        
        Required?                    true
        Position?                    named
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -DocumentGUID <String>
        Document GUID for executing the expression in document attribute 
        context
        
        Required?                    true
        Position?                    named
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -FormFields <Hashtable>
        Form field names and values
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    named
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -LoadFormFields <Boolean>
        Load all form field name value pairs for this document from the 
        database into the FormFields Hashtable for use in expression (as 
        'thisForm.name'). Default is true, so not specifying parameter will 
        load form fields from the database.  Specify false to skip the load 
        and use only form fields specified by the -FormFields parameter. 
        Note that name value pairs specified in the -FormFields parameter 
        override any database values loaded.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    named
        Default value                True
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    <CommonParameters>
        This cmdlet supports the common parameters: Verbose, Debug,
        ErrorAction, ErrorVariable, WarningAction, WarningVariable,
        OutBuffer, PipelineVariable, and OutVariable. For more information, 
    see 
        about_CommonParameters 
    (https:/go.microsoft.com/fwlink/?LinkID=113216). 
    
INPUTS
    
OUTPUTS
    System.Collections.Generic.List<Bentley.ProjectWise.PowerShell.Common.Cle
    aredResult>
        Invoke-PWCelExpression returns a list of ClearedResult objects 
        (which contain Value and Description strings). If there are no 
        results, an empty list is returned.
    
    
NOTES
    
    
        
    
    ----------------------- Version context -----------------------
    
    Invoke-PWCelExpression "currentVersion.incrementAlphaVersion()" 
    -CurrentVersion "A"
    
    Executing CEL expressions in version context requires specifying the 
    -CurrentVersion parameter. It's value is injected into the expression as 
    currentVersion.
    
    Value Description ----- ----------- B
    --------------------- Access Value in List ---------------------
    
    (Invoke-PWCelExpression "currentVersion.incrementAlphaVersion()" 
    -CurrentVersion "A").Value
    
    Same incremenetAlphaVersion example as above, access the Value property 
    of single element List returned.
    
    B
    ------------------ Document Attribute Context ------------------
    
    Invoke-PWCelExpression "'{0} {1} {2}'.format([thisDocument.name, 
    thisFolder.name, thisForm.otherField])" -DocumentGUID 
    "e899a390-d4c1-4904-8147-4d281139e516" -FormFields 
    @{otherField="jungle"} -LoadFormFields $false
    
    Executing CEL expressions in document attribute context requires 
    specifying the -DocumentGUID parameter. The document metadata is loaded 
    and injected into expression as variables thisDocument and parentFolder. 
     Because LoadFormFields is false, no form field values are loaded from 
    the database.
    
    The optional -FormFields parameter is injected as thisForm.
    
    Value                          Description -----                         
     ----------- AllDGNLinkSets searches jungle
    -------- Document Attribute Context with LoadFormFields --------
    
    (Invoke-PWCelExpression "'{0} {1} {2}'.format([thisDocument.name, 
    thisForm.tb_title_line_1, thisForm.rv_rev_1])" -DocumentGUID 
    "e899a390-d4c1-4904-8147-4d281139e516" -LoadFormFields $true).Value
    
    thisForm values are referenced in the expression, but not specified via 
    any -FormFields parameter (all form field values are loaded from the 
    database).
    
    AllDGNLinkSets This is the title line 1 v1.0.1
    -------- Document Attribute Context with LoadFormFields --------
    
    (Invoke-PWCelExpression "'{0} {1} {2}'.format([thisDocument.name, 
    thisForm.tb_title_line_1, thisForm.rv_rev_1])" -DocumentGUID 
    "e899a390-d4c1-4904-8147-4d281139e516" -FormFields 
    @{rv_rev_1="v9.0.1234"}).Value
    
    thisForm values are referenced in the expression.  Values are loaded 
    from the database (no -LoadFormFields parameter, but default value is 
    true), but the rv_rev_1 form field is specified via the -FormFields 
    parameter, so that specified values overrides what was loaded from the 
    database.
    
    AllDGNLinkSets This is the title line 1 v9.0.1234
    ------------------------- Contextless -------------------------
    
    (Invoke-PWCelExpression "1+1").Value
    
    If no additional parameters are specifyied, the expression is executed 
    in contextless mode.
    
    2
    ----------------------- Multiline output -----------------------
    
    (Invoke-PWCelExpression "['one', 'two', 'three']").Value
    
    If the expression returns an array, the cmdlet returns a list of values.
    
    one two three
    
    
    (Invoke-PWCelExpression "['one', 'two', 'three']")[0]
    
    Access element of list by index using array accessor syntax.
    
    Value Description ----- ----------- one
    
    
    (Invoke-PWCelExpression "['one', 'two', 'three']").Item(0).Value
    
    Access element of list using Item() method, specifying index.
    
    one
    
RELATED LINKS

## Move-PWDocumentsToFolder


NAME
    Move-PWDocumentsToFolder
    
SYNOPSIS
    Moves documents to a target folder.
    
    
SYNTAX
    Move-PWDocumentsToFolder [-InputDocument] <ProjectWiseDocument[]> 
    [-TargetFolderPath] <string> [[-CreateVersion]] [[-DoNotPreserveState]] 
    [<CommonParameters>]
    
    
DESCRIPTION
    Takes an array of document objects from the pipeline and moves them to a 
    target folder.
    

PARAMETERS
    -InputDocument <ProjectWiseDocument[]>
        Array of document objects from pipeline (Required).
        
        Required?                    true
        Position?                    0
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       True (ByValue)
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -TargetFolderPath <string>
        Path to target folder (Required).
        
        Required?                    true
        Position?                    1
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -CreateVersion [<SwitchParameter>]
        Set this to create a new version if the document exists. If not set, 
        document will be skipped.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    2
        Default value                False
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -DoNotPreserveState [<SwitchParameter>]
        Set this to bypass workflow state preservation.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    3
        Default value                False
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    <CommonParameters>
        This cmdlet supports the common parameters: Verbose, Debug,
        ErrorAction, ErrorVariable, WarningAction, WarningVariable,
        OutBuffer, PipelineVariable, and OutVariable. For more information, 
    see 
        about_CommonParameters 
    (https:/go.microsoft.com/fwlink/?LinkID=113216). 
    
INPUTS
    PWPS_DAB.CommonTypes+ProjectWiseDocument[]
        Array of document objects from pipeline (Required).
    
    
OUTPUTS
    
NOTES
    
    
        
    
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 1 --------------------------
    
    # Get the documents in the given path and move them to a target folder. 
    Document states will be preserved in the target folder.
    Get-PWDocumentsByGUIDs -DocumentGUIDs 
    'd0bc426a-973e-4ebe-8764-7739e81e9182' | Move-PWDocumentsToFolder 
    -TargetFolderPath 'New\Folder\Path'
    
    
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 2 --------------------------
    
    # Get the documents in the folder and move them to a target folder. Do 
    not preserve the source state for each document.
    $pwDocs = Get-PWDocumentsByGUIDs -DocumentGUIDs 
    'd0bc426a-973e-4ebe-8764-7739e81e9182', 
    '31050aa0-bf0c-4804-a9f4-236c54496f33'
                $pwDocsMoved = Move-PWDocumentsToFolder -InputDocument 
    $pwDocs -TargetFolderPath 'PowerShell\PowerShell_Target' 
    -DoNotPreserveState -Verbose
    
    
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 3 --------------------------
    
    # Get the documents in the folder and move them to a target folder. 
    Create versions if documents exist in target. Document states will be 
    preserved in the target folder.
    $pwDocs = Get-PWDocumentsByGUIDs -DocumentGUIDs 
    'd0bc426a-973e-4ebe-8764-7739e81e9182', 
    '31050aa0-bf0c-4804-a9f4-236c54496f33'
                $pwDocsMoved = Move-PWDocumentsToFolder -InputDocument 
    $pwDocs -TargetFolderPath 'PowerShell\PowerShell_Target' -CreateVersion 
    -Verbose
    
    
    
RELATED LINKS

## Move-PWFolder


NAME
    Move-PWFolder
    
SYNOPSIS
    The Move-PWFolder cmdlet allows you to move a project with all 
    sub-folders and documents to another parent folder or the root directory.
    
    
SYNTAX
    Move-PWFolder [-InputFolder] <ProjectWiseFolder> 
    [-NewParentPath_Or_ROOT_] <string> [<CommonParameters>]
    
    Move-PWFolder [-FolderPath] <string> [-NewParentPath_Or_ROOT_] <string> 
    [<CommonParameters>]
    
    
DESCRIPTION
    Allows you to move a project with all sub-folders and documents to 
    another parent folder or the root directory. To move to the root 
    directory, use ROOT in the NewParentPath_Or_ROOT_ parameter.
    

PARAMETERS
    -InputFolder <ProjectWiseFolder>
        Input folder object (from the pipeline) to move.
        
        Required?                    true
        Position?                    0
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       True (ByValue)
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -FolderPath <string>
        Full path to the folder to be moved.
        
        Required?                    true
        Position?                    0
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -NewParentPath_Or_ROOT_ <string>
        Target folder or root directory to move folder into. Use ROOT to 
        move to the root directory.
        
        Required?                    true
        Position?                    1
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    <CommonParameters>
        This cmdlet supports the common parameters: Verbose, Debug,
        ErrorAction, ErrorVariable, WarningAction, WarningVariable,
        OutBuffer, PipelineVariable, and OutVariable. For more information, 
    see 
        about_CommonParameters 
    (https:/go.microsoft.com/fwlink/?LinkID=113216). 
    
INPUTS
    PWPS_DAB.CommonTypes+ProjectWiseFolder
        Input folder object (from the pipeline) to move.
    
    
OUTPUTS
    
NOTES
    
    
        
    
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 1 --------------------------
    
    Get-PWFolders -FolderPath 'test\ziptest' -JustOne | Move-PWFolder 
    -NewParentPath_Or_ROOT_ "VS_Test" -Verbose
    
    Move the folder `test\ziptest` to the `VS_Test` folder by piping the 
    folder object to the `Move-PWFolder` cmdlet.
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 2 --------------------------
    
    $folder = Show-PWFolderBrowserDialog
    Move-PWFolder -InputFolder $folder -NewParentPath_Or_ROOT_ "test" 
    -Verbose
    
    Select folder using the `Show-PWFolderBrowserDialog` cmdlet. Pass the 
    returned folder object to the `Move-PWFolder` cmdlet.
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 3 --------------------------
    
    Move-PWFolder -FolderPath 'test\ziptest' -NewParentPath_Or_ROOT_ "_ROOT" 
    -Verbose
    
    Move the specified folder to the ROOT folder.
    
RELATED LINKS

## New-PWDatasource


NAME
    New-PWDatasource
    
SYNOPSIS
    Creates and initializes a ProjectWise datasource.
    
    
SYNTAX
    New-PWDatasource [-DatasourceName] <String> [-OdbcName] <String> 
    [-DatabaseUserName] <String> [-DatabasePassword] <SecureString> 
    [-AdminUserName] <String> [-AdminPassword] <Object> [-StorageHost] 
    <String> [-StoragePath] <String> [-AdminDescription <String>] 
    [-AdminEmail <String>] [-AdminGroupDescription <String>] 
    [-AdminGroupName <String>] [-AutoStorageLocation] [-KeepOpenConnection] 
    [-AddPrivateProtection] [-LoginSSO] [-LoginSTS] [-NoLoginSTS] 
    [-LoginDMS] [-NoLoginDMS] [-LoginOIDC] [-RestrictedAdminGroupDescription 
    <String>] [-RestrictedAdminGroupName <String>] [-StorageDescription 
    <String>] [-StorageName <String>] [-StorageType <StorageType>] 
    [-NoProviderSTS] [-NoProviderOIDC] [-ProviderOIDCClientId] 
    [<CommonParameters>]
    
    
DESCRIPTION
    Creates a ProjectWise datasource with an initial administrator account 
    and storage area. Run the cmdlet from an elevated Windows process. An 
    existing ProjectWise connection is not required.
    
    Before running the cmdlet, ensure the database, ODBC data source, 
    storage host, and storage path are ready. Use the optional parameters to 
    customize administrator groups, storage, login methods, and identity 
    providers.
    
    The cmdlet returns the active datasource object after it creates the 
    datasource. By default, it closes the connection when it completes; use 
    KeepOpenConnection to leave the connection open.
    

PARAMETERS
    -AdminDescription <String>
        The description of administrator user.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    named
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       True (ByPropertyName)
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -AdminEmail <String>
        The email adress of administrator user.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    named
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       True (ByPropertyName)
        Accept wildcard characters?  false

        Position?                    named
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       True (ByPropertyName)
        Accept wildcard characters?  false

        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -NoProviderSTS [<SwitchParameter>]
        Do not create Bentley IMS/STS identity provider in datasource. 
        Creates only if IMS/STS is enabled. Creates by default.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    named
        Default value                False
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -NoProviderOIDC [<SwitchParameter>]
        Do not create Bentley OIDC identity provider in datasource. Creates 
        only if OIDC is enabled. Creates by default.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    named
        Default value                False
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -ProviderOIDCClientId [<SwitchParameter>]
        Use this Client ID for Bentley OIDC identity provider. Default is to 
        use projectwise-explorer-client.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    named
        Default value                False
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    <CommonParameters>
        This cmdlet supports the common parameters: Verbose, Debug,
        ErrorAction, ErrorVariable, WarningAction, WarningVariable,
        OutBuffer, PipelineVariable, and OutVariable. For more information, 
    see 
        about_CommonParameters 
    (https:/go.microsoft.com/fwlink/?LinkID=113216). 
    
INPUTS
    
OUTPUTS
    
NOTES
    
    
        
    
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 1 --------------------------
    
    $SecurePassword = Read-Host -Prompt "Enter password" -AsSecureString
    New-PWDatasource -DatasourceName "NewDatasourceName" -OdbcName 
    "PWOdbcName" -DatabaseUserName "DatabaseUserName" -DatabasePassword 
    $SecurePassword -AdminUserName "DatasourceAdminName" -AdminPassword 
    "DatasourceAdminPassword" -StorageHost "HostName" -StoragePath 
    C:\NewStorageName
    
    Create a new datasouse by entering the database password by hand.
    
    InternalName         HostName     ServerAddress DatabaseType IsSSO IsSTS 
    IsSecure ------------         --------     ------------- ------------ 
    ----- ----- -------- New Datasource Name  HostName     10.87.7.41     
    SQLServer     N     N      N
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 2 --------------------------
    
    $SecurePassword = ConvertTo-SecureString "password" -AsPlainText -Force
    New-PWDatasource -DatasourceName "NewDatasourceName" -OdbcName 
    "PWOdbcName" -DatabaseUserName "DatabaseUserName" -DatabasePassword 
    $SecurePassword -AdminUserName "DatasourceAdminName" -AdminPassword 
    "DatasourceAdminPassword" -StorageHost "HostName" -StoragePath 
    C:\NewStorageName
    
    Create a new datasouse by defining the password in a variable.
    
    InternalName         HostName     ServerAddress DatabaseType IsSSO IsSTS 
    IsSecure ------------         --------     ------------- ------------ 
    ----- ----- -------- New Datasource Name  HostName     10.87.7.41     
    SQLServer     N     N      N

## New-PWDocument


NAME
    New-PWDocument
    
SYNOPSIS
    Creates ProjectWise documents from local files.
    
    
SYNTAX
    New-PWDocument [[-InputFolders] <ProjectWiseFolder[]>] [-FilePath] 
    <string> [[-Description] <string>] [[-Version] <string>] [[-Application] 
    <string>] [[-DocumentName] <string>] [<CommonParameters>]
    
    New-PWDocument [[-FolderPath] <string>] [-FilePath] <string> 
    [[-Description] <string>] [[-Version] <string>] [[-Application] 
    <string>] [[-DocumentName] <string>] [<CommonParameters>]
    
    
DESCRIPTION
    Creates ProjectWise documents from an existing local file in selected 
    folders. Select folders with `InputFolders` or `FolderPath`.
    
    The document name defaults to the local file name, and the description 
    defaults to the document name. You can provide a version, application, 
    name, and description.
    

PARAMETERS
    -InputFolders <ProjectWiseFolder[]>
        Target folder object(s) from pipeline.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    0
        Default value                PWPS_DAB.CommonTypes+ProjectWiseFolder[]
        Accept pipeline input?       True (ByValue)
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -FolderPath <string>
        Target folder path (not required if sending folder(s) on pipeline.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    1
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -FilePath <string>
        Path to file on disk.
        
        Required?                    true
        Position?                    2
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -Description <string>
        Description of new document (optional).
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    3
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -Version <string>
        Version string for new document (optional).
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    4
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -Application <string>
        Application Name to use (optional). Will use default application for 
        file extension if not supplied.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    5
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -DocumentName <string>
        Document Name to use. FileName used if not supplied.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    6
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    <CommonParameters>
        This cmdlet supports the common parameters: Verbose, Debug,
        ErrorAction, ErrorVariable, WarningAction, WarningVariable,
        OutBuffer, PipelineVariable, and OutVariable. For more information, 
    see 
        about_CommonParameters 
    (https:/go.microsoft.com/fwlink/?LinkID=113216). 
    
INPUTS
    PWPS_DAB.CommonTypes+ProjectWiseFolder[]
        Target folder object(s) from pipeline.
    
    
OUTPUTS
    
NOTES
    
    
        
    
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 1 --------------------------
    
    Get-PWFolders 20315 | New-PWDocument -FilePath c:\temp\NewDrawing.dgn
    
    
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 2 --------------------------
    
    Get-PWFolders -FolderPath 'Level 1\Level 2\Level 3\Level 4' -JustOne | 
    New-PWDocument -FilePath c:\temp\NewDrawing.dgn -Description 'Drawing 1'
    
    
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 3 --------------------------
    
    New-PWFolder -FolderPath 'PowerShell Demo\Level 2\Level 3\Level 4' 
    -StorageArea Storage01 | New-PWDocument -FilePath c:\temp\NewDrawing.dgn 
    -Description 'Drawing 1'
    
    
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 4 --------------------------
    
    New-PWDocument -FolderPath 'Demo\Folder 1' -FilePath 
    c:\temp\NewDrawing.dgn -Description 'Drawing 1'

## New-PWDocumentAbstract


NAME
    New-PWDocumentAbstract
    
SYNOPSIS
    Given a folder object or folder path, create one or more abstract 
    documents without attributes.
    
    
SYNTAX
    New-PWDocumentAbstract [-InputFolders] <ProjectWiseFolder[]> 
    [[-DocumentName] <string>] [[-Description] <string>] [[-Version] 
    <string>] [-Count <int>] [-DoNotAddSuffix] [<CommonParameters>]
    
    New-PWDocumentAbstract [[-DocumentName] <string>] [[-Description] 
    <string>] [[-Version] <string>] -FolderPath <string> [-Count <int>] 
    [-DoNotAddSuffix] [<CommonParameters>]
    
    
DESCRIPTION
    Given a folder object or folder path, create one or more abstract 
    documents without attributes. When the DoNotAddSuffix parameter is 
    included, the 5 digit serial number will not be placed at the end of the 
    document name and only one document will be added to each folder 
    regardless of the count entered.
    

PARAMETERS
    -InputFolders <ProjectWiseFolder[]>
        Target folder object(s) from pipeline.
        
        Required?                    true
        Position?                    0
        Default value                PWPS_DAB.CommonTypes+ProjectWiseFolder[]
        Accept pipeline input?       True (ByValue)
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -FolderPath <string>
        Target folder path (not required if sending folder(s) on pipeline).
        
        Required?                    true
        Position?                    named
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -DocumentName <string>
        Name to prefix abstract document (Optional) If excluded, 'doc' will 
        be used.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    1
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -Description <string>
        Description of new document (optional). If excluded, the document 
        name will be used.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    2
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -Version <string>
        Version string to use for new document (optional).
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    3
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -Count <int>
        Number of abstract documents to create. (Optional) If excluded, only 
        one will be created
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    named
        Default value                0
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -DoNotAddSuffix [<SwitchParameter>]
        When included, the 5 digit serial number suffix will not be added to 
        the document name and only one document will be added to each folder 
        regardless of the count entered.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    named
        Default value                False
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    <CommonParameters>
        This cmdlet supports the common parameters: Verbose, Debug,
        ErrorAction, ErrorVariable, WarningAction, WarningVariable,
        OutBuffer, PipelineVariable, and OutVariable. For more information, 
    see 
        about_CommonParameters 
    (https:/go.microsoft.com/fwlink/?LinkID=113216). 
    
INPUTS
    PWPS_DAB.CommonTypes+ProjectWiseFolder[]
        Target folder object(s) from pipeline.
    
    
OUTPUTS
    
NOTES
    
    
        
    
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 1 --------------------------
    
    #Example usage getting folders and creating new documents.
    # To create abstract documents without getting InputFolders from the 
    pipeline
        Supply the folder full path using -FolderPath, supply document name 
    (optional), 
        document description (optional) and/or count (optional) and create a 
    new document there
    New-PWDocumentAbstract -FolderPath 'Level 1\' -DocumentName 'doc' 
    -Description 'Abstract Document'
    New-PWDocumentAbstract -FolderPath 'Level 1\' -DocumentName 
    'AbstractDoc' -Count 2
    
    
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 2 --------------------------
    
    # To create abstract documents while getting InputFolders from the 
    pipeline, supply document name (optional), 
        document description (optional) and/or count (optional) and create a 
    new document there
    Get-PWFolders -FolderPath 'Level 1\Level 2\Level 3\Level 4' -JustOne | 
    New-PWDocumentAbstract -DocumentName 'doc'
    Get-PWFolders -FolderPath 'Level 1\' | New-PWDocumentAbstract 
    -DocumentName 'AbstractDoc' -Count 3
    
    
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 3 --------------------------
    
    The following example will create one document within each folder 
    without adding the 5 digit serial number suffix.
    Get-PWFolders -FolderPath 'Level 1' | New-PWDocumentAbstract 
    -DocumentName 'doc' -DoNotAddSuffix
    
    
    
RELATED LINKS

## New-PWDocumentComment


NAME
    New-PWDocumentComment
    
SYNOPSIS
    New-PWDocumentComment adds a comment to the audit trail for the 
    specified document.
    
    
SYNTAX
    New-PWDocumentComment [-InputDocument] <ProjectWiseDocument[]> 
    [-Comment] <string> [<CommonParameters>]
    
    New-PWDocumentComment [-FolderPath] <string> [-DocumentName] <string> 
    [-Comment] <string> [<CommonParameters>]
    
    
DESCRIPTION
    New-PWDocumentComment adds a comment to the audit trail for the 
    specified document within the active datasource.
    

PARAMETERS
    -InputDocument <ProjectWiseDocument[]>
        Input array of ProjectWiseDocument objects from the pipeline 
        (Required). Use Get-PWDocumentBySearch or New-PWDocument to feed 
        pipeline.
        
        Required?                    true
        Position?                    0
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       True (ByValue)
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -FolderPath <string>
        Folder path to folder containing the specified document.
        
        Required?                    true
        Position?                    0
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -DocumentName <string>
        Document Name of document to add commen to.
        
        Required?                    true
        Position?                    1
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -Comment <string>
        Comment to be added to the documents audit trail.
        
        Required?                    true
        Position?                    2
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    <CommonParameters>
        This cmdlet supports the common parameters: Verbose, Debug,
        ErrorAction, ErrorVariable, WarningAction, WarningVariable,
        OutBuffer, PipelineVariable, and OutVariable. For more information, 
    see 
        about_CommonParameters 
    (https:/go.microsoft.com/fwlink/?LinkID=113216). 
    
INPUTS
    PWPS_DAB.CommonTypes+ProjectWiseDocument[]
        Input array of ProjectWiseDocument objects from the pipeline 
        (Required). Use Get-PWDocumentBySearch or New-PWDocument to feed 
        pipeline.
    
    
OUTPUTS
    
NOTES
    
    
        
    
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 1 --------------------------
    
    New-PWDocumentComment -FolderPath 'Projects' -DocumentName 
    'MyDrawing.dgn' -Comment 'Updated drawing. Added elevations.' -Verbose
    
    Adds a comment to the document specified by folder path and document 
    name.
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 2 --------------------------
    
    $docs = Get-PWDocumentsBySearch -FolderPath "Project XXXXXX" 
    -JustThisFolder
    New-PWDocumentComment -InputDocument $docs -Comment "Test Comment" 
    -Verbose
    
    Adds a comment to each document within the `$docs` variable.
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 3 --------------------------
    
    Get-PWDocumentsBySearch -FolderPath "Project XXXXXX" -JustThisFolder |
    New-PWDocumentComment -Comment "Another Test Comment" -Verbose
    
    Adds a comment to each document being passed via the pipeline.

## New-PWDocumentVersion


NAME
    New-PWDocumentVersion
    
SYNOPSIS
    Creates new versions of ProjectWise documents passed on the pipeline.
    
    
SYNTAX
    New-PWDocumentVersion [-InputDocument] <ProjectWiseDocument[]> 
    [[-VersionString] <string>] [<CommonParameters>]
    
    
DESCRIPTION
    Search for documents and version them to version A.
    
    Get-PWDocumentsBySearch -SearchName "Documents in Released State" | 
    New-PWDocumentVersion -VersionString A
    
    Search for documents in folder and version to next version.
    
    Get-PWDocumentsBySearch -FolderPath "Project A\Files to Version" | 
    New-PWDocumentVersion
    

PARAMETERS
    -InputDocument <ProjectWiseDocument[]>
        List of documents from pipeline (Required).
        
        Required?                    true
        Position?                    0
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       True (ByValue)
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -VersionString <string>
        Optional version string.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    1
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    <CommonParameters>
        This cmdlet supports the common parameters: Verbose, Debug,
        ErrorAction, ErrorVariable, WarningAction, WarningVariable,
        OutBuffer, PipelineVariable, and OutVariable. For more information, 
    see 
        about_CommonParameters 
    (https:/go.microsoft.com/fwlink/?LinkID=113216). 
    
INPUTS
    PWPS_DAB.CommonTypes+ProjectWiseDocument[]
        List of documents from pipeline (Required).
    
    
OUTPUTS
    
NOTES
    
    
        
    
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 1 --------------------------
    
    #Search for documents and version them to version A.
    Get-PWDocumentsBySearch -SearchName "Documents in Released State" | 
    New-PWDocumentVersion -VersionString A
    #Search for documents in folder version to next version.
    Get-PWDocumentsBySearch -FolderPath "Project A\Files to Version" | 
    New-PWDocumentVersion

## New-PWGroup


NAME
    New-PWGroup
    
SYNOPSIS
    Creates a new User Group in the current ProjectWise Datasource.
    
    
SYNTAX
    New-PWGroup [-GroupName] <String> [[-Description] <String>] 
    [-SecProvider] <String> -DomainGroup [-MembershipIsUpdatedBySTS] 
    [<CommonParameters>]
    
    
DESCRIPTION
    The New-PWGroup cmdlet creates a new User Group in the current 
    ProjectWise Datasource.
    
    By default, New-PWGroup creates a logical Group in the current 
    ProjectWise Datasource.  To add a Windows Domain Group to the 
    Datasource, set the -DomainGroup flag.
    

PARAMETERS
    -Description <String>
        The Description of the Group to create.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    1
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       True (ByPropertyName)
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -DomainGroup [<SwitchParameter>]
        If set, creates a Windows Domain Group, otherwise creates a Logical 
        Group.
        
        Required?                    true
        Position?                    named
        Default value                False
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -GroupName <String>
        The Name of the Group to create. (must be unique!)
        
        Required?                    true
        Position?                    0
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       True (ByPropertyName)
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -MembershipIsUpdatedBySTS [<SwitchParameter>]
        If set, enables group membership to be updated by IMS/STS Claims.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    named
        Default value                False
        Accept pipeline input?       True (ByPropertyName)
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -SecProvider <String>
        The Domain of the Group to create (only used for Windows Domain 
        Groups).
        
        Required?                    true
        Position?                    2
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       True (ByPropertyName)
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    <CommonParameters>
        This cmdlet supports the common parameters: Verbose, Debug,
        ErrorAction, ErrorVariable, WarningAction, WarningVariable,
        OutBuffer, PipelineVariable, and OutVariable. For more information, 
    see 
        about_CommonParameters 
    (https:/go.microsoft.com/fwlink/?LinkID=113216). 
    
INPUTS
    Bentley.ProjectWise.PowerShell.Common.Group
        You can pipe Group objects to New-PWGroup to clone a Group.
    
    
OUTPUTS
    Bentley.ProjectWise.PowerShell.Common.Group
        New-PWGroup returns an object that represents the new Group.
    
    
NOTES
    
    
        New-PWGroup requires an open ProjectWise Connection in the current 
        thread.  You can open a Connection with Open-PWConnection before 
        calling New-PWGroup.
        
        New-PWGroup requires an Admin session.  To open an admin session, 
        use the -Admin flag when opening the ProjectWise Connection with 
        Open-PWConnection.
    
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 1 --------------------------
    
    New-PWGroup -GroupName ANewGroup -Description "This parameter is 
    optional"
    
    Create a new ProjectWise (Logical) Group.
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 2 --------------------------
    
    New-PWGroup -DomainGroup -GroupName DomainGroup -SecProvider DOMAIN 
    -Description "This parameter is optional"
    
    Create a new ProjectWise (Windows) Group.
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 3 --------------------------
    
    New-PWGroup -GroupName ANewGroup -Description "STS enabled group" 
    -MembershipIsUpdatedBySTS
    
    Create a new ProjectWise (Logical) Group whose membership is updated by 
    IMS/STS Claims.
    
RELATED LINKS





--

## New-PWIndexServer


NAME
    New-PWIndexServer

    Datasource.
    
    
SYNTAX
    New-PWIndexServer [-Name] <String> [-HostName] <String> [[-ServiceUrl] 
    <String>] [-Guid <Guid>] [<CommonParameters>]
    
    
DESCRIPTION
    The New-PWIndexServer cmdlet registers a new Index Server in the active 
    ProjectWise Datasource.
    

PARAMETERS
    -Guid <Guid>
        Index Server GUID.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    named
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       True (ByValue)
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -HostName <String>
        Index Server host name.
        
        Required?                    true
        Position?                    1
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       True (ByValue)
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -Name <String>
        Index Server name.
        
        Required?                    true
        Position?                    0
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       True (ByValue)
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -ServiceUrl <String>
        Index Server service URL.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    2
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       True (ByValue)
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    <CommonParameters>
        This cmdlet supports the common parameters: Verbose, Debug,
        ErrorAction, ErrorVariable, WarningAction, WarningVariable,
        OutBuffer, PipelineVariable, and OutVariable. For more information, 
    see 
        about_CommonParameters 
    (https:/go.microsoft.com/fwlink/?LinkID=113216).

OUTPUTS
    Bentley.ProjectWise.PowerShell.Common.IndexServer
        
    
    
NOTES
    
    
        This cmdlet requires an open administrative ProjectWise Connection 
        in the current thread. You can open an administrative Connection 
        using the Open-PWConnection cmdlet and setting the -Admin flag.
    
    -------------------------- Example 1 --------------------------
    
    New-PWIndexServer SomeName SomeHostname
    
    If no GUID is specified, the cmdlet creates it automatically.
    
    Name     HostName     ServiceUrl GUID ----     --------     ---------- 
    ----
    SomeName SomeHostname            875603be-af07-40c4-ac6a-1145c0a14c8f
    -------------------------- Example 2 --------------------------
    
    New-PWIndexServer SomeName SomeHostname -GUID 
    11111111-2222-3333-4444-555555555555
    
    A GUID can be explicitly specified.
    
    Name     HostName     ServiceUrl GUID ----     --------     ---------- 
    ----
    SomeName SomeHostname            11111111-2222-3333-4444-555555555555

## New-PWLogin


NAME
    New-PWLogin
    
SYNOPSIS
    Opens a ProjectWise datasource connection.
    
    
SYNTAX
    New-PWLogin [[-DatasourceName] <string>] [[-UserName] <string>] 
    [[-Password] <SecureString>] [[-UseGui]] 
    [[-DoNotCreateWorkingDirectory]] [[-BentleyIMS]] [[-LoadWRE]] 
    [[-NonAdminLogin]] [[-Token] <string>] [<CommonParameters>]
    
    
DESCRIPTION
    Opens a connection to a ProjectWise datasource. Specify a datasource 
    name or select one interactively.
    
    Use the sign-in options that match your account, including Bentley IMS 
    when required.
    

PARAMETERS
    -DatasourceName <string>
        Datasource to log into (Required). ServerName:DatasourceName format 
        is suggested though will probably work with just display name.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    0
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -UserName <string>
        User name. Leave blank for single sign-on
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    1
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -Password <SecureString>
        Password. Leave blank for single sign-on
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    2
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -UseGui [<SwitchParameter>]
        Flag to force Login GUI
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    3
        Default value                False
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -DoNotCreateWorkingDirectory [<SwitchParameter>]
        Do not create working directory
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    4
        Default value                False
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -BentleyIMS [<SwitchParameter>]
        Use Connection Client to log in
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    5
        Default value                False
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -LoadWRE [<SwitchParameter>]
        Load Workflow Rules Engine
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    6
        Default value                False
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -NonAdminLogin [<SwitchParameter>]
        Login as a non-admin user. User must be member of "PowerShell Users" 
        group in the datasource.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    7
        Default value                False
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -Token <string>
        Use with -BentleyIMS
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    8
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    <CommonParameters>
        This cmdlet supports the common parameters: Verbose, Debug,
        ErrorAction, ErrorVariable, WarningAction, WarningVariable,
        OutBuffer, PipelineVariable, and OutVariable. For more information, 
    see 
        about_CommonParameters 
    (https:/go.microsoft.com/fwlink/?LinkID=113216). 
    
INPUTS
    
OUTPUTS
    System.Boolean
        
    
    
NOTES
    
    
        
    
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 1 --------------------------
    
    #logical user login
    $encryptedPassword = Read-Host -Prompt "Enter Password:" -AsSecureString
    New-PWLogin Server:DSN -UserName UserName -Password $encryptedPassword
    
    
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 2 --------------------------
    
    #Single sign-on login. Non-admin login. User must be in "PowerShell 
    Users" group.
    New-PWLogin Server:DSN -DoNotCreateWorkingDirectory -NonAdminLogin
    
    
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 3 --------------------------
    
    #IMS login. Load Workflow Rules Engine.
    New-PWLogin Server:DSN -BentleyIMS -LoadWRE
    
    
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 4 --------------------------
    
    #Use dialog to login. Non-admin login. User must be in "PowerShell 
    Users" group.
    New-PWLogin -UseGUI -NonAdminLogin
