## New-PWLookup


NAME
    New-PWLookup
    
SYNOPSIS
    Registeres a new lookup.
    
    
SYNTAX
    New-PWLookup [-LookupName] <String> [-Columns] <String[]> 
    [-DefaultValueColumn] <String> [[-DefaultDescriptionColumn] <String>] 
    [[-SortColumns] <IOrderedDictionary>] [[-EnvironmentName] <String>] 
    [[-PartitionFilter] <IOrderedDictionary>] [<CommonParameters>]
    
    
DESCRIPTION
    The New-PWLookup cmdlet registers a new lookup, with either default data 
    location or bound to an existing Attribute Envronment.
    

PARAMETERS
    -LookupName <String>
        The name of new lookup to register. Note: PW has lenght and 
        character restrictions for lookup names. Amongst others, "DMS." 
        prefix is forbidden.
        
        Required?                    true
        Position?                    0
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       True (ByPropertyName)
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -Columns <String[]>
        Lookup column list.  Note: PW has lenght and character restrictions 
        for column names. For lookups that are reading data from PW 
        environment table, the column names must match environment attribute 
        (column) names.
        
        Required?                    true
        Position?                    1
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       True (ByPropertyName)
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -DefaultValueColumn <String>
        The name of column to use as value column by default.
        
        Required?                    true
        Position?                    2
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       True (ByPropertyName)
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -DefaultDescriptionColumn <String>
        The name of column to use as description column by default.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    3
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       True (ByPropertyName)
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -SortColumns <IOrderedDictionary>
        Optional. The ordering (column_name = 'asc'/'desc'  list) to apply 
        on lookup select results.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    4
        Default value                []
        Accept pipeline input?       True (ByPropertyName)
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -EnvironmentName <String>
        Optional. The name of PW attribute environment to use as a source. 
        Only to be supplied for lookups that will read data from PW 
        environment table. If not supplied or empty, will register a lookup 
        reading from the default lookup table.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    5
        Default value                Null
        Accept pipeline input?       True (ByPropertyName)
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -PartitionFilter <IOrderedDictionary>
        Optional. The filter (column_name = value list) to apply on lookup 
        table.  Only allowed for lookups that are reading data from the PW 
        environment table.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    6
        Default value                []
        Accept pipeline input?       True (ByPropertyName)
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    <CommonParameters>
        This cmdlet supports the common parameters: Verbose, Debug,
        ErrorAction, ErrorVariable, WarningAction, WarningVariable,
        OutBuffer, PipelineVariable, and OutVariable. For more information, 
    see 
        about_CommonParameters 
    (https:/go.microsoft.com/fwlink/?LinkID=113216). 
    
INPUTS
    None
        You cannot pipe objects to this cmdlet.
    
    
OUTPUTS
    None
        Does not return anything.
    
    
NOTES
    
    
        
    
    ------------------- Register Standard Lookup -------------------
    
    $columns = "country_code", "state_code", "region_code", "name", 
    "county", "municipality", "sort_order"
    $sort = [ordered]@{ 'sort_order' = 'asc' }
    New-PWLookup -LookupName "my.empty.towns" -Columns $columns 
    -DefaultValueColumn "name" -DefaultDescriptionColumn "municipality" 
    -SortColumns $sort
    
    Registers a new lookup named "my.empty.towns"
    
    True
    ----------------- Register Environment Lookup -----------------
    
    $columns = "PW_CODE", "PW_DESCRIPTION", "PW_MODEL", "PW_DRAWING", 
    "PW_DOCUMENT", "cde_state"
    $defaultValueColumn = "PW_CODE"
    $defaultDescriptionColumn = "PW_DESCRIPTION"
    $sort = [ordered]@{ 'PW_SORTORDER' = 'asc' }
    $Environment = "Lookup Table - General Attributes"
    $filter = [ordered]@{ 'PW_FILTER' = 'Status' }
    New-PWLookup -LookupName "LKP.Status" -Columns $columns 
    -DefaultValueColumn $defaultValueColumn -DefaultDescriptionColumn 
    $defaultDescriptionColumn -SortColumns $sort -EnvironmentName 
    $Environment -PartitionFilter $filter
    
    registers a new lookup "LKP.Status" that will be filled by creating 
    documents in environment "Lookup Table - General Attributes"
    
RELATED LINKS
    Remove-PWLookup 
    Set-PWLookupData 





--

## New-PWStorage


NAME
    New-PWStorage
    
SYNOPSIS
    Creates a new Storage Area in the current ProjectWise Datasource.
    
    
SYNTAX
    New-PWStorage [-Name] <String> [-HostName] <String> [-Path] <String> 
    [-AutoStorageLocation] [-Description <String>] [-Type <StorageType>] 
    [<CommonParameters>]
    
    
DESCRIPTION
    The New-PWStorage cmdlet creates a new Storage Area in the current 
    ProjectWise Datasource.
    
    Note that all Storage Names and Paths must be unique in a ProjectWise 
    Datasource.
    

PARAMETERS
    -AutoStorageLocation [<SwitchParameter>]
        Either StorageLocation entry must be automatically added into 
        configuration. Not by default.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    named
        Default value                False
        Accept pipeline input?       True (ByPropertyName)
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -Description <String>
        The Name of the Storage Area to create. (must be unique!)
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    named
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       True (ByPropertyName)
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -HostName <String>
        The hostname of the machine where the new Storage Area will be 
        created.
        
        Required?                    true
        Position?                    1
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       True (ByPropertyName)
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -Name <String>
        The Name of the Storage Area to create. (must be unique!)
        
        Required?                    true
        Position?                    0
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       True (ByPropertyName)
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -Path <String>
        The full path of the Storage Area's base directory in File System.
        
        If the target is Azure Blob Storage (-Driver 1) then the path must 
        be formatted the following way: 
        AzureStorageAccount:AzureStorageContainer where AzureStorageAccount 
        is Azure storage account, AzureStorageContainer is Azure storage 
        container name.
        
        Required?                    true
        Position?                    2
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       True (ByPropertyName)
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -Type <StorageType>
        The type of the storage.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    named
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       True (ByPropertyName)
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    <CommonParameters>
        This cmdlet supports the common parameters: Verbose, Debug,
        ErrorAction, ErrorVariable, WarningAction, WarningVariable,
        OutBuffer, PipelineVariable, and OutVariable. For more information, 
    see 
        about_CommonParameters 
    (https:/go.microsoft.com/fwlink/?LinkID=113216). 
    
INPUTS
    Bentley.ProjectWise.PowerShell.Common.Storage
        You can pipe Storage objects to New-PWStorage to clone a Storage 
        Area.
    
    
OUTPUTS
    Bentley.ProjectWise.PowerShell.Common.Storage
        New-PWStorage returns an object that represents the new Storage Area.
    
    
NOTES
    
    
        New-PWStorage requires an open ProjectWise Connection in the current 
        thread.  You can open a Connection with Open-PWConnection before 
        calling New-PWStorage.
        
        New-PWStorage requires an Admin session.  To open an admin session, 
        use the -Admin flag when opening the ProjectWise Connection with 
        Open-PWConnection.
    
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 1 --------------------------
    
    New-PWStorage -Name "New Storage Name" -Host "PWHOST" -Path 
    "C:\ProjectWise Storage Path" -Description "This parameter is optional"
    
    Creates ProjectWise Storage at the specified ProjectWise server host 
    with the specified name, storage directory path and description 
    (optional)
    
    StorageID Name              Description       Host             Path 
    --------- ----              -----------       ----             ----     
    1     New Storage Name  This parameter... PWHOST           
    C:\ProjectWis...
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 2 --------------------------
    
    New-PWStorage -Name "New Storage Name 2" -Host "PWHOST" -Path 
    "C:\ProjectWise Storage Path 2"
    
    Creates ProjectWise Storage at the specified ProjectWise server host 
    with the specified name, storage directory path and description 
    (optional)
    
    StorageID Name              Description       Host             Path 
    --------- ----              -----------       ----             ----     
    2     New Storage Na...  PWHOST           C:\ProjectWis...
    
RELATED LINKS
    Get-PWStorage

## New-PWStorageLocation


NAME

SYNOPSIS
    Adds a new StorageLocation entry into configuration of Caching Server.
    
    
SYNTAX
    New-PWStorageLocation [-HostName] <String> [-Path] <String> 
    [-ForceCreation] [<CommonParameters>]
    
    
DESCRIPTION
    The New-PWStorageLocation cmdlet creates a new configuration entry 
    StorageLocation in the specified Caching Server.
    

PARAMETERS
    -ForceCreation [<SwitchParameter>]
        Either StorageLocation entry must be added into configuration 
        despite entry which covers it exactly or through subpath exists.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    named
        Default value                False
        Accept pipeline input?       True (ByPropertyName)
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -HostName <String>
        The hostname of the machine where the new StorageLocation entry will 
        be created.
        
        Required?                    true
        Position?                    1
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       True (ByPropertyName)
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -Path <String>
        The full path of the base for Storage Areas' directories in Windows 
        File System.
        
        Required?                    true
        Position?                    2
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       True (ByPropertyName)
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    <CommonParameters>
        This cmdlet supports the common parameters: Verbose, Debug,
        ErrorAction, ErrorVariable, WarningAction, WarningVariable,
        OutBuffer, PipelineVariable, and OutVariable. For more information, 
    see 
        about_CommonParameters 
    (https:/go.microsoft.com/fwlink/?LinkID=113216). 
    
INPUTS
    None
        You cannot pipe objects to this cmdlet.
    
    
OUTPUTS
    Boolean
        Returns true if the entry was added or new path is already covered 
        by existing entries.
    
    
NOTES
    
    
        
    
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 1 --------------------------
    
    New-PWStorageLocation -Host "PWHOST" -Path "C:\ProjectWise Storage Path"
    
    Creates StorageLocation entry in the configuration file of PWHOST 
    caching server
    
    True

## New-PWUser


NAME
    New-PWUser
    
SYNOPSIS
    Creates a new User in the current ProjectWise Datasource.
    
    
SYNTAX
    New-PWUser [-UserName] <String> [[-Password] <Object>] [-Identity] 
    <String> [-IdentityProviderName] <String> [-Description <String>] 
    [-Email <String>] [<CommonParameters>]
    
    New-PWUser [-UserName] <String> [[-Password] <Object>] [-Identity] 
    <String> [-IdentityProviderId] <Int32> [-Description <String>] [-Email 
    <String>] [<CommonParameters>]
    
    New-PWUser [-UserName] <String> [[-Password] <Object>] 
    [-WindowsIdentity] <String> [-DomainName] <String> [-Description 
    <String>] [-Email <String>] [<CommonParameters>]
    
    New-PWUser [-UserName] <String> [[-Password] <Object>] 
    [-WindowsIdentity] <String> [-DomainId] <Int32> [-Description <String>] 
    [-Email <String>] [<CommonParameters>]
    
    
DESCRIPTION
    The New-PWUser cmdlet creates a new User in the current ProjectWise 
    Datasource.
    
    By default, New-PWUser creates a native ProjectWise user account in the 
    current ProjectWise Datasource.
    
    You can optionally add a federated identity using -Identity with either 
    -IdentityProviderName or -IdentityProviderId.
    
    You can optionally add a Windows identity using -WindowsIdentity with 
    either -DomainName or -DomainId.
    

PARAMETERS
    -Description <String>
        The Description of the User to create.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    named
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       True (ByPropertyName)
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -Email <String>
        The Email address of the User to create.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    named
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       True (ByPropertyName)
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -Identity <String>
        External identity to associate this user account with.
        
        Required?                    true
        Position?                    2
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       True (ByPropertyName)
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -IdentityProviderName <String>
        The name of the Identity Provider to associate the federated 
        identity with.
        
        Required?                    true
        Position?                    3
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       True (ByPropertyName)
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -IdentityProviderId <Int32>
        The numeric identifier of the Identity Provider to associate the 
        federated identity with.
        
        Required?                    true
        Position?                    3
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       True (ByPropertyName)
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -Password <Object>
        The Password of the User to create (only used for Logical Users). 
        Password can be passed as plain text (String) or SecureString 
        object. It is recommended to use SecureString (see examples).
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    1
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       True (ByPropertyName)
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -WindowsIdentity <String>
        Windows account name to associate this user account with.
        
        Required?                    true
        Position?                    2
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       True (ByPropertyName)
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -UserName <String>
        The UserName of the User to create.
        
        Required?                    true
        Position?                    0
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       True (ByPropertyName)
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -DomainName <String>
        The name of the Windows Domain for -WindowsIdentity.
        
        Required?                    true
        Position?                    3
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       True (ByPropertyName)
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -DomainId <Int32>
        The numeric identifier of the Windows Domain for -WindowsIdentity.
        
        Required?                    true
        Position?                    3
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       True (ByPropertyName)
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    <CommonParameters>
        This cmdlet supports the common parameters: Verbose, Debug,
        ErrorAction, ErrorVariable, WarningAction, WarningVariable,
        OutBuffer, PipelineVariable, and OutVariable. For more information, 
    see 
        about_CommonParameters 
    (https:/go.microsoft.com/fwlink/?LinkID=113216). 
    
INPUTS
    Bentley.ProjectWise.PowerShell.Common.User
        You can pipe User objects to New-PWUser to clone a User.
    
    
OUTPUTS
    Bentley.ProjectWise.PowerShell.Common.User
        New-PWUser returns an object that represents the new User.
    
    
NOTES
    
    
        New-PWUser requires an open ProjectWise Connection in the current 
        thread.  You can open a Connection with Open-PWConnection before 
        calling New-PWUser.
        
        New-PWUser requires an Admin session.  To open an admin session, use 
        the -Admin flag when opening the ProjectWise Connection with 
        Open-PWConnection.
        
        It is recommended to store and pass password as secure string object.
    
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 1 --------------------------
    
    New-PWUser -UserName ANewUser -Password p@ssw0rd -Description "This is 
    optional" -Email "also@optional.com"
    
    Creates a new native user account in the connected ProjectWise 
    datasource.
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 2 --------------------------
    
    New-PWUser -UserName AnotherUser -Identity "external_identity@email.com" 
    -IdentityProviderName "MyIdP"
    
    Creates a new user account and adds a federated identity using provider 
    name.
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 3 --------------------------
    
    New-PWUser -UserName AnotherUser2 -Identity 
    "external_identity2@email.com" -IdentityProviderId 12
    
    Creates a new user account and adds a federated identity using provider 
    id.
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 4 --------------------------
    
    New-PWUser -UserName Win.User -WindowsIdentity "Win.User" -DomainName 
    DOMAIN -Description "Optional" -Email also@optional.com
    
    Creates a new user account and adds a Windows identity using domain name.
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 5 --------------------------
    
    New-PWUser -UserName Win.User2 -WindowsIdentity "Win.User2" -DomainId 5
    
    Creates a new user account and adds a Windows identity using domain id.
    
RELATED LINKS
    Get-PWUser 
    Remove-PWUser 
    Update-PWUser 





--

## New-PWUserList


NAME
    New-PWUserList
    
SYNOPSIS
    Creates a new User List in the current ProjectWise Datasource.
    
    
SYNTAX
    New-PWUserList [-UserListName] <String> [-Type] <UserListType> 
    [-Description <String>] [-Owner <User>] [<CommonParameters>]
    
    
DESCRIPTION
    The New-PWUserList cmdlet creates a new User List in the current 
    ProjectWise Datasource.
    
    By default, New-PWUserList creates a global User List in the current 
    ProjectWise Datasource.  To create an AddressBook UserList with a 
    particular owner, user the the -Owner parameter.
    

PARAMETERS
    -Description <String>
        The Description of the UserList to create.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    named
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       True (ByPropertyName)
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -Owner <User>
        If set, user this User as the "Owner" of the UserList to create 
        (Only valid for AddressBook UserLists)
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    named
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       True (ByValue)
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -Type <UserListType>
        The Type of UserList to create (either AccessControl or AddressBook).
        
        Required?                    true
        Position?                    1
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       True (ByPropertyName)
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -UserListName <String>
        The UserListName of the UserList to create.
        
        Required?                    true
        Position?                    0
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       True (ByPropertyName)
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    <CommonParameters>
        This cmdlet supports the common parameters: Verbose, Debug,
        ErrorAction, ErrorVariable, WarningAction, WarningVariable,
        OutBuffer, PipelineVariable, and OutVariable. For more information, 
    see 
        about_CommonParameters 
    (https:/go.microsoft.com/fwlink/?LinkID=113216). 
    
INPUTS
    Bentley.ProjectWise.PowerShell.Common.UserList
        You can pipe UserList objects to New-PWUserList to clone a UserList.
    
    
OUTPUTS
    Bentley.ProjectWise.PowerShell.Common.UserList
        New-PWUserList returns an object that represents the new UserList.
    
    
NOTES
    
    
        New-PWUserList requires an open ProjectWise Connection in the 
        current thread.  You can open a Connection with Open-PWConnection 
        before calling New-PWUserList.
    
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 1 --------------------------
    
    New-PWUserList -UserListName "A New Access List" -Type AccessControl 
    -Description "This parameter is optional"
    
    Create a new AccessControl UserList
    
    UserListID UserListName       Description       Type          Owner 
    ---------- ------------       -----------       ----          -----     
    1      New Access List    This parameter... AccessControl [global]
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 2 --------------------------
    
    New-PWUserList -UserListName "New Global Mailing List" -Type AddressBook 
    -Description "This parameter is optional"
    
    Create a new (global) AddressBook UserList
    
    UserListID UserListName       Description       Type          Owner 
    ---------- ------------       -----------       ----          -----     
    2      New Global Mail... This parameter... AddressBook   [global]
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 3 --------------------------
    
    Get-PWUser -UserID 1 | New-PWUserList -UserListName "List With Owner" 
    -Type AddressBook
    
    Create a new AddressBook UserList with a User (from the pipeline) as 
    Owner
    
    UserListID UserListName       Description       Type          Owner 
    ---------- ------------       -----------       ----          -----     
    3      List With Owner                      AddressBook   admin
    
RELATED LINKS
    Get-PWUserList 
    Remove-PWUserList 
    Update-PWUserList

## New-PWWorkArea


NAME
    New-PWWorkArea
    
SYNOPSIS
    Creates individual work areas and upgrades existing folders to work 
    areas.
    
    
SYNTAX
    New-PWWorkArea [[-InputFolder] <ProjectWiseFolder[]>] [[-NewFolderPath] 
    <string>] [[-UserListTemplatePrefix] <string>] 
    [[-SkipFolderWorkflowSecurityReplication]] [[-FolderDepthToCopy] <int>] 
    [[-TemplateName] <string>] [[-StorageArea] <string>] 
    [[-BlockSizeInSeconds] <double>] [[-DoNotCopyDocuments]] 
    [[-UserListPrefix] <string>] [[-ProjectProperties] <Hashtable>] 
    [[-UpgradeIfExists]] [[-ProjectType] <string>] [<CommonParameters>]
    
    
DESCRIPTION
    Takes a path or folders from pipeline, a template name and a hashtable 
    of project properties in order to create a new rich project or upgrade 
    an existing folder.
    

PARAMETERS
    -InputFolder <ProjectWiseFolder[]>
        Array of folders from pipeline.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    0
        Default value                PWPS_DAB.CommonTypes+ProjectWiseFolder[]
        Accept pipeline input?       True (ByValue)
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -NewFolderPath <string>
        Full folder path of new project to create. Error if already exists.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    1
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -TemplateName <string>
        Template name. Error if not found.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    2
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -StorageArea <string>
        Storage area name is required since that is the thing most likely to 
        vary from the template.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    3
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -BlockSizeInSeconds <double>
        Geospatial block size to create if latitude and longitude properties 
        are provided (not yet implemented).
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    4
        Default value                0
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -DoNotCopyDocuments [<SwitchParameter>]
        Will copy documents from the template by default. Set this switch to 
        prevent.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    5
        Default value                False
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -UserListPrefix <string>
        If provided, user lists that start with '[TEMPLATE]' will be copied 
        and changed to use this value.
        
        The idea is to create project-specific user lists.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    6
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -UserListTemplatePrefix <string>
        Defaults to '[TEMPLATE]'. Can be used to override that string.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    10
        Default value                [TEMPLATE]
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -ProjectProperties <Hashtable>
        Hashtable with project properties.
        
        In script would be like this: @{PROJECT_Project_Name = "My new 
        project"; PROJECT_Project_Number = "00007"; 
        PROJECT_Project_Manager_Email = "joe.blow@bentley.com" }
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    7
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -UpgradeIfExists [<SwitchParameter>]
        Upgrade the folder if it exists.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    8
        Default value                False
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -ProjectType <string>
        ProjectType for updates. You may get the possible by using the 
        Get-PWRichProjectTypes cmdlet and looking at the class names 
        returned.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    9
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -SkipFolderWorkflowSecurityReplication [<SwitchParameter>]
        Skip the Folder + Workflow security replication which can be quite 
        time consuming. Workflow security whould probably be set globally 
        anyway...
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    10
        Default value                False
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -FolderDepthToCopy <int>
        Defaults to -1, all folders. 0 will just copy top node, 1 will copy 
        folders one level down, 2 will copy folders two levels down, etc.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    11
        Default value                -1
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    <CommonParameters>
        This cmdlet supports the common parameters: Verbose, Debug,
        ErrorAction, ErrorVariable, WarningAction, WarningVariable,
        OutBuffer, PipelineVariable, and OutVariable. For more information, 
    see 
        about_CommonParameters 
    (https:/go.microsoft.com/fwlink/?LinkID=113216). 
    
INPUTS
    PWPS_DAB.CommonTypes+ProjectWiseFolder[]
        Array of folders from pipeline.
    
    
OUTPUTS
    
NOTES
    
    
        
    
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 1 --------------------------
    
    #This creates a new project from a path using a project template
    New-PWWorkArea -NewFolderPath "Test Projects 5\From Script\Project N" 
    -StorageArea Storage1 -TemplateName "Civil Template" -DoNotCopyDocuments 
    -ProjectProperties @{PROJECT_Project_Name='A Project Name'; 
    PROJECT_Project_Number='YYYYY'; 
    PROJECT_Project_Manager_Email='dave.brumbaugh@bentley.com'}
    
    
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 2 --------------------------
    
    #This returns a folder object so you could save it to a variable and use 
    it for additional operations.
    New-PWWorkArea -NewFolderPath "Test Projects 5\From Script\Project N" 
    -UpgradeIfExists -ProjectType "Civil"
    
    
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 3 --------------------------
    
    # This upgrades all folders in datasource starting with "Civil 
    Project..." to work areas of type "Civil" and sets the project 
    properties.
    Get-PWFolders -FolderName "Civil Project%" | New-PWWorkArea 
    -UpgradeIfExists -ProjectType "Civil" -ProjectProperties 
    @{PROJECT_Project_Name='A Project Name'; PROJECT_Project_Number='YYYYY'; 
    PROJECT_Project_Manager_Email='dave.brumbaugh@bentley.com'}

## New-PWWorkAreaFromTemplate


NAME
    New-PWWorkAreaFromTemplate
    
SYNOPSIS
    Creates a new work area from a template project.
    
    
SYNTAX
    New-PWWorkAreaFromTemplate [-InputFolder] <ProjectWiseFolder> 
    [-NewProjectName] <string> [-TemplateName] <string> [-StorageArea] 
    <string> [-NewProjectDescription <string>] [-UpdateAllStorageAreas] 
    [-DoNotCopyDocuments] [<CommonParameters>]
    
    New-PWWorkAreaFromTemplate [-NewFolderPathName] <string> 
    [-NewProjectName] <string> [-TemplateName] <string> [-StorageArea] 
    <string> [-NewProjectDescription <string>] [-UpdateAllStorageAreas] 
    [-DoNotCopyDocuments] [<CommonParameters>]
    
    
DESCRIPTION
    Accepts a folder object from the pipeline or a project path including 
    the new project name.
    

PARAMETERS
    -InputFolder <ProjectWiseFolder>
        Folder object from pipeline to create new Work Area in. Requires the 
        new project name to be included in the NewProjectName parameter.
        
        Required?                    true
        Position?                    0
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       True (ByValue)
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -NewProjectName <string>
        Name of new Work Area.
        
        Required?                    true
        Position?                    1
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -NewFolderPathName <string>
        Full folder path and name of new Work Area to create. Error if 
        already exists.
        
        Required?                    true
        Position?                    0
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -NewProjectDescription <string>
        Project description for new Work Area.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    named
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -TemplateName <string>
        Name of Template project to be used for new Work Area.
        
        Required?                    true
        Position?                    2
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -StorageArea <string>
        Storage area name to be used when creating new Work Area. Required. 
        Error if not found. Only the project folder will use this storage 
        area unless the UpdateAllStorageAreas switch parameter is included.
        
        Required?                    true
        Position?                    3
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -UpdateAllStorageAreas [<SwitchParameter>]
        When included, will use the provided storage area for ALL folders in 
        the new Work Area.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    named
        Default value                False
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -DoNotCopyDocuments [<SwitchParameter>]
        Will copy documents from the template by default. Set this switch to 
        prevent.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    named
        Default value                False
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    <CommonParameters>
        This cmdlet supports the common parameters: Verbose, Debug,
        ErrorAction, ErrorVariable, WarningAction, WarningVariable,
        OutBuffer, PipelineVariable, and OutVariable. For more information, 
    see 
        about_CommonParameters 
    (https:/go.microsoft.com/fwlink/?LinkID=113216). 
    
INPUTS
    PWPS_DAB.CommonTypes+ProjectWiseFolder
        Folder object from pipeline to create new Work Area in. Requires the 
        new project name to be included in the NewProjectName parameter.
    
    
OUTPUTS
    PWPS_DAB.CommonTypes+ProjectWiseFolder
        
    
    
NOTES
    
    
        
    
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 1 --------------------------
    
    Example usage for creating a new project from a folder object with the 
    option to NOT copy documents. Returns a folder object for the new 
    project created.
    New-PWWorkAreaFromTemplate -InputFolder (Get-PWFolders -FolderPath 
    'PowerShell' -NewProjectName 'MyNewProject' -TemplateName "Civil 
    Template"  -StorageArea 'Storage_01' -DoNotCopyDocuments -Verbose
    
    
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 2 --------------------------
    
    Example usage for creating a new project from a folder path and name. 
    Also, includes an optional description, and the option to update the 
    storage area for all sub-folders. Returns a folder object for the new 
    project created.
    New-PWWorkArea -NewFolderPathName "PowerShell\MyNewProject" 
    -NewProjectDescription 'My New Project' -TemplateName "Civil Template"  
    -StorageArea 'Storage_01' -UpdateAllStorageAreas -Verbose

## New-PWWorkAreasFromSpreadsheet


NAME
    New-PWWorkAreasFromSpreadsheet
    
SYNOPSIS
    Creates or updates Work Areas from a spreadsheet containing project 
    definitions and properties.
    
    
SYNTAX
    New-PWWorkAreasFromSpreadsheet [-InputFileName] <String> 
    [-DefaultNewParentPath] <String> [-DefaultTemplateName] <String> 
    [-DefaultStorageArea] <String> [[-UserListTemplatePrefix] <String>] 
    [[-BlockSizeInSeconds] <Double>] [[-CreateAtRoot]] [[-ProjectTypes] 
    <String[]>] [[-SkipFolderWorkflowSecurityReplication]] 
    [[-FolderDepthToCopy] <Integer>] [<CommonParameters>]
    
    
DESCRIPTION
    This cmdlet reads project data from an Excel spreadsheet (.xlsx) and 
    creates new Work Areas or updates existing folders in ProjectWise. Each 
    worksheet in the spreadsheet represents a Project Type, and each row 
    represents a Work Area to be created or updated.
    
    The worksheet name must match a valid Project Type name in your 
    ProjectWise datasource (e.g., "General", "Civil", "Structural"). A 
    `FolderName` column is required to specify the name of each Work Area to 
    create.
    
    Optional columns for folder configuration include: ExistingProjectPath 
    (path to an existing project for reference), FolderDescription 
    (description for the Work Area), NewParentPath (parent folder path, 
    overrides -DefaultNewParentPath), TemplateName (template to use, 
    overrides -DefaultTemplateName), NewStorage (storage area, overrides 
    -DefaultStorageArea), CopyDocuments (set to "1", "true", or "yes" to 
    copy documents from template), TemplateSecurityColumn (name of column 
    containing user list replacement value), and ConnectedProjectId (GUID of 
    a Connected Project to associate).
    
    Optional columns for geospatial data include: ProjectLatitude (latitude 
    coordinate, -90 to 90) and ProjectLongitude (longitude coordinate, -180 
    to 180).
    
    Any column matching a Project Type property name will be used to set 
    properties on the Work Area. Property columns typically follow the 
    pattern `PROJECT_PropertyName` (e.g., `PROJECT_Budget`, 
    `PROJECT_Project_Manager`, `PROJECT_Start_Date`).
    
    If a Work Area with the specified path does not exist, it will be 
    created from the specified template. If a folder exists but is not a 
    Work Area, it will be upgraded to a Work Area of the specified Project 
    Type. If a Work Area already exists, its properties will be updated with 
    values from the spreadsheet. Row-level values for NewParentPath, 
    TemplateName, and NewStorage override the default command-line 
    parameters.
    
    The cmdlet supports dynamic security assignment through user list 
    template replacement. In your template, create user lists with names 
    containing the prefix pattern (default: `[TEMPLATE]`), e.g., `[TEMPLATE] 
    Project Team`. In your spreadsheet, add a column for the replacement 
    value (e.g., `ProjectCode` with value `PROJ001`). Add a 
    `TemplateSecurityColumn` column and set its value to the column name 
    containing the replacement (e.g., `ProjectCode`). When the Work Area is 
    created, `[TEMPLATE]` will be replaced with `PROJ001` in user list names.
    

PARAMETERS
    -InputFileName <String>
        Full path to the input Excel spreadsheet (.xlsx file). The 
        spreadsheet must contain at least one worksheet named after a valid 
        Project Type, with a "FolderName" column specifying the Work Area 
        names to create.
        
        Required?                    true
        Position?                    0
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -DefaultNewParentPath <String>
        Default parent folder path where new Work Areas will be created. 
        This path is used when the "NewParentPath" column is empty for a row 
        in the spreadsheet. The path must exist or will be created 
        automatically.
        
        Required?                    true
        Position?                    1
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -DefaultTemplateName <String>
        Default template name used to create new Work Areas. This template 
        is used when the "TemplateName" column is empty for a row in the 
        spreadsheet. The template must exist in the datasource.
        
        Required?                    true
        Position?                    2
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -DefaultStorageArea <String>
        Default storage area name for new Work Areas. This storage area is 
        used when the "NewStorage" column is empty for a row in the 
        spreadsheet.
        
        Required?                    true
        Position?                    3
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -UserListTemplatePrefix <String>
        Prefix pattern used to identify user lists that should have their 
        names modified during security replication. When a user list name 
        contains this prefix, it will be replaced with the value from the 
        column specified in "TemplateSecurityColumn". Default value is 
        `[TEMPLATE]`.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    4
        Default value                [TEMPLATE]
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -BlockSizeInSeconds <Double>
        Size in arc-seconds for the geospatial location square. When 
        provided along with "ProjectLatitude" and "ProjectLongitude" columns 
        in the spreadsheet, creates a geo-location square of this size 
        centered on the specified coordinates. Typical values range from 0.5 
        to 5.0 seconds. One arc-second equals approximately 30 meters at the 
        equator.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    5
        Default value                0
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -CreateAtRoot [<SwitchParameter>]
        When specified, creates all Work Areas at the root level of the 
        datasource, ignoring any "NewParentPath" column values and the 
        -DefaultNewParentPath parameter.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    6
        Default value                False
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -ProjectTypes <String[]>
        Array of Project Type names to process. When specified, only 
        worksheets with names matching these Project Types will be 
        processed. Worksheet names must match exactly (case-insensitive). If 
        not specified, all worksheets with valid Project Type names are 
        processed.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    7
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -SkipFolderWorkflowSecurityReplication [<SwitchParameter>]
        When specified, skips the folder and workflow security replication 
        step during Work Area creation. This can significantly reduce 
        processing time. Use this option when workflow security is 
        configured globally or when security replication is not needed.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    8
        Default value                False
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -FolderDepthToCopy <Integer>
        Controls how many levels of subfolders are copied from the template 
        when creating new Work Areas. A value of -1 copies all folders 
        (default), 0 copies the top node only with no subfolders, 1 copies 
        one level of subfolders, 2 copies two levels, and so on. This 
        parameter only has effect when the template contains subfolders.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    9
        Default value                -1
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    <CommonParameters>
        This cmdlet supports the common parameters: Verbose, Debug,
        ErrorAction, ErrorVariable, WarningAction, WarningVariable,
        OutBuffer, PipelineVariable, and OutVariable. For more information, 
    see 
        about_CommonParameters 
    (https:/go.microsoft.com/fwlink/?LinkID=113216). 
    
INPUTS
    None
        
    
    
OUTPUTS
    System.Collections.Generic.List[PWPS_DAB.Common.CommonTypes+ProjectWiseFo
    lder]
        Returns a list of ProjectWiseFolder objects representing the Work 
        Areas that were created or updated. Each object contains properties 
        such as ProjectID, Name, Description, FullPath, ProjectType, 
        Storage, IsRichProject, ConnectedProjectId, and ProjectProperties.
    
    
NOTES
    
    
        Worksheet names must exactly match a valid Project Type name 
        configured in your ProjectWise datasource. The `FolderName` column 
        is required and must contain the name for each Work Area to be 
        created. Include columns matching your Project Type's property names 
        to set values during creation or update.
        
        Date columns should use a parseable format such as `YYYY-MM-DD 
        HH:MM:SS` (e.g., `2024-01-15 08:00:00`) or standard date formats 
        recognized by .NET DateTime.TryParse().
        
        Use `-SkipFolderWorkflowSecurityReplication` when workflow security 
        is set globally to improve performance. Use `-FolderDepthToCopy` to 
        limit the number of subfolders copied from templates. Use 
        `-ProjectTypes` to process only specific worksheets when dealing 
        with large spreadsheets.
        
        ## RELATED LINKS
    
    ------------- EXAMPLE 1: Basic Work Area Creation -------------
    
    New-PWWorkAreasFromSpreadsheet -InputFileName "C:\Data\WorkAreas.xlsx" 
    -DefaultNewParentPath "Projects\2024" -DefaultTemplateName "General" 
    -DefaultStorageArea "Storage01"
    
    Creates Work Areas from the spreadsheet using the specified defaults. 
    Work Areas will be created under "Projects\2024" using the "General" 
    template and "Storage01" storage area.
    -------- EXAMPLE 2: Process Specific Project Types Only --------
    
    New-PWWorkAreasFromSpreadsheet -InputFileName "C:\Data\WorkAreas.xlsx" 
    -DefaultNewParentPath "Projects\2024" -DefaultTemplateName "General" 
    -DefaultStorageArea "Storage01" -ProjectTypes "Civil", "Structural"
    
    Processes only the worksheets named "Civil" and "Structural" from the 
    spreadsheet, ignoring all other worksheets.
    ------- EXAMPLE 3: Create Work Areas at Datasource Root -------
    
    New-PWWorkAreasFromSpreadsheet -InputFileName "C:\Data\WorkAreas.xlsx" 
    -DefaultNewParentPath "Projects\2024" -DefaultTemplateName "General" 
    -DefaultStorageArea "Storage01" -CreateAtRoot
    
    Creates Work Areas at the root level of the datasource, ignoring any 
    NewParentPath values in the spreadsheet or the -DefaultNewParentPath 
    parameter.
    ---- EXAMPLE 4: Create Work Areas with Geospatial Location ----
    
    New-PWWorkAreasFromSpreadsheet -InputFileName "C:\Data\WorkAreas.xlsx" 
    -DefaultNewParentPath "Projects\2024" -DefaultTemplateName "General" 
    -DefaultStorageArea "Storage01" -BlockSizeInSeconds 1.0
    
    Creates Work Areas and assigns geospatial locations based on 
    `ProjectLatitude` and `ProjectLongitude` columns in the spreadsheet. A 
    geo-location square of 1.0 arc-second will be created centered on each 
    coordinate.
    -- EXAMPLE 5: Skip Security Replication for Faster Processing --
    
    New-PWWorkAreasFromSpreadsheet -InputFileName "C:\Data\WorkAreas.xlsx" 
    -DefaultNewParentPath "Projects\2024" -DefaultTemplateName "General" 
    -DefaultStorageArea "Storage01" -SkipFolderWorkflowSecurityReplication
    
    Creates Work Areas but skips the folder and workflow security 
    replication step, which can significantly reduce processing time. Use 
    this when workflow security is configured globally.
    ----- EXAMPLE 6: Control Folder Depth Copied from Template -----
    
    New-PWWorkAreasFromSpreadsheet -InputFileName "C:\Data\WorkAreas.xlsx" 
    -DefaultNewParentPath "Projects\2024" -DefaultTemplateName "General" 
    -DefaultStorageArea "Storage01" -FolderDepthToCopy 2
    
    Creates Work Areas and copies subfolders from the template up to 2 
    levels deep. If the template has a nested subfolder structure, only the 
    first 2 levels will be copied to each new Work Area.
    - EXAMPLE 7: Use Custom User List Template Prefix for Security -
    
    New-PWWorkAreasFromSpreadsheet -InputFileName "C:\Data\WorkAreas.xlsx" 
    -DefaultNewParentPath "Projects\2024" -DefaultTemplateName "General" 
    -DefaultStorageArea "Storage01" -UserListTemplatePrefix "[PROJECT]"
    
    Creates Work Areas using a custom user list template prefix `[PROJECT]` 
    instead of the default `[TEMPLATE]`. User lists containing `[PROJECT]` 
    in their name will be replaced with values from the 
    TemplateSecurityColumn.
    ------- EXAMPLE 8: Spreadsheet with Row-Level Overrides -------
    
    New-PWWorkAreasFromSpreadsheet -InputFileName 
    "C:\Data\WorkAreasWithOverrides.xlsx" -DefaultNewParentPath 
    "Projects\Default" -DefaultTemplateName "BasicTemplate" 
    -DefaultStorageArea "Storage01"
    
    Spreadsheet columns can override defaults on a per-row basis: - 
    NewParentPath : Override -DefaultNewParentPath for specific rows - 
    TemplateName : Override -DefaultTemplateName for specific rows - 
    NewStorage : Override -DefaultStorageArea for specific rows - 
    CopyDocuments : Set to "1", "true", or "yes" to copy documents from 
    template - TemplateSecurityColumn : Specify which column contains the 
    user list replacement value - ConnectedProjectId : GUID of a Connected 
    Project to associate with the Work Area
    
    Rows with blank values for these columns will use the default 
    parameters; rows with values will use the row-specific values.
    EXAMPLE 9: Template Security Replacement Using TemplateSecurityColumn
    
    New-PWWorkAreasFromSpreadsheet -InputFileName 
    "C:\Data\WorkAreasWithSecurity.xlsx" -DefaultNewParentPath 
    "Projects\2024" -DefaultTemplateName "SecuredTemplate" 
    -DefaultStorageArea "Storage01" -UserListTemplatePrefix "[TEMPLATE]"
    
    To use dynamic security replacement:
    
    1. In your template, create user lists with names containing 
    `[TEMPLATE]` (e.g., `[TEMPLATE] Project Team`) 2. In your spreadsheet, 
    add a column for the replacement value (e.g., `ProjectCode` with value 
    `PROJ001`) 3. Add a `TemplateSecurityColumn` column and set its value to 
    the column name containing the replacement (e.g., `ProjectCode`) 4. When 
    the Work Area is created, `[TEMPLATE]` will be replaced with `PROJ001` 
    in user list names
    
    This enables project-specific security by creating unique user lists for 
    each Work Area.
    
RELATED LINKS

## Purge-PWDocumentLocalCopy


NAME
    Purge-PWDocumentLocalCopy
    
SYNOPSIS
    Purge-PWDocumentLocalCopy removes the local working directory copy of 
    the specified document(s).
    
    
SYNTAX
    Purge-PWDocumentLocalCopy [-InputDocument] <ProjectWiseDocument[]> 
    [[-UserName] <string>] [<CommonParameters>]
    
    
DESCRIPTION
    Purge-PWDocumentLocalCopy removes the local working directory copy of 
    the specified document(s). Documents can be passed via the pipeline.
    

PARAMETERS
    -InputDocument <ProjectWiseDocument[]>
        Input array of ProjectWiseDocument objects from the pipeline 
        (Required). Use Get-PWDocumentBySearch to feed pipeline.
        
        Required?                    true
        Position?                    0
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       True (ByValue)
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -UserName <string>
        
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    1
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    <CommonParameters>
        This cmdlet supports the common parameters: Verbose, Debug,
        ErrorAction, ErrorVariable, WarningAction, WarningVariable,
        OutBuffer, PipelineVariable, and OutVariable. For more information, 
    see 
        about_CommonParameters 
    (https:/go.microsoft.com/fwlink/?LinkID=113216). 
    
INPUTS
    PWPS_DAB.CommonTypes+ProjectWiseDocument[]
        Input array of ProjectWiseDocument objects from the pipeline 
        (Required). Use Get-PWDocumentBySearch to feed pipeline.
    
    
OUTPUTS
    
NOTES
    
    
        
    
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 1 --------------------------
    
    The following will purge the local documents passed via the pipeline for 
    the current user.
    Get-PWDocumentsBySearch -FolderPath "Projects\Test" | 
    Purge-PWDocumentLocalCopy -Verbose
    
    
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 2 --------------------------
    
    The following will purge the local documents passed via the pipeline for 
    the provided user.
    Get-PWDocumentsBySearch -FolderPath "Projects\Test" | 
    Purge-PWDocumentLocalCopy -UserName 'powershellUser' -Verbose

## Remove-CloudProject


NAME
    Remove-CloudProject
    
SYNOPSIS
    Removes Bentley Infrastructure Cloud Project.
    
    
SYNTAX
    Remove-CloudProject [[-InputFolders] <ProjectWiseFolder[]>] [-OIDCToken] 
    <OIDCAuthToken> [[-ClearAssociation]] [<CommonParameters>]
    
    Remove-CloudProject [[-CloudProjectId] <string>] [-OIDCToken] 
    <OIDCAuthToken> [[-ClearAssociation]] [<CommonParameters>]
    
    
DESCRIPTION
    Removes Bentley Infrastructure Cloud Project.  Optionally removes 
    associations with work areas.
    

PARAMETERS
    -InputFolders <ProjectWiseFolder[]>
        Array of ProjectWise folders from pipeline.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    0
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       True (ByValue)
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -CloudProjectId <string>
        Bentley Infrastructure Cloud Project ID
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    0
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       True (ByValue)
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -OIDCToken <OIDCAuthToken>
        Token from Get-OIDCToken -Authority https://ims.bentley.com
        
        Required?                    true
        Position?                    1
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -ClearAssociation [<SwitchParameter>]
        Clears all found associations of the Bentley Infrastructure Cloud 
        project to work areas. You must be logged into the datasource(s) of 
        the work areas.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    2
        Default value                False
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    <CommonParameters>
        This cmdlet supports the common parameters: Verbose, Debug,
        ErrorAction, ErrorVariable, WarningAction, WarningVariable,
        OutBuffer, PipelineVariable, and OutVariable. For more information, 
    see 
        about_CommonParameters 
    (https:/go.microsoft.com/fwlink/?LinkID=113216). 
    
INPUTS
    PWPS_DAB.CommonTypes+ProjectWiseFolder[]
        Array of ProjectWise folders from pipeline.
    
    System.String
        Bentley Infrastructure Cloud Project ID
    
    
OUTPUTS
    
NOTES
    
    
        
    
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 1 --------------------------
    
    #Remove a Bentley Infrastructure Cloud project associated with a chosen 
    work area.
     Also clears the association.
    
    New-PWLogin
    $OIDCToken=Get-OIDCToken -Authority https://ims.bentley.com
    $WorkArea=Get-PWWorkAreas -FolderPath 
    (Show-PWFolderBrowserDialog).FullPath
    Remove-CloudProject -InputFolders $WorkArea -OIDCToken $OIDCToken 
    -ClearAssociation
    
    
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 2 --------------------------
    
    #Remove a Bentley Infrastructure Cloud project given its ID.
    $OIDCToken=Get-OIDCToken -Authority https://ims.bentley.com
    Remove-CloudProject -CloudProjectId xxxxxxxx-xxxx-xxxx-xxxx-xxxxxxxxxxxx 
    -OIDCToken $OIDCToken
    
    
    
RELATED LINKS

## Remove-CloudProjectAssociation


NAME
    Remove-CloudProjectAssociation
    
SYNOPSIS
    Removes the Association between a work area and a Bentley Infrastructure 
     Cloud project.
    
    
SYNTAX
    Remove-CloudProjectAssociation [-InputFolder] <ProjectWiseFolder> 
    [<CommonParameters>]
    
    Remove-CloudProjectAssociation [-FolderGUID] <string> 
    [<CommonParameters>]
    
    
DESCRIPTION
    Removes the Bentley Infrastructure Cloud Project Association for the 
    provided Work Area's InputFolder object or GUID.
    

PARAMETERS
    -InputFolder <ProjectWiseFolder>
        The folder object to remove the Bentley Infrastructure Cloud Project 
        Association from.
        
        Required?                    true
        Position?                    0
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       True (ByValue)
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -FolderGUID <string>
        The Folder Guid for the Work Area to remove the Bentley 
        Infrastructure Cloud Project Association from.
        
        Required?                    true
        Position?                    0
        Default value                None

## Remove-PWFolder


NAME
    Remove-PWFolder
    
SYNOPSIS
    Remove folders in a tree given folder path, folder id or array from 
    pipeline. -RemoveFolders switch will just delete the empty leaf folders. 
    -RemoveDocuments will just remove the documents in the given tree. 
    Combine -RemoveFolders -RemoveDocuments to completely remove the tree.
    
    -ProceedWithDelete is a required parameter.
    
    
SYNTAX
    Remove-PWFolder [-InputFolder] <ProjectWiseFolder[]> 
    [[-RemoveDocuments]] [[-RemoveFolders]] [-ProceedWithDelete] 
    [[-DeletePasses] <int>] [<CommonParameters>]
    
    Remove-PWFolder [-FolderID] <int> [[-RemoveDocuments]] 
    [[-RemoveFolders]] [-ProceedWithDelete] [[-DeletePasses] <int>] 
    [<CommonParameters>]
    
    Remove-PWFolder [[-FolderPath] <string>] [[-RemoveDocuments]] 
    [[-RemoveFolders]] [-ProceedWithDelete] [[-DeletePasses] <int>] 
    [<CommonParameters>]
    
    
DESCRIPTION
    Remove folders in a tree given a folder path, folder id or an array of 
    folders from the pipeline. The -RemoveFolders switch will just delete 
    the empty leaf folders. The -RemoveDocuments switch will just remove the 
    documents in the given tree. Combine -RemoveFolders and -RemoveDocuments 
    to completely remove the tree.
    
    -ProceedWithDelete is a required parameter.
    

PARAMETERS
    -InputFolder <ProjectWiseFolder[]>
        
        
        Required?                    true
        Position?                    0
        Default value                PWPS_DAB.CommonTypes+ProjectWiseFolder[]
        Accept pipeline input?       True (ByValue)
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -FolderID <int>
        Folder ID for folder to be removed.
        
        Required?                    true
        Position?                    0
        Default value                0
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -FolderPath <string>
        Folder Path.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    0
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -RemoveDocuments [<SwitchParameter>]
        Set to remove documents.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    1
        Default value                False
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -RemoveFolders [<SwitchParameter>]
        Set to remove folders.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    2
        Default value                False
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -ProceedWithDelete [<SwitchParameter>]
        Required to proceed.
        
        Required?                    true
        Position?                    3
        Default value                False
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -DeletePasses <int>
        Defaults to 10. Less is probably fine. More probably not required.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    4
        Default value                10
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    <CommonParameters>
        This cmdlet supports the common parameters: Verbose, Debug,
        ErrorAction, ErrorVariable, WarningAction, WarningVariable,
        OutBuffer, PipelineVariable, and OutVariable. For more information, 
    see 
        about_CommonParameters 
    (https:/go.microsoft.com/fwlink/?LinkID=113216). 
    
INPUTS
    PWPS_DAB.CommonTypes+ProjectWiseFolder[]
        
    
    
OUTPUTS
    System.Boolean
        
    
    
NOTES
    
    
        
    
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 1 --------------------------
    
    Get-PWFolders -FolderPath 'Top Level\Sub-Folder' | Remove-PWFolder 
    -RemoveDocuments -RemoveFolders -ProceedWithDelete
    
    
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 2 --------------------------
    
    Remove-PWFolder -FolderPath 'Top Level\Sub-Folder' -RemoveDocuments 
    -RemoveFolders -ProceedWithDelete
    
    
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 3 --------------------------
    
    Remove-PWFolder -FolderPath 'Top Level\Sub-Folder' -RemoveDocuments 
    -RemoveFolders -ProceedWithDelete -DeletePasses 15
    
    
    
RELATED LINKS

## Remove-PWFolderSecurity


NAME
    Remove-PWFolderSecurity
    
SYNOPSIS
    Remove-PWFolderSecurity removes document or folder/project access 
    permissions set on a folder/project for a certain member.
    
    
SYNTAX
    Remove-PWFolderSecurity [-InputFolder] <ProjectWiseFolder[]> 
    [-MemberType] <string> [[-MemberName] <string>] [-FolderSecurity] 
    [-DocumentSecurity] [-WorkflowName <string>] [-StateName <string>] 
    [<CommonParameters>]
    
    
DESCRIPTION
    Remove-PWFolderSecurity removes document or folder/project access 
    permissions set on a folder/project for a certain member. The possible 
    members that can have document or folder/project access permissions 
    assigned are user, group, userlist, everyone.  This command can also 
    remove access permissions assigned to the folder/project workflow states 
    for that particular folder/project.
    

PARAMETERS
    -InputFolder <ProjectWiseFolder[]>
        
        
        Required?                    true
        Position?                    0
        Default value                PWPS_DAB.CommonTypes+ProjectWiseFolder[]
        Accept pipeline input?       True (ByValue)
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -MemberType <string>
        
        
        Required?                    true
        Position?                    1
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -MemberName <string>
        
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    2
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -FolderSecurity [<SwitchParameter>]
        
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    named
        Default value                False
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -DocumentSecurity [<SwitchParameter>]
        
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    named
        Default value                False
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -WorkflowName <string>
        
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    named
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -StateName <string>
        
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    named
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    <CommonParameters>
        This cmdlet supports the common parameters: Verbose, Debug,
        ErrorAction, ErrorVariable, WarningAction, WarningVariable,
        OutBuffer, PipelineVariable, and OutVariable. For more information, 
    see 
        about_CommonParameters 
    (https:/go.microsoft.com/fwlink/?LinkID=113216). 
    
INPUTS
    PWPS_DAB.CommonTypes+ProjectWiseFolder[]
        
    
    
OUTPUTS
    
NOTES
    
    
        
    
    -------------------------- Example 1 --------------------------
    
    PS C:\> Remove-PWFolderSecurity 
    	-InputFolder $folders 
    	-MemberType "user" 
    	-MemberName "admin" 
    	-DocumentSecurity 
    	-WorkflowName "ProjectWorkflow" 
    	-StateName "In progress"
    
    
    -------------------------- Example 2 --------------------------
    
    Remove-PWFolderSecurity -InputFolder (Show-PWFolderBrowserDialog) 
    -MemberType 'u' -MemberName 'username' -FolderSecurity -DocumentSecurity 
    -Verbose
    
    
    -------------------------- Example 3 --------------------------
    
    Remove-PWFolderSecurity -InputFolder (Show-PWFolderBrowserDialog) 
    -MemberType Everyone -FolderSecurity -Verbose
    
    
    -------------------------- Example 4 --------------------------
    
    Remove-PWFolderSecurity -InputFolder (Show-PWFolderBrowserDialog) 
    -MemberType Everyone -FolderSecurity -WorkflowName "Default Workflow" 
    -StateName "Draft" -Verbose

## Remove-PWGroup


NAME
    Remove-PWGroup
    
SYNOPSIS
    Deletes a Group from the current ProjectWise Datasource.
    
    
SYNTAX
    Remove-PWGroup [-GroupID] <Int32> [-Confirm] [-Force] [-WhatIf] 
    [<CommonParameters>]
    
    Remove-PWGroup [-Confirm] [-Force] -GroupName <String> [-WhatIf] 
    [<CommonParameters>]
    
    
DESCRIPTION
    The Remove-PWGroup cmdlet deletes a given Group in the current 
    ProjectWise Datasource.
    
    The Group to delete can be selected either by ID or by name.
    

PARAMETERS
    -Confirm [<SwitchParameter>]
        Prompts you for confirmation before executing the command.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    named
        Default value                False
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -Force [<SwitchParameter>]
        If set, Remove-PWGroup will suppress confirmation prompts.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    named
        Default value                False
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -GroupID <Int32>
        The GroupID of the Group to delete.
        
        Required?                    true
        Position?                    0
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       True (ByPropertyName)
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -GroupName <String>
        The GroupName of the Group to delete. Wildcards (*) are technically 
        allowed, but Groups can only be deleted one at a time, so this name 
        must only match one Group.
        
        Required?                    true
        Position?                    named
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -WhatIf [<SwitchParameter>]
        Describes what would happen if you executed the command without 
        actually executing the command.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    named
        Default value                False
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    <CommonParameters>
        This cmdlet supports the common parameters: Verbose, Debug,
        ErrorAction, ErrorVariable, WarningAction, WarningVariable,
        OutBuffer, PipelineVariable, and OutVariable. For more information, 
    see 
        about_CommonParameters 
    (https:/go.microsoft.com/fwlink/?LinkID=113216). 
    
INPUTS
    Bentley.ProjectWise.PowerShell.Common.Group
        You can optionally pipe a Group object to Remove-PWGroup to delete 
        that Group.
    
    
OUTPUTS
    None
        This cmdlet does not return any output.
    
    
NOTES
    
    
        Remove-PWGroup requires an open ProjectWise Connection in the 
        current thread.  You can open a Connection with Open-PWConnection 
        before calling Remove-PWGroup.
        
        Remove-PWGroup requires an Admin session.  To open an admin session, 
        use the -Admin flag when opening the ProjectWise Connection with 
        Open-PWConnection.
    
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 1 --------------------------
    
    Remove-PWGroup -GroupID 3
    
    Delete a Group by ID
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 2 --------------------------
    
    Remove-PWGroup -GroupID 3 -Force
    
    Delete a Group by ID (suppress confimation prompt)
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 3 --------------------------
    
    Remove-PWGroup -GroupName DomainGroup
    
    Delete a Group by Name
    
RELATED LINKS
    Get-PWGroup 
    New-PWGroup 
    Update-PWGroup

## Remove-PWGroupMember


NAME
    Remove-PWGroupMember
    
SYNOPSIS
    Removes a User from a Group in the current ProjectWise Datasource.
    
    
SYNTAX
    Remove-PWGroupMember [-GroupID] <Int32> [-UserID] <Int32> 
    [<CommonParameters>]
    
    Remove-PWGroupMember [-GroupID] <Int32> [-UserName] <String> 
    [<CommonParameters>]
    
    Remove-PWGroupMember [-GroupName] <String> [-UserID] <Int32> 
    [<CommonParameters>]
    
    Remove-PWGroupMember [-GroupName] <String> [-UserName] <String> 
    [<CommonParameters>]
    
    
DESCRIPTION
    The Remove-PWGroupMember cmdlet removes a User from a User Group in the 
    current ProjectWise Datasource.
    
    Users and Groups can be selected either by ID or by name.
    

PARAMETERS
    -GroupID <Int32>
        The ID of the User Group to remove from.
        
        Required?                    true
        Position?                    0
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       True (ByPropertyName)
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -GroupName <String>
        The name of the User Group to remove from. Wildcards (*, ?) are 
        technically allowed, but Group Members can only be removed one at a 
        time, so this name must only match one Group.
        
        Required?                    true
        Position?                    0
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -UserID <Int32>
        The UserID of the User to remove.
        
        Required?                    true
        Position?                    1
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       True (ByPropertyName)
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -UserName <String>
        The UserName of the User to remove. Wildcards (*) are technically 
        allowed, but Group Members can only be removed one at a time, so 
        this name must only match one User.
        
        Required?                    true
        Position?                    1
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    <CommonParameters>
        This cmdlet supports the common parameters: Verbose, Debug,
        ErrorAction, ErrorVariable, WarningAction, WarningVariable,
        OutBuffer, PipelineVariable, and OutVariable. For more information, 
    see 
        about_CommonParameters 
    (https:/go.microsoft.com/fwlink/?LinkID=113216). 
    
INPUTS
    None
        You can optionally pipe either User or Group objects to this cmdlet.
    
    
OUTPUTS
    Bentley.ProjectWise.PowerShell.Common.User
        Remove-PWGroupMember returns an object that represents the User that 
        was removed from the User Group.
    
    
NOTES
    
    
        Remove-PWGroupMember requires an open ProjectWise Connection in the 
        current thread.  You can open a Connection with Open-PWConnection 
        before calling Remove-PWGroupMember.
        
        Remove-PWGroupMember requires an Admin session.  To open an admin 
        session, use the -Admin flag when opening the ProjectWise Connection 
        with Open-PWConnection.
    
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 1 --------------------------
    
    Remove-PWGroupMember -GroupID 3 -UserName admin
    
    Remove a User with UserName = admin from the Group with GroupID = 3
    
    UserID UserName      Description   Type    SecProvider  IsConnected 
    IsDisabled ------ --------      -----------   ----    -----------  
    ----------- ----------   1    admin                       Logical        
               Y           N
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 2 --------------------------
    
    Remove-PWGroupMember -GroupID 3 -UserID 2
    
    Remove a User with UserID = 2 from the Group with GroupID = 3
    
    UserID UserName      Description   Type    SecProvider  IsConnected 
    IsDisabled ------ --------      -----------   ----    -----------  
    ----------- ----------   2    user                        Logical        
               N           N
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 3 --------------------------
    
    Remove-PWGroupMember -GroupName DomainGroup -UserName admin
    
    Remove a User with UserName = admin from the Group with GroupName = 
    DomainGroup
    
    UserID UserName      Description   Type    SecProvider  IsConnected 
    IsDisabled ------ --------      -----------   ----    -----------  
    ----------- ----------   1    admin                       Logical        
               Y           N
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 4 --------------------------
    
    Remove-PWGroupMember -GroupName DomainGroup -UserID 2
    
    Remove a User with UserID = 2 from the Group with GroupName = DomainGroup
    
    UserID UserName      Description   Type    SecProvider  IsConnected 
    IsDisabled ------ --------      -----------   ----    -----------  
    ----------- ----------   2    user                        Logical        
               N           N
    
RELATED LINKS
    Add-PWGroupMember 
    Get-PWGroupMember
