NAME
    Remove-PWLookup
    
SYNOPSIS
    Removes (unregisters) a registered lookup, and cleans lookup data.
    
    
SYNTAX
    Remove-PWLookup [-LookupName] <String> [-WhatIf] [-Confirm] 
    [<CommonParameters>]
    
    
DESCRIPTION
    The Remove-PWLookup cmdlet removes (unregisters) a registered lookup, 
    and (if lookup is not pointing to environment table) deletes lookup data.
    

PARAMETERS
    -LookupName <String>
        The name of lookup to be removed.
        
        Required?                    true
        Position?                    0
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       True (ByPropertyName)
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -WhatIf [<SwitchParameter>]
        @{Text=}
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    named
        Default value                False
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -Confirm [<SwitchParameter>]
        @{Text=}
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    named
        Default value                False
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    <CommonParameters>
        This cmdlet supports the common parameters: Verbose, Debug,
        ErrorAction, ErrorVariable, WarningAction, WarningVariable,
        OutBuffer, PipelineVariable, and OutVariable. For more information, 
    see 
        about_CommonParameters 
    (https:/go.microsoft.com/fwlink/?LinkID=113216). 
    
INPUTS
    None
        You cannot pipe objects to this cmdlet.
    
    
OUTPUTS
    None
        Does not return anything.
    
    
NOTES
    
    
        
    
    ------------------------ Remove lookup ------------------------
    
    Remove-PWLookup -LookupName "MyCfg.DocTypes"
    
    Removes lookup named "MyCfg.DocTypes"
    
RELATED LINKS
    New-PWLookup 
    Set-PWLookupData

## Remove-PWStorage


NAME
    Remove-PWStorage
    
SYNOPSIS
    Removes a Storage Area from the current ProjectWise Datasource.
    
    
SYNTAX
    Remove-PWStorage [-Force] [-StorageID <Int32>] [<CommonParameters>]
    
    
DESCRIPTION
    The Remove-PWStorage cmdlet removes the provided Storage Area from the 
    current ProjectWise Datasource.
    

PARAMETERS
    -Force [<SwitchParameter>]
        If set, Remove-PWUser will suppress confirmation prompts.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    named
        Default value                False
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -StorageID <Int32>
        The StorageID of the Storage to delete.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    named
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       True (ByPropertyName)
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    <CommonParameters>
        This cmdlet supports the common parameters: Verbose, Debug,
        ErrorAction, ErrorVariable, WarningAction, WarningVariable,
        OutBuffer, PipelineVariable, and OutVariable. For more information, 
    see 
        about_CommonParameters 
    (https:/go.microsoft.com/fwlink/?LinkID=113216). 
    
INPUTS
    
OUTPUTS
    
NOTES
    
    
        This cmdlet requires an open administrative ProjectWise Connection 
        in the current thread. You can open an administrative Connection 
        using the Open-PWConnection cmdlet and setting the -Admin flag.
    
    -------------------------- Example 1 --------------------------
    
    Remove-PWStorage -StorageID 11
    
    Remove the Storage Area from the current ProjectWise Datasource.

## Remove-PWStorageLocation


NAME
    Remove-PWStorageLocation
    
SYNOPSIS
    Removes StorageLocation entry from configuration of Caching Server.
    
    
SYNTAX
    Remove-PWStorageLocation [-HostName] <String> -ID <Int32> 
    [<CommonParameters>]
    
    
DESCRIPTION
    The Remove-PWStorageLocation cmdlet removes configuration entry 
    StorageLocation from the specified Caching Server.
    

PARAMETERS
    -HostName <String>
        The hostname of the machine where the StorageLocation entry will be 
        removed.
        
        Required?                    true
        Position?                    1
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       True (ByPropertyName)
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -ID <Int32>
        The numeric identifier of StorageLocation entry.
        
        Required?                    true
        Position?                    named
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       True (ByPropertyName)
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    <CommonParameters>
        This cmdlet supports the common parameters: Verbose, Debug,
        ErrorAction, ErrorVariable, WarningAction, WarningVariable,
        OutBuffer, PipelineVariable, and OutVariable. For more information, 
    see 
        about_CommonParameters 
    (https:/go.microsoft.com/fwlink/?LinkID=113216). 
    
INPUTS
    None
        You cannot pipe objects to this cmdlet.
    
    
OUTPUTS
    Boolean
        Returns true if the entry was removed or did not exist.
    
    
NOTES
    
    
        
    
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 1 --------------------------
    
    Remove-PWStorageLocation -Host "PWHOST" -ID 0
    
    Removes StorageLocation entry with idex 0 in the configuration file of 
    PWHOST caching server
    
    True

## Remove-PWUser


NAME
    Remove-PWUser
    
SYNOPSIS
    Deletes a User from the current ProjectWise Datasource.
    
    
SYNTAX
    Remove-PWUser [-UserID] <Int32> [-Confirm] [-Force] [-WhatIf] 
    [<CommonParameters>]
    
    Remove-PWUser [-Confirm] [-Force] -UserName <String> [-WhatIf] 
    [<CommonParameters>]
    
    
DESCRIPTION
    The Remove-PWUser cmdlet deletes a given User in the current ProjectWise 
    Datasource.
    
    The User to delete can be selected either by ID or by name.
    

PARAMETERS
    -Confirm [<SwitchParameter>]
        Prompts you for confirmation before executing the command.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    named
        Default value                False
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -Force [<SwitchParameter>]
        If set, Remove-PWUser will suppress confirmation prompts.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    named
        Default value                False
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -UserID <Int32>
        The UserID of the User to delete.
        
        Required?                    true
        Position?                    0
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       True (ByPropertyName)
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -UserName <String>
        The UserName of the User to delete. Wildcards (*) are technically 
        allowed, but Users can only be deleted one at a time, so this name 
        must only match one User.
        
        Required?                    true
        Position?                    named
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -WhatIf [<SwitchParameter>]
        Describes what would happen if you executed the command without 
        actually executing the command.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    named
        Default value                False
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    <CommonParameters>
        This cmdlet supports the common parameters: Verbose, Debug,
        ErrorAction, ErrorVariable, WarningAction, WarningVariable,
        OutBuffer, PipelineVariable, and OutVariable. For more information, 
    see 
        about_CommonParameters 
    (https:/go.microsoft.com/fwlink/?LinkID=113216). 
    
INPUTS
    Bentley.ProjectWise.PowerShell.Common.User
        You can optionally pipe a User object to Remove-PWUser to delete 
        that User.
    
    
OUTPUTS
    None
        This cmdlet does not return any output.
    
    
NOTES
    
    
        Remove-PWUser requires an open ProjectWise Connection in the current 
        thread.  You can open a Connection with Open-PWConnection before 
        calling Remove-PWUser.
        
        Remove-PWUser requires an Admin session.  To open an admin session, 
        use the -Admin flag when opening the ProjectWise Connection with 
        Open-PWConnection.
    
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 1 --------------------------
    
    Remove-PWUser -UserID 6
    
    Description
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 2 --------------------------
    
    Remove-PWUser -UserID 6 -Force
    
    Description
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 3 --------------------------
    
    Remove-PWUser -UserName Domain.User
    
    Description
    
RELATED LINKS
    Get-PWUser 
    New-PWUser 
    Update-PWUser

## Remove-PWUserIdentity


NAME
    Remove-PWUserIdentity
    
SYNOPSIS
    Disassociates the Federated User Identity from the User account.
    
    
SYNTAX
    Remove-PWUserIdentity [[-UserID] <Int32>] [[-UserName] <String>] 
    [[-ProviderID] <Int32>] [[-ProviderName] <String>] [[-Name] <String>] 
    [[-ProviderProtocol] <String>] [<CommonParameters>]
    
    
DESCRIPTION
    The Remove-PWUserIdentity cmdlet disassociates the Federated User 
    Identity from the User account in the current ProjectWise Datasource.
    

PARAMETERS
    -Name <String>
        The Name aka Identity of the Federated Identity.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    2
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       True (ByPropertyName)
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -ProviderID <Int32>
        The numerical identifier of the Identity Provider the Federated 
        Identity belongs to.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    1
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       True (ByPropertyName)
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -ProviderName <String>
        The name/identity/URL of the Identity Provider the Federated 
        Identity belongs to. It can be accompanied by ProviderProtocol 
        parameter to distinguish between multiple providers served by same 
        service.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    1
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -ProviderProtocol <String>
        The protocol/type of the Identity Provider as string. Currently 
        supported options are "STS" and "OIDC".
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    3
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       True (ByPropertyName)
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -UserID <Int32>
        The numerical identifier of the ProjectWise user the Federated 
        Identity is assigned to.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    0
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       True (ByPropertyName)
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -UserName <String>
        The User Name of the ProjectWise user the Federated Identity is 
        assigned to.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    0
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    <CommonParameters>
        This cmdlet supports the common parameters: Verbose, Debug,
        ErrorAction, ErrorVariable, WarningAction, WarningVariable,
        OutBuffer, PipelineVariable, and OutVariable. For more information, 
    see 
        about_CommonParameters 
    (https:/go.microsoft.com/fwlink/?LinkID=113216). 
    
INPUTS
    Bentley.ProjectWise.PowerShell.Common.UserIdentity
        You can pipe UserIdentity objects to Remove-PWUserIdentity.
    
    
OUTPUTS
    bool value True
        Remove-PWUserIdentity returns true if removal was successful.
    
    
NOTES
    
    
        Remove-PWUserIdentity requires an open ProjectWise Connection in the 
        current thread.  You can open a Connection with New-PWLogin before 
        calling Remove-PWUserIdentity.
        
        Remove-PWUserIdentity requires an Admin session.
    
    --------------------------- EXAMPLE1 ---------------------------
    
    Remove-PWUserIdentity -UserName "user"
    
    Deletes all Federated Identities associated with ProjectWise user with 
    specified User Name.
    
    True
    --------------------------- EXAMPLE2 ---------------------------
    
    Remove-PWUserIdentity -ProviderProtocol OIDC
    
    Deletes all Federated Identities which belong to Identity Providers of 
    specified protocol.
    
RELATED LINKS
    Add-PWUserIdentity 
    Add-PWUserIdentityProvider 
    Get-PWUserIdentity 
    Get-PWUserIdentityProvider 
    Remove-PWUserIdentityProvider 
    Update-PWUserIdentityProvider

        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       True (ByPropertyName)
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -Name <String>
        The name/identity/URL of the Identity Provider. It can be 
        accompanied by Protocol parameter to distinguish between multiple 
        providers served by same service.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    0
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       True (ByPropertyName)
        Accept wildcard characters?  false

        supported options are "STS" and "OIDC".
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    1
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       True (ByPropertyName)
        Accept wildcard characters?  false

## Remove-PWUserListMember


NAME
    Remove-PWUserListMember
    
SYNOPSIS
    Removes a User, Group, or UserList from a UserList in the current 
    ProjectWise Datasource.
    
    
SYNTAX
    Remove-PWUserListMember [-ID] <Int32> -GroupID <Int32> 
    [<CommonParameters>]
    
    Remove-PWUserListMember [-ID] <Int32> -UserID <Int32> 
    [<CommonParameters>]
    
    Remove-PWUserListMember [-ID] <Int32> -UserListID <Int32> 
    [<CommonParameters>]
    
    
DESCRIPTION
    The Remove-PWUserListMember cmdlet removes a User, Group, or UserList 
    from a User List in the current ProjectWise Datasource.
    
    Users, Groups, and UserLists can be selected by ID.
    

PARAMETERS
    -GroupID <Int32>
        The GroupID of the Group to remove.
        
        Required?                    true
        Position?                    named
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       True (ByPropertyName)
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -ID <Int32>
        The ID of the User List to remove from.
        
        Required?                    true
        Position?                    0
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -UserID <Int32>
        The UserID of the User to remove.
        
        Required?                    true
        Position?                    named
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       True (ByPropertyName)
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -UserListID <Int32>
        The UserListID of the UserList to remove.
        
        Required?                    true
        Position?                    named
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       True (ByPropertyName)
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    <CommonParameters>
        This cmdlet supports the common parameters: Verbose, Debug,
        ErrorAction, ErrorVariable, WarningAction, WarningVariable,
        OutBuffer, PipelineVariable, and OutVariable. For more information, 
    see 
        about_CommonParameters 
    (https:/go.microsoft.com/fwlink/?LinkID=113216). 
    
INPUTS
    None
        You can optionally pipe User, Group, or UserList objects to this 
        cmdlet.
    
    
OUTPUTS
    None
        This cmdlet does not return any output.
    
    
NOTES
    
    
        Remove-PWUserListMember requires an open ProjectWise Connection in 
        the current thread.  You can open a Connection with 
        Open-PWConnection before calling Remove-PWUserListMember.
        
        Remove-PWUserListMember requires an Admin session.  To open an admin 
        session, use the -Admin flag when opening the ProjectWise Connection 
        with Open-PWConnection.
    
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 1 --------------------------
    
    Remove-PWUserListMember 1 -UserID 1
    
    Remove a User with UserID = 1 from the UserList with UserListID = 1
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 2 --------------------------
    
    Remove-PWUserListMember 1 -GroupID 1
    
    Remove a Group with GroupID = 1 from the UserList with UserListID = 1
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 3 --------------------------
    
    Remove-PWUserListMember 1 -UserListID 4
    
    Remove a UserList with UserListID = 4 from the UserList with UserListID 
    = 1

## Remove-PWUserNativeIdentity


NAME
    Remove-PWUserNativeIdentity
    
SYNOPSIS
    Removes current password assigned to a User in the current ProjectWise 
    Datasource.
    
    
SYNTAX
    Remove-PWUserNativeIdentity [-UserID] <Int32> [<CommonParameters>]
    
    Remove-PWUserNativeIdentity [-UserName] <String> [<CommonParameters>]
    
    
DESCRIPTION
    The Remove-PWUserNativeIdentity cmdlet removes password currently 
    assigned to a User in the current ProjectWise Datasource.
    
    User can be selected either by ID or by Name.
    

PARAMETERS
    -UserID <Int32>
        The numerical identifier of the User to remove password for.
        
        Required?                    true
        Position?                    0
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       True (ByPropertyName)
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -UserName <String>
        The textual identifier of the User to remove password for.
        
        Required?                    true
        Position?                    0
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       True (ByPropertyName)
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    <CommonParameters>
        This cmdlet supports the common parameters: Verbose, Debug,
        ErrorAction, ErrorVariable, WarningAction, WarningVariable,
        OutBuffer, PipelineVariable, and OutVariable. For more information, 
    see 
        about_CommonParameters 
    (https:/go.microsoft.com/fwlink/?LinkID=113216). 
    
INPUTS
    
OUTPUTS
    
        Remove-PWUserIdentity returns an object that represents the User for 
        which password was removed.
    
    
NOTES
    
    
        Remove-PWUserNativeIdentity requires an open ProjectWise Connection 
        in the current thread.  You can open a Connection with 
        Open-PWConnection before calling Remove-PWUserNativeIdentity.
        
        Remove-PWUserNativeIdentity requires an Admin session.  To open an 
        admin session, use the -Admin flag when opening the ProjectWise 
        Connection with Open-PWConnection.
    
    --------------------------- EXAMPLE1 ---------------------------
    
    Remove-PWUserNativeIdentity -UserName "user"
    
    Removes password of ProjectWise user named "user". It now becomes 
    impossible to perform Native login to PWDI server for this user.
    
    UserID UserName                                   Description            
                        Type                                      
    SecProvider                               IsConnected IsDisabled ------ 
    --------                                   -----------                   
                 ----                                      -----------       
                            ----------- ----------   3    user               
                                                                       
    Windows                                   bentley                        
                    N           N

    Update-PWUserNativeIdentity

## Remove-PWUserWindowsIdentity


NAME
    Remove-PWUserWindowsIdentity
    
SYNOPSIS
    Disassociates Windows Domain Identity from an User account in the 
    current ProjectWise Datasource.
    
    
SYNTAX
    Remove-PWUserWindowsIdentity [[-UserID] <Int32>] [[-UserName] <String>] 
    [[-DomainID] <Int32>] [[-DomainName] <String>] [[-Name] <String>] 
    [<CommonParameters>]
    
    
DESCRIPTION
    The Remove-PWUserWindowsIdentity cmdlet disassociates Windows Domain 
    Identity from an User account in the current ProjectWise Datasource.
    
    User can be selected either by ID or by Name.
    

PARAMETERS
    -DomainID <Int32>
        The numerical identifier of the Windows Domain to remove Windows 
        Identities from.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    1
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       True (ByPropertyName)
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -DomainName <String>
        The name of the Windows Domain to remove Windows Identities from.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    1
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -Name <String>
        The Windows Name of the Windows Domain Identity to to remove.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    2
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       True (ByPropertyName)
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -UserID <Int32>
        The numerical identifier of ProjectWise user to remove identities 
        from.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    0
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       True (ByPropertyName)
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -UserName <String>
        The textual identifier of ProjectWise user to remove identities from.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    0
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    <CommonParameters>
        This cmdlet supports the common parameters: Verbose, Debug,
        ErrorAction, ErrorVariable, WarningAction, WarningVariable,
        OutBuffer, PipelineVariable, and OutVariable. For more information, 
    see 
        about_CommonParameters 
    (https:/go.microsoft.com/fwlink/?LinkID=113216). 
    
INPUTS
    Bentley.ProjectWise.PowerShell.Common.WindowsIdentity
        You can pipe WindowsIdentity objects to Remove-PWUserWindowsIdentity.
    
    
OUTPUTS
    bool value True
        Remove-PWUserWindowsIdentity returns true if removal succeeded.
    
    
NOTES
    
    
        Remove-PWUserWindowsIdentity requires an open ProjectWise Connection 
        in the current thread.  You can open a Connection with New-PWLogin 
        before calling Remove-PWUserWindowsIdentity.
        
        Remove-PWUserWindowsIdentity requires an Admin session.
    
    --------------------------- EXAMPLE1 ---------------------------
    
    Remove-PWUserWindowsIdentity -Name john.doe
    
    Removes all Windows Identities with Windows Name "john.doe"

## Remove-PWWorkAreaResource


NAME
    Remove-PWWorkAreaResource
    
SYNOPSIS
    Removes a specific resource member from a resource type associated with 
    a Work Area.
    
    
SYNTAX
    Remove-PWWorkAreaResource [-InputFolder] <ProjectWiseFolder> 
    [-ResourceType] {application | department | environment | storagearea | 
    view | workflow | workspaceprofile | renditionprofile} 
    [-ResourceMemberName] <String> [<CommonParameters>]
    
    
DESCRIPTION
    The Remove-PWWorkAreaResource cmdlet removes a specific resource member 
    from a resource type associated with a Work Area. This cmdlet allows 
    administrators to manage resource assignments by removing individual 
    resource members such as applications, departments, environments, 
    storage areas, views, workflows, or rendition profiles from a Work Area.
    
    The cmdlet validates that the specified folder is a Work Area before 
    attempting removal. It also verifies that the specified resource member 
    exists and is currently assigned to the Work Area. If the resource type 
    is set to "None" for the Work Area, no removal is performed.
    
    Note: The 'workspaceprofile' resource type is currently not implemented 
    and will not function.
    

PARAMETERS
    -InputFolder <ProjectWiseFolder>
        Specifies the Work Area folder from which to remove the resource 
        member. The folder must be a valid Work Area; otherwise, a warning 
        is displayed and the cmdlet returns false.
        
        Required?                    true
        Position?                    0
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       True (ByValue)
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -ResourceType <String>
        Specifies the resource type containing the resource member to be 
        removed.
        
        Valid values are: - application
        
        - department
        
        - environment
        
        - storagearea
        
        - view
        
        - workflow
        
        - workspaceprofile (not implemented)
        
        - renditionprofile
        
        Required?                    true
        Position?                    1
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -ResourceMemberName <String>
        Specifies the name of the resource member to be removed from the 
        Work Area. The name must match an existing member of the specified 
        resource type. The matching is case-insensitive.
        
        Required?                    true
        Position?                    2
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    <CommonParameters>
        This cmdlet supports the common parameters: Verbose, Debug,
        ErrorAction, ErrorVariable, WarningAction, WarningVariable,
        OutBuffer, PipelineVariable, and OutVariable. For more information, 
    see 
        about_CommonParameters 
    (https:/go.microsoft.com/fwlink/?LinkID=113216). 
    
INPUTS
    PWPS_DAB.CommonTypes+ProjectWiseFolder
        A ProjectWiseFolder object representing the Work Area from which to 
        remove the resource member. This parameter accepts pipeline input.
    
    
OUTPUTS
    System.Boolean
        Returns $true if the resource member was successfully removed from 
        the Work Area. Returns $false if the operation failed due to any of 
        the following reasons: - The specified folder is not a Work Area
        
        - The specified resource member was not found
        
        - The resource type is set to "None" for the Work Area
        
        - The removal operation failed
    
    
NOTES
    
    
        - The specified folder must be a Work Area. If it is not, the cmdlet 
        displays a warning and returns false.
        
        - If the specified resource member does not exist or is not assigned 
        to the Work Area, the cmdlet displays a warning and returns false.
        
        - If the resource type is set to "None" for the Work Area, 
        indicating no resources are assigned, the cmdlet displays a warning 
        and returns false.
        
        - The ResourceMemberName parameter matching is case-insensitive.
        
        - The 'workspaceprofile' resource type is listed as a valid option 
        but is currently not implemented.
    
    EXAMPLE 1: Remove an application resource member from a Work Area
    
    $folder = Get-PWFolders -FolderPath 'Projects\Building Design'
    Remove-PWWorkAreaResource -InputFolder $folder -ResourceType 
    'application' -ResourceMemberName 'MicroStation'
    
    Removes the 'MicroStation' application resource member from the 
    specified Work Area.
    EXAMPLE 2: Remove a department resource member with verbose output
    
    $folder = Get-PWFolders -FolderPath 'Projects\Civil Engineering'
    Remove-PWWorkAreaResource -InputFolder $folder -ResourceType 
    'department' -ResourceMemberName 'Engineering' -Verbose
    
    Removes the 'Engineering' department resource member from the Work Area 
    and displays verbose messages during execution.
    ------- EXAMPLE 3: Remove an environment resource member -------
    
    $folder = Get-PWFolders -FolderPath 'Projects\Infrastructure'
    Remove-PWWorkAreaResource -InputFolder $folder -ResourceType 
    'environment' -ResourceMemberName 'Production'
    
    Removes the 'Production' environment resource member from the specified 
    Work Area.
    ------- EXAMPLE 4: Remove a storage area resource member -------
    
    $folder = Get-PWFolders -FolderPath 'Projects\Architectural'
    Remove-PWWorkAreaResource -InputFolder $folder -ResourceType 
    'storagearea' -ResourceMemberName 'ArchiveStorage'
    
    Removes the 'ArchiveStorage' storage area resource member from the Work 
    Area.
    ----------- EXAMPLE 5: Remove a view resource member -----------
    
    $folder = Get-PWFolders -FolderPath 'Projects\MEP'
    Remove-PWWorkAreaResource -InputFolder $folder -ResourceType 'view' 
    -ResourceMemberName 'StandardView'
    
    Removes the 'StandardView' view resource member from the specified Work 
    Area.
    --------- EXAMPLE 6: Remove a workflow resource member ---------
    
    $folder = Get-PWFolders -FolderPath 'Projects\Structural'
    Remove-PWWorkAreaResource -InputFolder $folder -ResourceType 'workflow' 
    -ResourceMemberName 'ApprovalWorkflow'
    
    Removes the 'ApprovalWorkflow' workflow resource member from the Work 
    Area.
    ---- EXAMPLE 7: Remove a rendition profile resource member ----
    
    $folder = Get-PWFolders -FolderPath 'Projects\Documentation'
    Remove-PWWorkAreaResource -InputFolder $folder -ResourceType 
    'renditionprofile' -ResourceMemberName 'PDFProfile'
    
    Removes the 'PDFProfile' rendition profile resource member from the 
    specified Work Area.
    --- EXAMPLE 8: Remove a resource member using pipeline input ---
    
    Get-PWFolders -FolderPath 'Projects\Highway Design' | 
    Remove-PWWorkAreaResource -ResourceType 'application' 
    -ResourceMemberName 'OpenRoads'
    
    Retrieves the Work Area folder using Get-PWFolders and pipes it to 
    Remove-PWWorkAreaResource to remove the 'OpenRoads' application resource 
    member.
    - EXAMPLE 9: Remove resource members from multiple Work Areas -
    
    $folders = Get-PWFolders -FolderPath 'Projects\Civil%'
    foreach ($folder in $folders) {
        Remove-PWWorkAreaResource -InputFolder $folder -ResourceType 
    'department' -ResourceMemberName 'LegacyDept' -Verbose
    }
    
    Iterates through multiple Work Areas matching the path pattern and 
    removes the 'LegacyDept' department resource member from each.
    
RELATED LINKS

## Rename-PWDocument


NAME
    Rename-PWDocument
    
SYNOPSIS
    Rename-PWDocument renames the provided document object with the provided 
    new document name. If the RenameFile switch parameter is included, the 
    file name will be updated as well.
    
    
SYNTAX
    Rename-PWDocument [-InputDocument] <ProjectWiseDocument> 
    [-DocumentNewName] <string> [-RenameFile] [<CommonParameters>]
    
    
DESCRIPTION
    Rename-PWDocument renames the provided document object with the provided 
    new document name. If the RenameFile switch parameter is included, the 
    file name will be updated as well.
    

PARAMETERS
    -InputDocument <ProjectWiseDocument>
        
        
        Required?                    true
        Position?                    0
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       True (ByValue)
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -DocumentNewName <string>
        
        
        Required?                    true
        Position?                    1
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -RenameFile [<SwitchParameter>]
        
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    named
        Default value                False
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    <CommonParameters>
        This cmdlet supports the common parameters: Verbose, Debug,
        ErrorAction, ErrorVariable, WarningAction, WarningVariable,
        OutBuffer, PipelineVariable, and OutVariable. For more information, 
    see 
        about_CommonParameters 
    (https:/go.microsoft.com/fwlink/?LinkID=113216). 
    
INPUTS
    PWPS_DAB.CommonTypes+ProjectWiseDocument
        
    
    
OUTPUTS
    
NOTES
    
    
        
    
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 1 --------------------------
    
    Renames the ProjectWise document passed via the pipeline to 
    'A111-View-4_Renamed.dwg'. Only the document name is changed.
    Get-PWDocumentsBySearch -FolderPath 'TEST\New' -JustThisFolder 
    -DocumentName 'A111-View-4.dwg' -Verbose |
                Rename-PWDocument -DocumentNewName 'A111-View-4_Renamed.dwg' 
    -Verbose
    
    
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 2 --------------------------
    
    Renames the ProjectWise document passed via the pipeline to 
    'A111-View-4_Renamed.dwg'. Both the document and file name are changed.
    Get-PWDocumentsBySearch -FolderPath 'TEST\New' -JustThisFolder 
    -DocumentName 'A111-View-4.dwg' -Verbose |
                Rename-PWDocument -DocumentNewName 'A111-View-4_Renamed.dwg' 
    -RenameFile -Verbose
    
    
    
RELATED LINKS

## Rename-PWDocuments


NAME
    Rename-PWDocuments
    
SYNOPSIS
    Bulk update of document names and/or filenames.
    
    
SYNTAX
    Rename-PWDocuments [-InputDocument] <ProjectWiseDocument[]> 
    [-ReplaceFileNameWithDocumentName <bool>] 
    [-ReplaceDocumentNameWithFileName <bool>] [-DocumentNamePart <string>] 
    [-FileNamePart <string>] [-DocumentNameReplacement <string>] 
    [-FileNameReplacement <string>] [<CommonParameters>]
    
    
DESCRIPTION
    Takes an array of document objects from the pipeline and allows you to 
    replace part or all of the document name, or file name.
    

PARAMETERS
    -InputDocument <ProjectWiseDocument[]>
        Array of documents to operate on.
        
        Required?                    true
        Position?                    0
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       True (ByValue)
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -ReplaceFileNameWithDocumentName <bool>
        Just replace the filename with the document name (preserves 
        extension).
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    named
        Default value                False
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -ReplaceDocumentNameWithFileName <bool>
        Just replace the document name with the filename.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    named
        Default value                False
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -DocumentNamePart <string>
        String to match in the document name. (e.g. "-A01-").
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    named
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -FileNamePart <string>
        String to match in the file name. (e.g. "-A01-").
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    named
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -DocumentNameReplacement <string>
        String to replace in the document name. (e.g. "-B02-").
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    named
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -FileNameReplacement <string>
        String to replace in the file name. (e.g. "-B02-").
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    named
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    <CommonParameters>
        This cmdlet supports the common parameters: Verbose, Debug,
        ErrorAction, ErrorVariable, WarningAction, WarningVariable,
        OutBuffer, PipelineVariable, and OutVariable. For more information, 
    see 
        about_CommonParameters 
    (https:/go.microsoft.com/fwlink/?LinkID=113216). 
    
INPUTS
    PWPS_DAB.CommonTypes+ProjectWiseDocument[]
        Array of documents to operate on.
    
    
OUTPUTS
    
NOTES
    
    
        
    
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 1 --------------------------
    
    #Get the documents in the given path and update them with the listed 
    attributes. Also supports changing name and filename, but, of course, 
    those must be unique in a folder.
    $docs = Get-PWDocumentsBySearch -FolderPath "Folder 01\Sub-Folder 02"
    Rename-PWDocuments $docs -DocumentNamePart "-A01-" 
    -DocumentNameReplacement "-B02-"

## Set-PWAsyncExtractionSchedule


NAME
    Set-PWAsyncExtractionSchedule
    
SYNOPSIS
    Sets the Schedule for an Asynchronous Extraction Component in the 
    current ProjectWise Datasource.
    
    
SYNTAX
    Set-PWAsyncExtractionSchedule [-FilePropertyExtraction] [[-RunTimes] 
    <String[]>] [-Friday <String[]>] [-Monday <String[]>] [-Saturday 
    <String[]>] [-Sleep <Nullable`1[Int32]>] [-Sunday <String[]>] [-Thursday 
    <String[]>] [-Tuesday <String[]>] [-Wednesday <String[]>] 
    [<CommonParameters>]
    
    Set-PWAsyncExtractionSchedule [-FullTextIndexing] [[-RunTimes] 
    <String[]>] [-Friday <String[]>] [-Monday <String[]>] [-Saturday 
    <String[]>] [-Sleep <Nullable`1[Int32]>] [-Sunday <String[]>] [-Thursday 
    <String[]>] [-Tuesday <String[]>] [-Wednesday <String[]>] 
    [<CommonParameters>]
    
    Set-PWAsyncExtractionSchedule [-ThumbnailExtraction] [[-RunTimes] 
    <String[]>] [-Friday <String[]>] [-Monday <String[]>] [-Saturday 
    <String[]>] [-Sleep <Nullable`1[Int32]>] [-Sunday <String[]>] [-Thursday 
    <String[]>] [-Tuesday <String[]>] [-Wednesday <String[]>] 
    [<CommonParameters>]
    
    
DESCRIPTION
    The Set-PWAsyncExtractionSchedule cmdlet sets the Schedule for an 
    Asynchronous Extraction Component in the current ProjectWise Datasource.
    
    The component to modify the Schedule for must be specified by one of the 
    following flags: -FilePropertyExtraction, -FullTextIndexing, or 
    -ThumbnailExtraction.
    
    By default, Set-PWAsyncExtractionSchedule resets the Schedule for the 
    given component (sets it to sleep all day every day). You can use the 
    Runtimes parameter to set time(s) of day for the component to run every 
    day of the week.  In addition, you can use the weekday parameters 
    (Sunday, Monday, Tuesday, etc.) to set time(s) for the component to run 
    on particular days of the week.
    

PARAMETERS
    -FilePropertyExtraction [<SwitchParameter>]
        If set, sets the Schedule for the File Property Extraction Component.
        
        Required?                    true
        Position?                    0
        Default value                False
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -Friday <String[]>
        A comma-separated list of times of day for the component to run on 
        Fridays.  Times can be specified as either individual hour-long 
        blocks (i.e. 8am) or as ranges of time (i.e. 8am-10pm). Times can 
        also be in 12-hour (am/pm) or 24-hour format.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    named
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -FullTextIndexing [<SwitchParameter>]
        If set, sets the Schedule for the Full Text Indexing Component.
        
        Required?                    true
        Position?                    0
        Default value                False
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -Monday <String[]>
        A comma-separated list of times of day for the component to run on 
        Mondays.  Times can be specified as either individual hour-long 
        blocks (i.e. 8am) or as ranges of time (i.e. 8am-10pm). Times can 
        also be in 12-hour (am/pm) or 24-hour format.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    named
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -RunTimes <String[]>
        A comma-separated list of times of day for the component to run 
        every day of the week.  Times can be specified as either individual 
        hour-long blocks (i.e. 8am) or as ranges of time (i.e. 8am-10pm). 
        Times can also be in 12-hour (am/pm) or 24-hour format.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    1
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -Saturday <String[]>
        A comma-separated list of times of day for the component to run on 
        Saturdays.  Times can be specified as either individual hour-long 
        blocks (i.e. 8am) or as ranges of time (i.e. 8am-10pm). Times can 
        also be in 12-hour (am/pm) or 24-hour format.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    named
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -Sleep <Nullable`1[Int32]>
        Sleep (delay) time between updates.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    named
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -Sunday <String[]>
        A comma-separated list of times of day for the component to run on 
        Sundays.  Times can be specified as either individual hour-long 
        blocks (i.e. 8am) or as ranges of time (i.e. 8am-10pm). Times can 
        also be in 12-hour (am/pm) or 24-hour format.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    named
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -ThumbnailExtraction [<SwitchParameter>]
        If set, sets the Schedule for the Thumbnail Extraction Component.
        
        Required?                    true
        Position?                    0
        Default value                False
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -Thursday <String[]>
        A comma-separated list of times of day for the component to run on 
        Thursdays.  Times can be specified as either individual hour-long 
        blocks (i.e. 8am) or as ranges of time (i.e. 8am-10pm). Times can 
        also be in 12-hour (am/pm) or 24-hour format.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    named
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -Tuesday <String[]>
        A comma-separated list of times of day for the component to run on 
        Tuesdays.  Times can be specified as either individual hour-long 
        blocks (i.e. 8am) or as ranges of time (i.e. 8am-10pm). Times can 
        also be in 12-hour (am/pm) or 24-hour format.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    named
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -Wednesday <String[]>
        A comma-separated list of times of day for the component to run on 
        Wednesdays.  Times can be specified as either individual hour-long 
        blocks (i.e. 8am) or as ranges of time (i.e. 8am-10pm). Times can 
        also be in 12-hour (am/pm) or 24-hour format.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    named
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    <CommonParameters>
        This cmdlet supports the common parameters: Verbose, Debug,
        ErrorAction, ErrorVariable, WarningAction, WarningVariable,
        OutBuffer, PipelineVariable, and OutVariable. For more information, 
    see 
        about_CommonParameters 
    (https:/go.microsoft.com/fwlink/?LinkID=113216). 
    
INPUTS
    None
        You cannot pipe objects to this cmdlet.
    
    
OUTPUTS
    Bentley.ProjectWise.PowerShell.Common.ExtractSchedule
        Set-PWAsyncExtractionSchedule returns an object that represents the 
        new Schedule for the given Asynchronous Extraction Component.
    
    
NOTES
    
    
        Set-PWAsyncExtractionSchedule requires an open ProjectWise 
        Connection in the current thread.  You can open a Connection with 
        Open-PWConnection before calling Set-PWAsyncExtractionSchedule.
        
        Set-PWAsyncExtractionSchedule requires an Admin session.  To open an 
        admin session, use the -Admin flag when opening the ProjectWise 
        Connection with Open-PWConnection.
    
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 1 --------------------------
    
    Set-PWAsyncExtractionSchedule -FilePropertyExtraction
    
    Reset (set to never run) the AFP Schedule for the File Property 
    Extraction Component
    
    12AM · 2AM · 4AM · 6AM · 8AM · 10AM· 12PM· 2PM · 4PM · 6PM · 8PM · 10PM· 
    12AM Sun: |  ·  ▌  ·  |  ·  ▌  ·  |  ·  ▌  ·  |  ·  ▌  ·  |  ·  ▌  ·  |  
    ·  ▌  · | Mon: |  ·  ▌  ·  |  ·  ▌  ·  |  ·  ▌  ·  |  ·  ▌  ·  |  ·  ▌  
    ·  |  ·  ▌  · | Tue: |  ·  ▌  ·  |  ·  ▌  ·  |  ·  ▌  ·  |  ·  ▌  ·  |  
    ·  ▌  ·  |  ·  ▌  · | Wed: |  ·  ▌  ·  |  ·  ▌  ·  |  ·  ▌  ·  |  ·  ▌  
    ·  |  ·  ▌  ·  |  ·  ▌  · | Thu: |  ·  ▌  ·  |  ·  ▌  ·  |  ·  ▌  ·  |  
    ·  ▌  ·  |  ·  ▌  ·  |  ·  ▌  · | Fri: |  ·  ▌  ·  |  ·  ▌  ·  |  ·  ▌  
    ·  |  ·  ▌  ·  |  ·  ▌  ·  |  ·  ▌  · | Sat: |  ·  ▌  ·  |  ·  ▌  ·  |  
    ·  ▌  ·  |  ·  ▌  ·  |  ·  ▌  ·  |  ·  ▌  · |
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 2 --------------------------
    
    Set-PWAsyncExtractionSchedule -ThumbnailExtraction 12am-6am -Sat 
    12am-12am -Sun 12am-12am
    
    Set the AFP Schedule for the Thumbnail Extraction Component to run from 
    12am to 6am on weekdays and all day on weekends
    
    12AM · 2AM · 4AM · 6AM · 8AM · 10AM· 12PM· 2PM · 4PM · 6PM · 8PM · 10PM· 
    12AM Sun: |XX·XX▌XX·XX|XX·XX▌XX·XX|XX·XX▌XX·XX|XX·XX▌XX·XX|XX·XX▌XX·XX|XX
    ·XX▌XX·XX| Mon: |XX·XX▌XX·XX|XX·XX▌  ·  |  ·  ▌  ·  |  ·  ▌  ·  |  ·  ▌  
    ·  |  ·  ▌  · | Tue: |XX·XX▌XX·XX|XX·XX▌  ·  |  ·  ▌  ·  |  ·  ▌  ·  |  
    ·  ▌  ·  |  ·  ▌  · | Wed: |XX·XX▌XX·XX|XX·XX▌  ·  |  ·  ▌  ·  |  ·  ▌  
    ·  |  ·  ▌  ·  |  ·  ▌  · | Thu: |XX·XX▌XX·XX|XX·XX▌  ·  |  ·  ▌  ·  |

    |XX·XX▌XX·XX|XX·XX▌XX·XX|XX·XX▌XX·XX|XX·XX▌XX·XX|XX·XX▌XX·XX|XX·XX▌XX·XX|
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 3 --------------------------
    
    Set-PWAsyncExtractionSchedule -FullTextIndexing -Sun 12am-4am,8pm-24 
    -Mon 12am,3,8pm,23 -Tue 12am-4AM,20-24 -Fri 5am-7,5pm-7pm -Sat 7-5pm
    
    Set a detailed AFP Schedule for the Full Text Indexing Component
    
    12AM · 2AM · 4AM · 6AM · 8AM · 10AM· 12PM· 2PM · 4PM · 6PM · 8PM · 10PM· 
    12AM Sun: |XX·XX▌XX·XX|  ·  ▌  ·  |  ·  ▌  ·  |  ·  ▌  ·  |  ·  ▌  ·

## Set-PWAsyncExtractionSetting


NAME
    Set-PWAsyncExtractionSetting
    
SYNOPSIS
    Sets various settings for an Asynchronous Extraction Component in the 
    current ProjectWise Datasource.
    
    
SYNTAX
    Set-PWAsyncExtractionSetting -FilePropertyExtraction -Off 
    [<CommonParameters>]
    
    Set-PWAsyncExtractionSetting -FilePropertyExtraction [-MaxDocuments 
    <Int32>] -On [-Password <Object>] [-RetryTime <Int32>] [-UserName 
    <String>] [<CommonParameters>]
    
    Set-PWAsyncExtractionSetting -FullTextIndexing [-Live] [-MaxDocuments 
    <Int32>] -On [-Password <Object>] [-RetryTime <Int32>] [-SkipSchedule] 
    [-UserName <String>] [<CommonParameters>]
    
    Set-PWAsyncExtractionSetting -FullTextIndexing -Off [<CommonParameters>]
    
    Set-PWAsyncExtractionSetting [-MaxDocuments <Int32>] -On [-Password 
    <Object>] [-RetryTime <Int32>] -ThumbnailExtraction [-UserName <String>] 
    [<CommonParameters>]
    
    Set-PWAsyncExtractionSetting -Off -ThumbnailExtraction 
    [<CommonParameters>]
    
    
DESCRIPTION
    The Set-PWAsyncExtractionSetting cmdlet sets various settings for an 
    Asynchronous Extraction Component in the current ProjectWise Datasource.
    
    The component to modify a setting for must be specified by one of the 
    following flags: -FilePropertyExtraction, -FullTextIndexing, or 
    -ThumbnailExtraction.
    
    The simplest use for Set-PWAsyncExtractionSetting uses either the -On or 
    -Off flag to enable or disable the given Asynchronous Extraction 
    Component.
    
    With the UserName and Password parameters, the user that the component 
    runs as can be set.  Other settings can also be set with the the 
    MaxDocuments and RetryTime parameters.  When the component is specified 
    as FullTextIndexing, the -SkipSchedule flag can also be used to skip the 
    Schedule for Document Property Indexing.
    

PARAMETERS
    -FilePropertyExtraction [<SwitchParameter>]
        If set, sets various settings for the File Property Extraction 
        Component.
        
        Required?                    true
        Position?                    named
        Default value                False
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -FullTextIndexing [<SwitchParameter>]
        If set, sets various settings for the Full Text Indexing Component.
        
        Required?                    true
        Position?                    named
        Default value                False
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -Live [<SwitchParameter>]
        @{Text=}
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    named
        Default value                False
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -MaxDocuments <Int32>
        The maximum number of documents to process in a single pass (must be 
        greater than 0).
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    named
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -Off [<SwitchParameter>]
        If set, disables the given component.
        
        Required?                    true
        Position?                    named
        Default value                False
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -On [<SwitchParameter>]
        If set, enables the given component.
        
        Required?                    true
        Position?                    named
        Default value                False
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -Password <Object>
        The password of the User this component should run as.  If a 
        Password is provided, the UserName parameter must also be provided. 
        Password can be passed as plain text (String) or SecureString 
        object. It is recommended to use SecureString (see examples).
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    named
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -RetryTime <Int32>
        The amount of time (in minutes) to wait before retrying extraction 
        (must be between 10 and 1440 minutes).
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    named
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -SkipSchedule [<SwitchParameter>]
        If set, the component will skip the Schedule for document property 
        indexing.  Only applicable to the Full Text Indexing component.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    named
        Default value                False
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -ThumbnailExtraction [<SwitchParameter>]
        If set, sets various settings for the Thumbnail Extraction Component.
        
        Required?                    true
        Position?                    named
        Default value                False
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -UserName <String>
        The UserName of the User this component should run as.  If a 
        UserName is provided, that User's password must also be provided 
        with the Password parameter.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    named
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    <CommonParameters>
        This cmdlet supports the common parameters: Verbose, Debug,
        ErrorAction, ErrorVariable, WarningAction, WarningVariable,
        OutBuffer, PipelineVariable, and OutVariable. For more information, 
    see 
        about_CommonParameters 
    (https:/go.microsoft.com/fwlink/?LinkID=113216). 
    
INPUTS
    None
        You cannot pipe objects to this cmdlet.
    
    
OUTPUTS
    None
        This cmdlet does not return any output.
    
    
NOTES
    
    
        Set-PWAsyncExtractionSetting requires an open ProjectWise Connection 
        in the current thread.  You can open a Connection with 
        Open-PWConnection before calling Set-PWAsyncExtractionSetting.
        
        Set-PWAsyncExtractionSetting requires an Admin session.  To open an 
        admin session, use the -Admin flag when opening the ProjectWise 
        Connection with Open-PWConnection.
    
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 1 --------------------------
    
    Set-PWAsyncExtractionSetting -FilePropertyExtraction -Off
    
    Turn Off File Property Extraction
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 2 --------------------------
    
    Set-PWAsyncExtractionSetting -FilePropertyExtraction -On
    
    Turn On File Property Extraction
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 3 --------------------------
    
    Set-PWAsyncExtractionSetting -FilePropertyExtraction -On -UserName admin 
    -Password admin
    
    Turn On File Property Extraction and set it to run as a particular User
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 4 --------------------------
    
    Set-PWAsyncExtractionSetting -ThumbnailExtraction -On -MaxDocuments 100 
    -RetryTime 15
    
    Turn On File Thumbnail Extraction and set MaxDocuments and RetryTime
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 5 --------------------------
    
    Set-PWAsyncExtractionSetting -FullTextIndexing -On -Live
    
    Turn On Full Text Indexing and enable Live Extraction (FullTextIndexing 
    only )
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 6 --------------------------
    
    Set-PWAsyncExtractionSetting -FullTextIndexing -On -SkipSchedule
    
    Turn On Full Text Indexing and set it to Skip Schedule for Document 
    Property Indexing (FullTextIndexing only )
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 7 --------------------------
    
    $SecurePassword = Read-Host -Prompt "Enter password" -AsSecureString
    Set-PWAsyncExtractionSetting -FilePropertyExtraction -On -UserName admin 
    -Password $SecurePassword
    
    
    
RELATED LINKS

## Set-PWAuditTrailSetting


NAME
    Set-PWAuditTrailSetting
    
SYNOPSIS
    Set individual datasource audit trail setting on.
    
    
SYNTAX
    Set-PWAuditTrailSetting [-AuditTrailType] {AADMSAT_TYPE_FIRST | 
    AADMSAT_TYPE_VAULT | AADMSAT_TYPE_DOCUMENT | AADMSAT_TYPE_DOCUMENT_SET | 
    AADMSAT_TYPE_WORKFLOW | AADMSAT_TYPE_STATE | AADMSAT_TYPE_USER | 
    AADMSAT_TYPE_GROUP | AADMSAT_TYPE_USER_LIST | AADMSAT_TYPE_LAST | 
    AADMSAT_TYPE_DS_REPORT | AADMSAT_TYPE_USER_LILO} [-AuditTrailAction] 
    {AADMSAT_ACT_DOC_FIRST | AADMSAT_ACT_DOC_UNKNOWN | 
    AADMSAT_ACT_DOC_CREATE | AADMSAT_ACT_DOC_MODIFY | AADMSAT_ACT_DOC_ATTR | 
    AADMSAT_ACT_DOC_FILE_ADD | AADMSAT_ACT_DOC_FILE_REM | 
    AADMSAT_ACT_DOC_FILE_REP | AADMSAT_ACT_DOC_CIN | AADMSAT_ACT_DOC_VIEW | 
    AADMSAT_ACT_DOC_CHOUT | AADMSAT_ACT_DOC_CPOUT | AADMSAT_ACT_DOC_GOUT | 
    AADMSAT_ACT_DOC_STATE | AADMSAT_ACT_DOC_FINAL_S | 
    AADMSAT_ACT_DOC_FINAL_R | AADMSAT_ACT_DOC_VERSION | AADMSAT_ACT_DOC_MOVE 
    | AADMSAT_ACT_DOC_COPY | AADMSAT_ACT_DOC_SECUR | AADMSAT_ACT_DOC_REDLINE 
    | AADMSAT_ACT_DOC_DELETE | AADMSAT_ACT_DOC_EXPORT | AADMSAT_ACT_DOC_FREE 
    | AADMSAT_ACT_DOC_EXTRACT | AADMSAT_ACT_DOC_DISTRIBUTE | 
    AADMSAT_ACT_DOC_SEND_TO | AADMSAT_ACT_DOC_COMMENT | 
    AADMSAT_ACT_DOC_IMPORT | AADMSAT_ACT_DOC_ACL_ASSIGN | 
    AADMSAT_ACT_DOC_ACL_MODIFY | AADMSAT_ACT_DOC_ACL_REMOVE | 
    AADMSAT_ACT_DOC_REVIT | AADMSAT_ACT_DOC_PACK | AADMSAT_ACT_DOC_UNPACK | 
    AADMSAT_ACT_DOC_LAST | AADMSAT_ACT_SET_FIRST | AADMSAT_ACT_SET_CREATE | 
    AADMSAT_ACT_SET_ADD | AADMSAT_ACT_SET_REMOVE | AADMSAT_ACT_SET_LAST | 
    AADMSAT_ACT_NONE | AADMSAT_ACT_DEFAULT | AADMSAT_ACT_VLT_FIRST | 
    AADMSAT_ACT_VLT_CREATE | AADMSAT_ACT_VLT_MODIFY | AADMSAT_ACT_VLT_WFLOW 
    | AADMSAT_ACT_VLT_DELETE | AADMSAT_ACT_VLT_STATE | 
    AADMSAT_ACT_VLT_ACL_ASSIGN | AADMSAT_ACT_VLT_ACL_MODIFY | 
    AADMSAT_ACT_VLT_ACL_REMOVE | AADMSAT_ACT_VLT_LAST | 
    AADMSAT_ACT_USER_FIRST | AADMSAT_ACT_USER_LOGIN | 
    AADMSAT_ACT_USER_LOGOUT | AADMSAT_ACT_USER_CREATE | 
    AADMSAT_ACT_USER_LAST | AADMSAT_ACT_GROUP_FIRST | 
    AADMSAT_ACT_GROUP_CREATE | AADMSAT_ACT_GROUP_MODIFY | 
    AADMSAT_ACT_GROUP_ADD | AADMSAT_ACT_GROUP_REMOVE | 
    AADMSAT_ACT_GROUP_LAST | AADMSAT_ACT_USER_LIST_FIRST | 
    AADMSAT_ACT_USER_LIST_CREATE | AADMSAT_ACT_USER_LIST_MODIFY | 
    AADMSAT_ACT_USER_LIST_ADD | AADMSAT_ACT_USER_LIST_REMOVE | 
    AADMSAT_ACT_USER_LIST_LAST | AADMSAT_ACT_VLT_CUSTOM_FIRST | 
    AADMSAT_ACT_VLT_CUSTOM_LAST | AADMSAT_ACT_DOC_CUSTOM_FIRST | 
    AADMSAT_ACT_DOC_CUSTOM_LAST | AADMSAT_ACT_SET_CUSTOM_FIRST | 
    AADMSAT_ACT_SET_CUSTOM_LAST | AADMSAT_ACT_GEN_CUSTOM_FIRST | 
    AADMSAT_ACT_GEN_CUSTOM_LAST | AADMSAT_ACT_DOC_ATTR_CREATE | 
    AADMSAT_ACT_DOC_ATTR_MODIFY | AADMSAT_ACT_DOC_ATTR_DELETE | 
    AADMSAT_ACT_USER_CONNCOUNT} [<CommonParameters>]
    
    
DESCRIPTION
    Takes an audit trail setting type and action as parameters and turns the 
    corresponding setting on. Intellisense will help you choose the 
    parameter values but you must pair the two parameters by matching 
    subtext.
    

PARAMETERS
    -AuditTrailType <AuditTrailTypes>
        Possible values: AADMSAT_TYPE_FIRST, AADMSAT_TYPE_VAULT, 
        AADMSAT_TYPE_DOCUMENT, AADMSAT_TYPE_DOCUMENT_SET, 
        AADMSAT_TYPE_WORKFLOW, AADMSAT_TYPE_STATE, AADMSAT_TYPE_USER, 
        AADMSAT_TYPE_GROUP, AADMSAT_TYPE_USER_LIST, AADMSAT_TYPE_LAST, 
        AADMSAT_TYPE_DS_REPORT, AADMSAT_TYPE_USER_LILO
        
        Required?                    true
        Position?                    0
        Default value                0
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -AuditTrailAction <AuditTrailActions>
        Possible values: AADMSAT_ACT_DOC_FIRST, AADMSAT_ACT_DOC_UNKNOWN, 
        AADMSAT_ACT_DOC_CREATE, AADMSAT_ACT_DOC_MODIFY, 
        AADMSAT_ACT_DOC_ATTR, AADMSAT_ACT_DOC_FILE_ADD, 
        AADMSAT_ACT_DOC_FILE_REM, AADMSAT_ACT_DOC_FILE_REP, 
        AADMSAT_ACT_DOC_CIN, AADMSAT_ACT_DOC_VIEW, AADMSAT_ACT_DOC_CHOUT, 
        AADMSAT_ACT_DOC_CPOUT, AADMSAT_ACT_DOC_GOUT, AADMSAT_ACT_DOC_STATE, 
        AADMSAT_ACT_DOC_FINAL_S, AADMSAT_ACT_DOC_FINAL_R, 
        AADMSAT_ACT_DOC_VERSION, AADMSAT_ACT_DOC_MOVE, AADMSAT_ACT_DOC_COPY, 
        AADMSAT_ACT_DOC_SECUR, AADMSAT_ACT_DOC_REDLINE, 
        AADMSAT_ACT_DOC_DELETE, AADMSAT_ACT_DOC_EXPORT, 
        AADMSAT_ACT_DOC_FREE, AADMSAT_ACT_DOC_EXTRACT, 
        AADMSAT_ACT_DOC_DISTRIBUTE, AADMSAT_ACT_DOC_SEND_TO, 
        AADMSAT_ACT_DOC_COMMENT, AADMSAT_ACT_DOC_IMPORT, 
        AADMSAT_ACT_DOC_ACL_ASSIGN, AADMSAT_ACT_DOC_ACL_MODIFY, 
        AADMSAT_ACT_DOC_ACL_REMOVE, AADMSAT_ACT_DOC_REVIT, 
        AADMSAT_ACT_DOC_PACK, AADMSAT_ACT_DOC_UNPACK, AADMSAT_ACT_DOC_LAST, 
        AADMSAT_ACT_SET_FIRST, AADMSAT_ACT_SET_CREATE, AADMSAT_ACT_SET_ADD, 
        AADMSAT_ACT_SET_REMOVE, AADMSAT_ACT_SET_LAST, AADMSAT_ACT_NONE, 
        AADMSAT_ACT_DEFAULT, AADMSAT_ACT_VLT_FIRST, AADMSAT_ACT_VLT_CREATE, 
        AADMSAT_ACT_VLT_MODIFY, AADMSAT_ACT_VLT_WFLOW, 
        AADMSAT_ACT_VLT_DELETE, AADMSAT_ACT_VLT_STATE, 
        AADMSAT_ACT_VLT_ACL_ASSIGN, AADMSAT_ACT_VLT_ACL_MODIFY, 
        AADMSAT_ACT_VLT_ACL_REMOVE, AADMSAT_ACT_VLT_LAST, 
        AADMSAT_ACT_USER_FIRST, AADMSAT_ACT_USER_LOGIN, 
        AADMSAT_ACT_USER_LOGOUT, AADMSAT_ACT_USER_CREATE, 
        AADMSAT_ACT_USER_LAST, AADMSAT_ACT_GROUP_FIRST, 
        AADMSAT_ACT_GROUP_CREATE, AADMSAT_ACT_GROUP_MODIFY, 
        AADMSAT_ACT_GROUP_ADD, AADMSAT_ACT_GROUP_REMOVE, 
        AADMSAT_ACT_GROUP_LAST, AADMSAT_ACT_USER_LIST_FIRST, 
        AADMSAT_ACT_USER_LIST_CREATE, AADMSAT_ACT_USER_LIST_MODIFY, 
        AADMSAT_ACT_USER_LIST_ADD, AADMSAT_ACT_USER_LIST_REMOVE, 
        AADMSAT_ACT_USER_LIST_LAST, AADMSAT_ACT_VLT_CUSTOM_FIRST, 
        AADMSAT_ACT_VLT_CUSTOM_LAST, AADMSAT_ACT_DOC_CUSTOM_FIRST,

        AADMSAT_ACT_DOC_ATTR_MODIFY, AADMSAT_ACT_DOC_ATTR_DELETE,

        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false

    Note the matching of the subtext VAULT in the type and VLT in the action.

## Set-PWDatasourceSetting


NAME
    Set-PWDatasourceSetting
    
SYNOPSIS
    Sets a setting for the current ProjectWise datasource.
    
    
SYNTAX
    Set-PWDatasourceSetting -DatasourceSettingCategory 
    <DatasourceSetting+Category> [<CommonParameters>]
    
    
DESCRIPTION
    The Set-PWDatasourceSetting cmdlet sets the value of a given datasource 
    setting in the current ProjectWise datasource. Set-PWDatasourceSetting 
    can set either a string or numeric setting.
    

PARAMETERS
    -DatasourceSettingCategory <DatasourceSetting+Category>
        The category (group) of the setting to set.
        
        Required?                    true
        Position?                    named
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       True (ByPropertyName)
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    <CommonParameters>
        This cmdlet supports the common parameters: Verbose, Debug,
        ErrorAction, ErrorVariable, WarningAction, WarningVariable,
        OutBuffer, PipelineVariable, and OutVariable. For more information, 
    see 
        about_CommonParameters 
    (https:/go.microsoft.com/fwlink/?LinkID=113216). 
    
INPUTS
    
OUTPUTS
    
NOTES
    
    
        Set-PWDatasourceSetting requires an open ProjectWise Connection in 
        the current thread.  You can open a Connection with 
        Open-PWConnection before calling Set-PWDatasourceSetting.
    
    -------------------------- Example 1 --------------------------
    
    Set-PWDatasourceSetting -DatasourceSettingCategory Miscellaneous 
    -DatasourceSettingName ForceCaseInsensitiveSearch 
    -DatasourceSettingValue 1
    
    Sets the 'Force case-insensitive search' setting value to TRUE.
    -------------------------- Example 2 --------------------------
    
    Set-PWDatasourceSetting -DatasourceSettingCategory Restrictions 
    -DatasourceSettingName CanDelete -DatasourceSettingValue "AdminOnly"
    
    Sets the 'Can Delete' setting value to 'Admin only'.
    -------------------------- Example 3 --------------------------
    
    Set-PWDatasourceSetting -DatasourceSettingCategory Projects 
    -DatasourceSettingName FolderForTemplateProjects-DatasourceSettingValue 
    "dmsSystem\Spatial\Background Map DPR Files"
    
    Sets the 'Folder for template projects' setting to 
    'dmsSystem\Spatial\Background Map DPR Files' project.
    -------------------------- Example 4 --------------------------
    
    Set-PWDatasourceSetting -DatasourceSettingCategory Projects 
    -DatasourceSettingName FolderForTemplateProjects-DatasourceSettingValue 
    56
    
    Sets the 'Folder for template projects' setting to project with the ID 
    of 56.
    
RELATED LINKS
    Get-PWDatasourceSetting

## Set-PWDocumentApplication


NAME
    Set-PWDocumentApplication
    
SYNOPSIS
    Sets the application associated with a document.
    
    
SYNTAX
    Set-PWDocumentApplication [-InputDocuments] <ProjectWiseDocument[]> 
    [-Application] <string> [[-ChangeFinalStatusApplications]] 
    [<CommonParameters>]
    
    
DESCRIPTION
    Takes a document object from the pipeline and sets the associated 
    application.
    

PARAMETERS
    -InputDocuments <ProjectWiseDocument[]>
        The document objects to act on.
        
        Required?                    true
        Position?                    0
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       True (ByValue)
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -Application <string>
        Name of application to associate with document.
        
        Required?                    true
        Position?                    1
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -ChangeFinalStatusApplications [<SwitchParameter>]
        Change application of documents with final status set.
        
        Requires current user to be an administrator.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    2
        Default value                False
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    <CommonParameters>
        This cmdlet supports the common parameters: Verbose, Debug,
        ErrorAction, ErrorVariable, WarningAction, WarningVariable,
        OutBuffer, PipelineVariable, and OutVariable. For more information, 
    see 
        about_CommonParameters 
    (https:/go.microsoft.com/fwlink/?LinkID=113216). 
    
INPUTS
    PWPS_DAB.CommonTypes+ProjectWiseDocument[]
        
    
    
OUTPUTS
    
NOTES
    
    
        ## RELATED LINKS
    
    ------------- EXAMPLE 1: Basic application change -------------
    
    $docs = Show-PWDocumentSelectionDialog
    Set-PWDocumentApplication -InputDocuments $docs -Application 
    'MicroStation'
    
    Sets the associated application to MicroStation for the selected 
    documents. Documents whose application cannot be changed are skipped.
    ---------- EXAMPLE 2: Application change via pipeline ----------
    
    Show-PWDocumentSelectionDialog | Set-PWDocumentApplication -Application 
    'MicroStation'
    
    Pipes selected documents directly into Set-PWDocumentApplication to 
    associate them with MicroStation.
    EXAMPLE 3: Application change for documents with final status
    
    $docs = Show-PWDocumentSelectionDialog
    Set-PWDocumentApplication -InputDocuments $docs -Application 
    'MicroStation' -ChangeFinalStatusApplications
    
    Changes the application on documents that have final status set. The 
    cmdlet temporarily clears the final-status flag, performs the 
    application change, then restores the final-status flag. Requires 
    administrator privileges.
    
RELATED LINKS





--

## Set-PWDocumentFinalStatus


NAME
    Set-PWDocumentFinalStatus
    
SYNOPSIS
    Sets or clears a document's final status.
    
    
SYNTAX
    Set-PWDocumentFinalStatus [-InputDocuments] <ProjectWiseDocument[]> 
    [[-RemoveFinalStatus]] [[-Comment] <string>] [<CommonParameters>]
    
    
DESCRIPTION
    Sets the final status on the ProjectWise documents you supply. Use 
    `RemoveFinalStatus` to clear final status instead.
    
    The cmdlet returns the processed document objects. Use `Comment` to 
    record a reason for the change.
    

PARAMETERS
    -InputDocuments <ProjectWiseDocument[]>
        
        
        Required?                    true
        Position?                    0
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       True (ByValue)
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -RemoveFinalStatus [<SwitchParameter>]
        Set to clear final status.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    1
        Default value                False
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -Comment <string>
        
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    2
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    <CommonParameters>
        This cmdlet supports the common parameters: Verbose, Debug,
        ErrorAction, ErrorVariable, WarningAction, WarningVariable,
        OutBuffer, PipelineVariable, and OutVariable. For more information, 
    see 
        about_CommonParameters 
    (https:/go.microsoft.com/fwlink/?LinkID=113216). 
    
INPUTS
    PWPS_DAB.CommonTypes+ProjectWiseDocument[]
        
    
    
OUTPUTS
    
NOTES
    
    
        
    
    -------------------------- EXAMPLE 1 --------------------------
    
    #Set final status for all documents in a folder.
    #Get all the documents in this folder tree and set them to final status
    Get-PWDocumentsBySearch -FolderPath "Test 6\Projects 2"  | 
    Set-PWDocumentFinalStatus
    #Get all the documents in this folder tree and clear final status
    Get-PWDocumentsBySearch -FolderPath "Test 6\Projects 2"  | 
    Set-PWDocumentFinalStatus -RemoveFinalStatus
    
    
    
RELATED LINKS

## Set-PWDocumentState


NAME
    Set-PWDocumentState
    
SYNOPSIS
    Sets a document's workflow state.
    
    
SYNTAX
    Set-PWDocumentState [-InputDocuments] <ProjectWiseDocument[]> [-State] 
    <string> [[-SkipIntermediateStates]] [[-ChangeFinalStatusStates]] 
    [[-Force]] [<CommonParameters>]
    
    
DESCRIPTION
    Takes a document object from the pipeline and sets it to the indicated 
    workflow state.
    
    The cmdlet uses the supported ProjectWise API to perform state 
    transitions. If the API rejects a transition, the cmdlet skips that 
    document and continues to the next one. The state is not changed in 
    those cases.
    

PARAMETERS
    -InputDocuments <ProjectWiseDocument[]>
        
        
        Required?                    true
        Position?                    0
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       True (ByValue)
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -State <string>
        Name of state to change to.
        
        Required?                    true
        Position?                    1
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -SkipIntermediateStates [<SwitchParameter>]
        Go directly to passed state and don't pass through intermediate 
        states.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    2
        Default value                False
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -ChangeFinalStatusStates [<SwitchParameter>]
        Change state of documents with final status set.
        
        Requires current user to be an administrator.
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    3
        Default value                False
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -Force [<SwitchParameter>]
        Used to ignore WRE
        
        Required?                    false
        Position?                    4
        Default value                False
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    <CommonParameters>
        This cmdlet supports the common parameters: Verbose, Debug,
        ErrorAction, ErrorVariable, WarningAction, WarningVariable,
        OutBuffer, PipelineVariable, and OutVariable. For more information, 
    see 
        about_CommonParameters 
    (https:/go.microsoft.com/fwlink/?LinkID=113216). 
    
INPUTS
    PWPS_DAB.CommonTypes+ProjectWiseDocument[]
        
    
    
OUTPUTS
    
NOTES
    
    
        
    
    ---------------- EXAMPLE 1: Basic state change ----------------
    
    $docs = Show-PWDocumentSelectionDialog
    Set-PWDocumentState -InputDocuments $docs -State 'Completed'
    
    Sets the selected documents to the 'Completed' state. Documents whose 
    current state cannot be transitioned to 'Completed' (per the workflow 
    definition) are skipped.
    ------------- EXAMPLE 2: State change via pipeline -------------
    
    Show-PWDocumentSelectionDialog | Set-PWDocumentState -State 'In Review'
    
    Pipes selected documents directly into Set-PWDocumentState to transition 
    them to the 'In Review' state.
    ----- EXAMPLE 3: State change skipping intermediate states -----
    
    $docs = Show-PWDocumentSelectionDialog
    Set-PWDocumentState -InputDocuments $docs -State 'Completed' 
    -SkipIntermediateStates
    
    Sets documents directly to 'Completed' without passing through 
    intermediate workflow states.
    --- EXAMPLE 4: State change for documents with final status ---
    
    $docs = Show-PWDocumentSelectionDialog
    Set-PWDocumentState -InputDocuments $docs -State 'Approved' 
    -ChangeFinalStatusStates
    
    Transitions documents that have final status set. The cmdlet temporarily 
    clears the final-status flag, performs the state transition, then 
    restores the final-status flag. Requires administrator privileges.
    
RELATED LINKS





--

        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false

## Set-PWFolderEnvironment


NAME
    Set-PWFolderEnvironment
    
SYNOPSIS
    Sets the environment on ProjectWise folders.
    
    
SYNTAX
    Set-PWFolderEnvironment [-InputFolder] <ProjectWiseFolder[]> 
    [-NewEnvironment] <string> [<CommonParameters>]
    
    
DESCRIPTION
    The Set-PWFolderEnvironment cmdlet sets the environment specified by 
    NewEnvironment on the ProjectWise folders supplied through InputFolder 
    or the pipeline. The target environment must exist in the current 
    datasource.
    
    The cmdlet processes only the folders supplied to it. It does not 
    process subfolders automatically.
    

PARAMETERS
    -InputFolder <ProjectWiseFolder[]>
        
        
        Required?                    true
        Position?                    0
        Default value                PWPS_DAB.CommonTypes+ProjectWiseFolder[]
        Accept pipeline input?       True (ByValue)
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    -NewEnvironment <string>
        
        
        Required?                    true
        Position?                    0
        Default value                None
        Accept pipeline input?       False
        Accept wildcard characters?  false
        
    <CommonParameters>
        This cmdlet supports the common parameters: Verbose, Debug,
        ErrorAction, ErrorVariable, WarningAction, WarningVariable,
        OutBuffer, PipelineVariable, and OutVariable. For more information, 
    see 
        about_CommonParameters 
    (https:/go.microsoft.com/fwlink/?LinkID=113216). 
    
INPUTS
    PWPS_DAB.CommonTypes+ProjectWiseFolder[]
        
    
    
OUTPUTS
    
NOTES
    
    
        
    
    -------------------------- Example 1 --------------------------
    
    Set-PWFolderEnvironment -InputFolder $folder -NewEnvironment 
    "SomeEnvironment"
    
    `$folder` variable holds ProjectWise folders.
    -------------------------- Example 2 --------------------------
    
    Get-PWFolders -FolderPath "PWPS Connected Project Test 6\Projects 2" | 
    Set-PWFolderEnvironment -NewEnvironment "SomeEnvironment"
